*Page 1&2*


Abuja, Nigeria

Mai Kyau  District

Doguwa ce dirarra ƙyaƙyawa ajin farko wacce idan aka ce kyau to fa ana zuwa kanta an rufe ƙofar kyau. A shekaru ba za ta haura twenty years or twenty one years ba a duniya.

Akwai ta da diri wanda a Turance ake kira da Coca Cola Shape, tana da manya-manya kuma dara daran kyawawan fararen idanu masu shining, masu egg shape. Yanayin launin kwayar idon nata su ba blue color ba, haka ma ba green color ba sannan kuma ba ash color ba. Su dai ga su nan yanayinsu sky purple masu matuƙar shining da ɗaukar hankalin duk wani mahaluki.

Akwai ta da wasu kalar laɓɓa ƙanana sirara jajayen gaske da su, ga shape nasu gwanin ban sha'awa da burgewa. Daɗi da ƙari ga ɗaukar hankali.

Hancinta kuwa wani kalar dogon hanci ne wanda ya zauna daidai a kykkyawar fuskarta ga shi ɗan daidai. Tsayawa bayyana yanayin kyawunta ya zama kauyanci. Ta hada dukkan wasu suffofi da mace ke bukata don jan hankalin namiji.

Tana sanye cikin wasu dakakkiyar doguwar gown ƙirar Malaysia, wacce ta kama jikinta ɗas ta zauna. Gown ɗin mai gajeren hannu ce saboda haka sai ta ɗaura after dress sky purple mai adon stones masu bala'in sheƙi, ita kanta rigar da ke jikinta sky purple ce doguwa har ƙasa.

Dogon gashin kanta wanda yake asalin dark brown, ya sha gyara ta tufke shi da wasu shegun ribbon masu masifar kyau da tsada guda uku wanda hakan ya ba dogon gashin damar zubo mata har tsakiyar gadon bayanta.

Ga wani yalolon mayafi da ta yafa gefen kafaɗarta, fuskarta kuwa tasha light make-up, ta yi kyau sosai. Ga wani haɗaɗɗen Wrist-Watch da ke saƙale a  hannunta. Yanayinta ta fi kama da mutane marasa hayaniya.

Tsaye take gaban dress mirror tana ƙoƙarin fesa wani perfect perfume mai masifar kyau da daɗin ƙamshi mai sanyaya ran bil-adam. Turo ƙofar katafaren haɗaɗɗen bedroom ɗin da take ciki aka yi. Ba tare da ta juya ba sanin da ta yi kowace ce ta shigo, ta ce cikin wata kalar murya mai masifar daɗin sauraro ga ta siririya yanayin sautin ta kamar ana busa sarewa.

"Oh my lovely dad mister mai kyau, ɗiyarka tana so ta fa takura mini. Yanzu zan iya cewa fa na fasa zuwa wajen birthday party ɗin nan kuma ta zauna, kin san da wannan tunda da ma can satar hanya za mu yi zuwa birthday ɗin saboda an ga daddy ba ya nan."

Ta yi maganar like a little girl kuma cike da tsabar shagwaɓa wacce ba ƙaramin kyau hakan ya ƙara mata ba, sannan kuma maganar duka da ta yi tana yi tana hutawa ne.

Dariya mai sauti wacce ta shigo bedroom ɗin tayi, sannan ta ce, "My Feefarh! Ba na son rashin mutunci fa, sai da aski ya zo gaban goshi za ki ce kin fasa haba ɗiyar mister mai kyau! Kar ki yi wa Zahra haka mana, ko dai kin manta wace ce ita gurinki?"

Mayar da perfect perfume da ke hannunta ta yi zuwa ma'ajinsa saman haɗaɗɗen dressing mirror ɗin sannan ta juyo gabaɗayanta tana fuskan tar budurwar da ta kammala yi mata magana.

Kawar da zancen zuwa birthday ɗin ta yi da faɗi, "To ya ya na yi kyau kuwa kamar SARAUNIYAR ƘYAU ta duniya?" Ta yi maganar cike da mafificin nishaɗi tana dariya.

Wannan karon ƙayataccen murmushi mai ɗauke da sauti mai cike farinciki da nishaɗi, budurwar ta kuma yi a karo na biyu tun da ta shigo sannan ta zauna gefen gado tana fuskantar ta....

A hankali ta fara magana ita ma cikin lallausar muryarta, "My Feefarh tawa ta kaina kin san ai shi mai kyau ba ya buƙatar tambaya a kan kyansa, da ganin shi kai ka san mai kyan ne shi, yanzu kamar ke dube ki. Kamar sananniyar jarumar India 'Anu  Emanuel', dogon hanci mai kyau da tsari kamar Chinese actress 'Janine Chang'. Ga ɗan ƙaramin siririn baki naki kamar a gurin wannan ɓarumar Korea 'Kim Sohyun'. Gashinki tsayi da yawa ga shi asalin dark brown kamar na fittaciyar jarumar Korea ɗin nan mai suna 'Park Shin Hye'. Ga fatarki mai masifar sheƙi da ɗaukar hankali kamar na..."

Ai tun kafin ta ƙarasa faɗin abin da take niyyar faɗa budurwa ta yi saurin tarar numfashinta da fadin, "Auntie Leesarth, ina ga wannan yabo kamar wata SARAUNIYAR ƘYAU ta duniya ya yi yawa gare ni."

"E, sarauniyar kyau ce mana tunda dai all most one month ya rage daga nan za a ga sarauniyar ce ko kuwa dai..."

"Oh bari  dai na yi shiru kawai tunda naga kamar Ba a so na yi maganar." Cewar wata ƙyaƙyawar matashiyar budurwa da ta shigo bedroom ɗin, ita ma tana rufe bakinta sannan ta canza maganar da cewa, "Ku yi ku fito mu tafi birthday party ɗin nan tun kafin daddy ya dawo fa."

Ta yi maganar ne tana zare manyan idanunta, wanda ba komai ya sa ta yi saurin sauya maganar da ta yi niyya ba, fa ce hararar da Haleesarth ta antaya mata, ba tare da ɗayar budurwar ta ankara ba.

Miƙewa Haleesarth ta yi tana faɗin, "Ai duk ga wacce duk ta bi ta ɓata mana Lokaci nan da kwalliyarta nan sai ka ce wacce za ta je wajen gasar BRITISH QUEEN."

Ta yi maganar ita ma tana antaya wa budurwar da still har yanzu tana nan tsaye gaban mirror wata uwar harara amma fa ta wasa.

Dariya ta yi sannan ta ce' "To mu je ba don halayenku ba, tunda dai na riga da na shirya." Ta faɗa tana yafa veil ɗinta da ta cire tare da ɗaukar haɗaɗɗiyar handbag ɗinta mai shige da kayan jikinta wato sky purple. Gabaɗaya waje suka yi, har sun fito ƙayataccen falon daya gaji da haɗuwa ta ko,ina fes-fes.

Wannan budurwar ta tsaya tana bin ko'ina na falon da kallo ko za ta hangi wacce take nema, sai dai har ta yi waige-waigenta ta ƙare ba ta ga wacce take da muradin gani ba a katafaren falon.

Juyowa suka yi suna kallon ta cike da ƙosawa Haleesarth ta ce, "To yanzu kuma mene ne ya faru kika tsaya kina ƙare wa falon kallo kamar baƙuwarsa?"

Ba tare  da ta kalle ta ba ta ce, "Ina anti Zainab  take? Kash damn it oh my God!"

Da sauri ta juya gabanta na mugun bugu, ba ta tsaya jira ko saurarar amsar da za su ba ta ba, ta nufi wani ɗan madaidaicin korido bedrooms biyu ne gurin, hannu ta kai da niyyar knocking ɗaya ciki wanda yake ɓangaren dama abin mamaki a buɗe ƙofar take.

Shiga ciki ta yi da sallama ɗauke saman laɓɓanta, ba ta ga kowa ba a ciki, saboda haka sai ta nufi hanyar bed. Tsayawa ta yi cak tana ƙarewa bedroom ɗin kallo sama da ƙasa gabas da yamma kudu da arewa, gabaɗaya a mugun hargitse yake kamar ba mutum ke rayuwa ciki ba, sai ka ce ba ɗazun nan yake tsaf-tsaf ba.

Pillows a ƙasa shi kansa bedsheet ɗin a ƙasa shi ma yake da alama an sha bidiri cikin bedroom ɗin. Daga can ƙarshen bangon ɗakin kuwa wata ƙyaƙyawar budurwa ce baje saman tiles na ƙofar shiga toilet, ga kwalabe a gabanta sun kai kala uku duka na Whiskey.

Da sauri ta ƙarasa kusa da ita, "Zainab, Zainab, anti Zainab! Please tell me! What happening to you? Kwalba kuma me kike yi da waɗannan kwalaben haka?"

Gabaɗaya ta bi ta ruɗe ta fita hayyacinta sabodaa tashin hankali, sam ƙwaƙwalwarta ta gaza tuna mata cewa yau ɗin ranar da suke ranar Valentine's Day ce, ranar da ta kasance wacce Zainab ba za ta taɓa mantawa da ita ba har abada kuwa a rayuwarta.

Zainab ba ta amsa mata ba sai ma  kokarin kafa kwalbar Whiskey da ke hannunta take a baki, da sauri ta kwace kwalbar cikin kunar rai.

Ta fara magana, "Wai anti Zainab sau nawa zan faɗa miki cewa Shannon giya na da matukar hadari ga lafiya? Amma ba kya ji. Ba ma wannan ba, shin ba kya tsoron fishin Allah ya sauka kanki?"

Cikin muryar mashaya Zainab ta ce, "Haba my kanwa Afeefarh, na faɗa miki ba yin kaina ba ne gabaɗaya jikina ji na nake kamar ba ni ba. Ni ma fa so nake na daina wannan mummunar halayyar ta dabbobi, amma hakan ya ci tura na kasa. Please Afeefarh ki rabu da ni wannan rayuwa ta ce kuma kaddarata ce."

Cike da tsabar tausayawa, matashiyar budurwar wacce Zainab ta yi mata kira da Afeefarh take kallon yayarta Zainab wacce kaddarar soyayya haɗe da son zuchiya ya jefa ta cikin wannan mummunan halin.

Ajiyar zuciya ta sauke sannan ta miƙe tsaye tana bin ƴar uwar tata da kallon tausayi ta ce, "Anti Zainab ɗita! Bari na kira anti Sa'adah ta gyara miki bedroom ɗin amma please kafin ta zo ki taimaka ki tashi ki kwashe wadannan kwalaben kuma a kullum ina kara tuna miki cewar ki kasance mace mai yawan yarda ya kaddara a duk ta inda ta zo miki sannan ki kasance mai juriya kin ji my anti? Ni yanzu zan tafi saboda za mu ta fi Birthday Party na Zahra da navyi miki maganar tun last sai mun dawo."

Tana gama  faɗin haka ba tare da jiran ta bakin Zainab ɗin ba ta fita daga bedroom ɗin.

Katafaren aljannar duniyar falon ta dawo sannan ta karisa gurin Haleesarth da ke tsaye tsakiyar falon, gabaɗaya ta gama tunzura da ɓata musu lokacin da Afeefarh ɗin take.

Ba tare da Afeefarh ta kula complain ɗin Haleesarth ɗin ke mata ba ta fita daga falon zuwa farfajiyar katafaren gidan ta karisa parking lot da ke shaƙare da manya-manyan motoci na alfarma a fake..




*SARAUNIYAR KYAU*♥

Story & Written By:


       Khadeejarth Sabi`u Yahyah

_In dedicated to my dear Classmates Science G.S.S.S BELE ROAD_



*Page 3&4*


Sautin dariyar da ake ɓaɓɓakawa ne yasa ta tsaya cak da tafiyar da takeyi,a hankali ta juyo gefan damarta ta saitin da dariyar take fitowa,ɗan zaro kyawawan idanunta tayi waje baki a hangame da mamaki ganin wa`inda suke wannan dariya haka kamar babu gobe..

Su bakwai ne,dukansu sanye cikin kayan ma`aikata Launin black and white suit,daga inda kowannen su yake tsaye akwai rata da ɗan uwansa,domin suna tsaitsaye ne tsayuwa irinna ma`aikatan tsaro wato {ESCORT} wasu daga cikin su ma Jami`ai ne na ƴan sanda wa`inda aikin su shine tsaron gidan,Dariya kawai suke ɓaɓɓakawa kamar masu cutar hauka,domin wasu daga cikin su ma harda hawaye saboda tsaban dariya,saidai da ganin yadda suke dariyar zaka fahimci na dole ne..


Hii Afeefarh wai menene kike kallo haka ne,ma`aikatanne baki taɓa gani ba,ko kuwa dai akwai sabo ne cikinsu,da kika tsaya haka Like a Gunki kina kallon su?....

Da sauri afeefarh ta juyo tana kallon haleesarth da tayi maganar hankali kwance tana ƙoƙarin buɗe tsadaddiyar mota ƙirar Bugatti, launin sky purple ta shiga..


Zuwa yanzu ta fahimci komai ajiyar zuciya ta sauke sannan tace  "a`a bawai kallon su nake ba dan ban gane wani ba,ko naga sabo cikin su, kallon su nake ganin yadda suke wannan dariyar da alama ɗaya daga cikin ku kodai ke,ko kuma LAILAH wata ta aikita haramci."

Eh kina da gaskiya wannan dariyar da sukeyi da akwai dalilinta kuma lailah ce sila bawai ni ba.Haleesarth tayi  maganar tana mai ya mata nuni da lailah data iso wajen tana baza ƙamshi cikin takun isa da gadara...

menene kuma naji ana Lailah ce sila gani nayi ba zasu taɓa bari mu fita cikin daɗin rai ba shiyasa kawai nayi abinda ya kamata dasu....

Abinda ya kamata fa kikace hakanne abinda ya kamata lailah are serious,mutane ne fa,waima tukunna menene abinda kika musu ne?. Afeefarh tayi maganar tana danne ɓacin rai dama cikin sa take tun lokacin da taga halin da zaynerb take ciki....

Haba  afeefarh menene na ɓacin rai da fushi kuma?,naga ma`aikata ne kuma a ƙarƙashin uba suke samun na ci da sha, da kike maganar menene abinda nayi musu,naga kema likita ce kama ta saboda haka kinsan abinda nayi,  kawai LAUGHING_GAS na fesa musu mintuna ƙalilan zasu ɗauka suna dariyar bazai cutar dasu ba nayi miki alƙawari...

Ɗan jim afeefarh tayi tana kallon lailah da tayi maganar hankalinta kwance tana ƙoƙarin shiga motar gidan gaba, daya ke  itace zatayi driving nasu. ..


Allah ya shirya ki lailah muguwar gidan mu,afeefarh tayi maganar cikin ƙyaɓe fuska da ɗan hararar lailah ɗin domin kuwa batasan abinda zatace mata ba,tasan wannan kaɗan daga cikin halin lailah ne, wato mugunta..

Madam nace ina zaku tafi ba tare da kunyi min magana ba mu shirya motoci kinsan dai fitarku ku kaɗai babu guards akwai hatsari ko?..

Fasa shiga motar afeefarh tayi ta juya tana kallon mahalukin da yayi maganar,saida gabanta yaɗan faɗi ganin wanda ke tsaye cikin black suit yana kallonta,ba kowa bane fa ce SALSA ɗaya daga cikin manyan ma`aikatan gidan zama`a iya kiransa da shugaban gabaɗaya ma`aikatan gidan.SALSA dai wani jibgegyan qaton Arne ne ɗan asalin ƙasar Philippine  qato ne mai qirar samudawa,wanda wannan ƙirar tashi ne tasa gaban afeefarh bugu, ai dole domin kuwa jinsa yake  shida qirar sa kamar basamude...

Rasa abinda zatace mishi tayi tsawon minti ɗaya,ajiyar zuciya ta sauke sannan ta fara magana kamar haka...

"Kaga mai ƙirar  samudawa tun wuri ka bari mu tafi tun muna mu kaɗai,kafin raina ya ɓaci"tayi maganar cikin haɗe gira kuma da hausa dama da gangar tayi sanin da tayi bayajin hausa,domin maganar da yayi mata da farko ma da turanci yayi..

Kinga madam koma menene zakice saidai ki faɗa dole tare zamu tafi domin alhakin kula da lafiyarki Boss ya damƙa a hannun mu,saboda haka koma miye zakice saidai ki faɗa dan tare zamu.....

Tunda haka ne, na fasa tafiyar kuma kasa ido zakaga abinda zai biyo baya, badai ka hanani fita ba,daddy zan kira na faɗama kasani daga yau,kabar aiki a gidannan tunda dani zakayi compromise.....

Tayi maganar tana kashewa lailah ido ɗaya sannan ta fara yunƙurin saka hannu a handbag ɗinta tana ƙoƙarin ciro wayarta...

Murmushi lailah tayi sannan cikin ɗan zaro ido da ɗaga murya ta fara yima salsa magana.
Kai amma fa baka da hankali yanzu ka gummace data kira da daddy ta faɗa mishi,kai kuma ka rasa aikin ka,kayi sauri ka ceci aikinka ta hanyar daya dace kafin ta kira daddy a waya,muddin ba so kake ka koma ƙasar philippine ba.....

Cikin tashin hankali yana share zufar data tsitato mishi salsa,cikin rarrabewar murya yace,"totto madam lailah meya kamata nayi banason rasa aikina oh jesus".....

Ya Allah abinda suka iya kenan kiran wannan suna chioooo cewar haleesarth tana jan tsakin takaici......

Hidden dariya lailah tayi ganin yadda lokaci guda gabaɗaya salsa yabi ya ruɗe daga kira masa rasa aiki,tana dariyar tace."kawai hakuri zaka bata sannan kace bazaka ƙara ba sannan kuma karka ƙara koda wasa,kana kasan yadda zakayi wa`incan baƙaƙen dake tsaron gate na farko su barmu mu fita,domin gate na biyu isah da sauran mutanen bamu da matsala dasu..

Da sauri cikin rawar jiki salsa yayi abinda lailah ta bugata ta hanyar bawa afeefarh hakuri da kuma yima jami`an dake tsaron gate na farko magana akan su barsu su fita sannan kuma yana mai ƙara basu hakuri duka...


Ɗan nema yaji maganar rasa aiki harda rawan jiki ah lallai na sama abin fakewa dashi dan ubansa da wata jar fatarsa a gurin kamar na akuya,cewar afeefarh tana dariya bayan ta shiga motar,dama can ba burinta ne ta kira daddy ta faɗa mishi ba,kawai dai kafce ne....

Hhhh aini tun four months back na fahimci raunin sa wato dai aikinne  bayaso ya rasa,to banda abinshi ma,ai momy ce ta ɗaukeshi aiki nan gidan bawai daddy ba,saboda haka ita kaɗai keda ikon korarsa lokacin dataso bawai daddy ,cewar lailah tana kuma dariya..

Itadai afeefarh murmushi kawai tayi tana lalubo lambar Auntie Sa`adah a wayarta domin umartanta da gyara bedroom ɗin zaynerb !!!


Wuraren ƙarfe Biyar dai dai na maraice suka isa wajen birthday ɗin wanda ya qawatu iya qawatuwa haɗuwa iya haɗuwa..

Fitowa daga tsadaddiyar motar nasu sukayi a tare kuma ko waccensu tana baza ƙamshi mai class ..
Afeefarh wacca babu abinda takeyi sai faman baza idanu tana ƙarewa gurin kallo babu abinda takeyi,indai ba idonta bane mai matsala gani take kamar dai "MASHAYA!" Sukazo da sauri ta juya tana binsu haleesarth da kallon mamaki da al`ajabi tsaban takaici tama kasa magana.....

Ganin ɓacin rai haɗe da mamakin dake saman kyakyawar fuskarta ne yasa da sauri haleesarth ta nuna lailah dake qumshe dariya dan kuwa da gangar taqi faɗa mata gurin da za`ayi birthday ɗin saboda bataso ta ɓata musu budget...

Da sauri lailah ta kama kunnenta alamar ta tuba tun kafin afeefarh tace wani abu...

Girgiza kai kawai afeefarh tayi tasan halin lailah farin sani akwaita da tsokana,barkwanci, saurin fushi ji_ji_da kai ɗaukar komai ba komai ba,sai tsoro kamar ƴar fari😁..

Qarisowar zahrah gurinne ya hana afeefarh faɗin abinda tayi niyya shiru tayi tana kallon zahrah ɗin...

Cike da farin ciki zahrah ta qariso gurin su tana washe baki dan kuwa batayi tunanin afeefarh zata yarda tazo  "MASHAYA!" Gurin birthday party ɗinta ba..

Hi!! Feefarh mai ƙyau Ashe zakizo birthday na zahrah ta faɗa tana kamo hannun afeefarh cike da farin cikin zuwanta...

Murmushi mai Bayyana dimples tayi sannan tace gani kuma nazo gurin  birthday party ɗin aminiyata zahrah...

"Wato dai  Ansama aminiya  an manta damu ƴan karere ko to kisani da ƙƴarma muka samu tazo birthday ɗin naki ae da kike wannan rawar jikin",cewar haleesarth tana antayawa zahrah harara,dan kuwa taji haushin ganin yadda zahrah ɗin duk  tabi ta firgice da ganin zuwan afeefarh ɗin saikace ba da ƙƴar aka samu tazo ba..


Oh sorry my friend banma kula dake ba,murnar ganin feefarh mai kyau ne,yasa ban gankiba wallahi,zahrah ta faɗa tana kama kunnenta alamar ban hakuri. .

Haleesarth zatayi magana lailah ta rigata da cewa "kunga kuzo muje anata kiran birthday girl fa,wani lokacin a ƙarisa wannan topic ɗin..

Murmushi zahrah tayi sannan tace "okay  shall we" ɗunguma sukayi dukansu ukun suka shiga wajen birthday party da yake cike da manyan yara maza da mata masuji da tashen quruciya da dukiya ta iyayensu...


An kammala komai an shirya komai ,anzo wajen yanka cake ,yanka cake ɗin zahrah tayi da wani madaidaicin ATM card  dake hannunta sannan taci taba saurayinta abdul a baki shima ya bata nan wajen ya ɗauka sowa samari da ƴan mata ana faɗin "happy birthday to you zahrah. ..

Hakadai abin nasu wallah👯


Ita dai afeefarh tana daga gefe burin ta ayi a gama su koma gida kafin daddy  MISTER MAI ƘYAU ya dawo basunan domin kuwa idan ya rigasu komawa da akwai ƙura over highly.


"Assalamu Alaiki Yake ƙyaƙyawa cikin kyawawa, tauraruwa cikin ƙyawawan taurari wacca take haske duk wani waje"

Wannan Maganar ne ya dira mata a kunnenta wanda hakan yasa saida tsigar jikinta ya miƙe saboda batayi tsammani ba taji muryar mai maganar ya dira a kunnenta.

Wata Sansanyar murya ta bayanta akayi mata  sallama da yabo lokaci guda.

Bata Juya ba kuma bata amsa sallamar ba saidai ta amsa cikin zuciyarta...

Sallamar aka ƙarayi mata still,a fusace ta juya da niyyar yima ko ma waye masifa,sai kuma me?.

Ido huɗu sukayi dashi yana kallon ta,itama tana kallon shi.

Zata iya cewa gaban ta ya ɗan faɗi kaɗan,sakamakon ƙyawawan idanun shi daya kafe ta dasu..

Shiru tayi tana ƙare mishi kallo from head to toe. ..

Fari ne shi amma ba fari cancan ba,bashi da tsayi highly highly,irin can ɗinnan amma fa,he`s so cute nd beautiful obaa..
Launin kwayar idanun shi Brown color kamar na mage,ya tara suma akanshi baki sidiq irinna  fulanin usul gata tasha gyara in stylishly...

*Page 5&6*


Sanye yake cikin ƙananan kaya T-shirt fara da wando crazy Jeans ash color, kansa ba hula, kunnensa ɗaya manne da ɗan kunnen barima irin wannan wanda maza suke sakawa na gayu, shi ɗin cikakken gaye ne mai ji da kuruciya da gayu ga kuma naira.


Cikin sakanin da ba za su wuce uku zuwa huɗu ba, ta gama ƙare mishi kallo ta cikin glashin dake fuskarta, kai ba za ka ce shi take kallo ba saboda yadda ta tsare gida. Shekarun shi baza su wuce ashirin da huɗu zuwa da biyar ba a iya lissafinta.


Muryarsa ce ta dawo da ita daga duniyar tunanin da ta lula.

"Hey babe am Sabbir Salis Mai Naira, amma an fi kira na da S.S Gidan Naira."

Ya faɗi hakan yana miƙa mata hannu alamar su gaisa. Hannun nasa take bi da kallo ita ba ta ba shi nata ba, kuma ba ta masa magana.

Ɗan sosa sumar kansa ya yi ganin ba ta kulashi ba. Sannan ya cusa hannunsa ɗaya cikin aljihun wando, ya gyara tsayuwarsa sannan ya ce, "Please beautiful! A taƙaice dai za mu iya zama abokai?" Ya faɗa yana wani kanne mata ido ɗaya irin na tatattun ƴan duniya.

"Oh da gaske amma saboda me za mu zama abokai?" Ta tambaya ba tare da ta bari sun haɗa ido ba, don kuwa sai ya sa ta ba shi eleven ɗinta ba tare da ta sani ba, da wadnnan shegun idanun nasa.

Murmushi ya yi, "Because you are so beautiful!" Ya ba ta amsa yana ƙara kashe murya.

Za ta iya cewa ya ba ta dariya wannan kashe murya haka, haka ko mace albarka. Ba tare da ta kuma kallon shi ba ta ce, "Hmmm! I'm Lailah Abubakar Mai Kyau!"

Ta faɗa tana yamutsa fuska don ya ta kura mata shi ya sa ta faɗa masa sunan Lailah komai ta fanjma fanjam.

"Lailah, wow nice name! Am so Amazed meeting you."

"Me too!" Ta ba shi amsa  tana kokarin barin gurin domin kuwa ta gaji da tsayuwar, da sauri ya sha gabanta sannan ya ce, "Sorry ba ki ba ni eleven naki ba?" Ya faɗa yana marairaicewa tamkar maraya. 

Ganin dai zai tara mata mutane ne ya sa ta ba shi lambar Lailah da yake duka wayoyin har da ma na Haleesarth suna hannunta.

Da sauri ya kwashe lambar data karanto masa cikin dankareriyar wayarsa, don bala'i saboda ya tabbatar sai da ya kira ya ji ko tatan ce ko kuwa, ɗauko wayar ta yi a handbag ɗinta ta nuna masa sannan ta ce,"Shi ke nan ko da wani abu?"

Murmushi ya yi ganin ya sama lambarta, ita kuwa Afeefarh ba ta tsaya jin surutun da yake koarin yi mata ba tabar gurin.

Jin an dafa shoulder ɗinsa ne yasa shi jujowa, "Ah Haydar, kai ne ashe."

Murmushi Hydar ya yi sannan ya ce, "To ya an samu nasara ko kuwa?" Ya tambaya yana kallon Sabbir da fuskarsa take ɗauke da ƙayataccen murmushi.

"An samu da ƙyar amma fa..." Ya faɗa yana dafe saitin zuciyarsa.

"To kai Shar Shar, tunda ka samu Haydar." Ya faɗa cikin murmushi sannan ya ci gaba da cewa, "Amma na ji kamar ƴar uwarta ka ce kana so fa, to ya haka?"


AFEEFARH


Ɓangaren  Afeefarh fita waje ta yi wajen da suka parker motocinsu, tana jiran su Haleesarth.


A taƙaice dai su Afeefarh ba su suka bar gurin Birthday ɗin ba sai wajen takwas na dare.

Burin su bai wuce ace daddy bai dawo ba,dan sunsan idan ya dawo kashin su ya bushe..

"Oh  my God !!  Rashin sa`a cewar Haleesarth Lokacin da security gate man ya wangale musu wagameman gate ɗin katafaren Gidan nasu,idansu bai sauka Ko`ina ba sai a parking space...

Da Jerin Motocin MISTER MAI ƘYAU da suke a pake  suka yi arba. Ko gama daidaita parking haleesarth datayi driving nasu yanzu batayi ba suka fito dukansu.


Palo suka shiga da Mister mai kyau suka fara cin karo yana kaikawa da qaton palon hankali a tashe....

A hankali suka fara taku saɗan saɗab kamar mara sa gaskiya cikin palon aikuwa kamar ance mister mai ƙyau juyo sukayi four eyes da afeefarh dama itace a gaba.


Da sauri  ya qariso gurin hannunta dake rawa kamar mazari ya kamo.

A hankali ya fara magana kamar mai jin tsoron wani abu ya raba shi da ita shalelensa.

"Shalelena ba abin da ya same ki please tell me. Gabaɗaya na shiga damuwa na dawo gida bakunan".
Murmushi Afeefarh ta yi tana kara jin ƙaunar mahaifin nata da wannan  ƙauna da yake nuna  mata da kulawa.

"Ah Daddy karka damu indai ni ce babu abin da zai same ni muddin kana tare da ni lovely daddy, yanzu birthday party ɗin zahrah classmate ɗinmu muka je mun yi tunanin bazaka barmu bane shi yasa muka yi satar hanya amma muna ba da haƙuri our apologize." 

"To amma shalele mai ya sa ba ku tafi da guards ba, kun fa san yadda ƙasar nan ta lalace." Mister mai Kyau ya kuma faɗa.

"Dad! Mu fa ba ma son hayaniya ne shi yasa ba mu tafi da escort ba."

Tun kafin ya kuma cewa komai ta riga shi da kara faɗin, "I need to take shower, na gaji sosai daddy."

Murmushi Mister Mai Kyau ya yi sannan ya ce, "To shi ke nan shalelena, yi sauri ki je ki yi wanka ki huta kin ji?" Ya faɗa yana duban inda su Haleesarth suke sai dai me? Wayam babu ba su sun gudu, ya girgiza kai cikin murmushi irin na manya.

Dariya Afeefarh ta yi ta ce "Daddy ke nan ai tun ɗazu suka shige bedroom nasu ta faɗa tana kuma murmushi.

Daddy Bai kuma cewa komai ba ya wuce dining table wajen Matar Gidan Haj. Rafi'at  {Mrs Mai Kyau}Sai dai yaranta sun fi kiran ta da Momy Ko kuma Momy Rafi'at, musamman Zaynerb da Afeefarh sun fi kiran ta da haka a matsayinta na kishiyar mahaifiyarsu Hajara da ta daɗe da rasuwa tun haihuwar Afeefarh.


Momy Rafi'at ta kasance kyakyawar Bafulatana ce kyakyawar ƙarshe ajin farko, ta kasance mace mai son a girmama ta na ɗaya, ta kasance mace mai son mijinta Mister Mai Kyau fiye da komai a rayuwarta na biyu, ta kasance mace mai bala'in saurin fushi da gaza fahimta kuma ita tana so a fahimce ta na uku, ta kasance mace mai Bala`in iya ɗaukar wanka da shiga na kece raini domin kuwa sometimes idan ta ɗauki wanka ta saka mayafi da glass kai ba za ka yi tunanin shekatunta Hamsin da ɗauriya a duniya ba, tunani za ka yi ba ta wuce 30yrs ba, na huɗu ta kasance babu wasu wadnda ta taɓa tsana a duniya ko na ce ta tsana sama da Zainab da kanwarta Afeefarh ta tsane su, sama da mutuwarta, sai dai ba ta taɓa nunawa ko a fuska ba, tun shekaru goma sha daya" baya da ta yi kuskuren yi wa Zaynerb  da Afeefarh dukan mutuwa a kan dalilin da bai kai ya kawo ba, ai kuwa lokacin ta ga masifa da bala'i domin sai da mijinta ya sake ta a lokacin, kuma ya ɗauki yaransa Zaynerb da Afeefarh ya bar ƙasar da su tsawon shekaru shida sannan da ƙyar aka samu ya dawo, tun lokacin  ba ta ƙara yunƙuri ko gigin sake dukan su ba, sai dai ba wai don tsanar su ta bar zuciyarta ba, a'a sai dai don ba ta so mijinta ya ƙara sakin ta, domin wancan lokacin da ya sake ta ta sha matukar wahala kafin ya dawo da ita don sai da ta shekara guda cur sannan aka samu ya haƙura aka mayar da aurensu.

Shi ya sa take danne tsanar tasu a zuciyarta ba wai don ta ƙyale su ba, a'a ɗaurin talala ta yi musu ya gama nuna musu ƙaunar.

Da yake tana da katafaren asibiti nata na ƙashin kanta a tsakiyar garin Abuja, tana aiki a can shi ya sa mafi yawan lokuta take cikin busy da yake asibitin nata Pediaric ne.

Yau ma around 6:pm ta dawo asibiti a gajiye ta sama mijinta Mister Mai Kyau cikin tashin hankali wai ya dawo gida bai ga shalelensa ba hankalin shi ya tashi yake faɗa mata.

Abin ba ƙaramim sosa mata rai da ba ta haushi  ya yi ba, kawai ba yadda ta iya ne ta danne ɓacin ranta ta ki kula shi, sai dai abin yana nuƙurƙusar ta ƙasan rai. To ban da latsinsa ma da rashin son gaskiya, Afeefarh ce kaɗai ba ta gidan, ai har da su Lailah da Haleesarth amma Afeefarh ya tuna saboda ita ce ƴar gold sauran ko oho.

Da ta gaji da abin nasa bayan ta yi wanka ta shirya cikin jallabiya ruwan madara da mayafi shi ne fa ta fito palon ƙasa inda yake tsaye har yanzu, ba ta kula shi ba ta karasa haɗaɗɗen dining room ma'aikata suka yi serving nata ta fara cin abinci hankali kwance.

Tana nan zaune Afeefarh suka shigo palon  kuma tana jin duka abin da suka tattauna sai dai ba ta kula su ba, har Mister Mai Kyau ya dawo dining room ɗin ya zauna cikin washe baki.

Ma'aikaciyar  ce cikin rawar  jiki ta fara kici-kicin serving ɗinsa, ai kuwa cikin zafin nama Momy Rafi'at ta yi saurin miƙewa tana mai daka wa 'yar aikin tsawa a kan za ta yi serving mijinta abinci abin da ko da gigin wasa ba su taɓa ba, tana mata tsiya dan bala'i gefe guda kuma tana antayawa Afeefarh da za ta wuce ta side ɗin hararar tsana.

Ita ma Afeefarh ba ta kuma magana ba ta nufi bedroom ɗinta ko kula momy Rafi'at da ke antaya mata harara ta gefan ido ba ta yi ba.


KANO


Da gudu ta shigo bedroom ɗin tana ƙwala wa Jieddarh kira, "Jieddarh, Jieddarh. Ke Jieddarh!" Hango ta ta yi saman gado ta yi ɗai-ɗai tana ƙwasar barci.

Da sauri ta hau kan bed ɗin tana kiran sunanta, "Jieddarh ki tashi takardar ki ta fito nan da wata ɗaya za ki fito gasar SARAUNIYAR KYAU matakin Jaha.

*page7&8*


A hankali ta buɗe fararen idanunta ta ware su akan fuskar ʜumaira dake mata magana.

maƙalƙale ʜumaira tayi ta miƙe tsaye tana rawa da juyi saman bed ɗin ita ma ʜumaira tana taya ta.

Da sauri ta dira daga saman bed ɗin. ᴛa fita daga bedroom ɗin gabaɗaya. ɪta ma ʜumaira ta rufa mata baya. ᴅa gudu ta ɗare jikin wata ƴar ƙyaƙyawar dumadumar mata baƙa,ᴅake palon wacca ba za ta wuce shekaru arba'in da huɗu zuwa da biyar ba.


Tana faɗin "Auntie nayi nasara zan fito gasar SARAUNIYAR KYAU matakin jaha nanda one month"...
Da sauri ƴar dumadumar matar da jieddarh ta kira da "Auntie tayi saurin yin, rijecting ɗin wayar da takeyi sannan ta juyo da jieddarh gabanta tana tambayarta,abinda ke faruwa.

Cike da farin ciki jieddarh ta ce "Auntie an zaɓeni, zan fito gasar SARAUNIYAR KYAU matakin jaha fa".. 
Ƙayataccen murmushi Auntie Tayi,ba tare da tace komai ba.

Ita ma jieddarh bakinta washe yake kamar gonar auduga, ᴢama tayi saman two sieter kusa da ʜumaira tana faɗin "Auntie yau ina cikin farin ciki burina Ya kusa cika zan zama SARAUNIYAR KYAU" ta faɗa tana rungumo hannun ta a ƙirji Tana lumshe kyawawan idanun ta kamar mai jin bacci.

"murnar mai akeyi ne BRITISH QUEEN?" muryar ʜeesham taji yana magana da sauri ta juya tana kallon ƙofar palon ta wajen da ta jiyo muryar shi, tsaye yake jikin ƙofar ya rungumo hannun shi ta baya yana sakar mata ƙayataccen murmushi.

Da gudu ta miƙe tsaye ta ƙarisa gurin shi, ʏi tayi kamar zata rungume shi, sai kuma ta fasa ta tsaya a gaban shi tana sauke ajiyar zuciya, cikin murya mai sanyi tace "Yaa .H. yau ina cikin farin ciki burina zai cika an zaɓeni a cikin waɗanda zasu fito gasar SARAUNIYAR KYAU matakin jaha.

"Da gaske take, ku rantse mana" ya tambaya yana kallon ʜumaira dake murmushi ɗaga mishi kanta tayi alamar haka ne.

Gyara tsayuwar sa sayayi a gaban ta kamar wani soja yayi saluting ɗinta yana dariya.

Ita ma salute ɗin shi tayi tana murmushi ta ce "Yaa .H. kaga daga na zama SARAUNIYAR KYAU ta jaha sai ta ƙasa daga ta ƙasa saina duniya ko", ta tambaya tana kallon shi.

Dariyar da ba shiri Auntie Tayi ta na faɗin "yarinya kenan waya faɗa miki daga na jaha saina ƙasa akwai wasu gaba"..

"Wanne da wanne kenan jieddarh ta tambayi Auntien tana zama a kujera, ʙayan ta dawo gurin.
Dukansu gyara zama sukayi suna kallon Auntie murmushi Auntie ta yi a zuciyar ta ta ce "anzo gurin Yarinya Jieddarh GO AHEAD Muje zuwa"...

Muskuɗawa tayi sannan "ta ce na farko dai da ace a ƙauye kike to saikin fara cin na local government sannan azo na State idan shima na state kinci sai azo.
Na state`s talatin da shida na ƙasar nan, shima sai kinyi winning sannan kuma azo na ƙasa, Bayan wasu ƙananu da za`ayi waɗanda su ba dole,bane domin ko anyi ko ba`ayi ba za`a iya shiga na ƙasa.
Kana azo a haɗa ƴan waɗannan ƙasar da wacan ƙasar shima idan kinyi winning akwai wasu da yawa sannan kuma azo uban gayyar na DUNIYA!.

Jikin jieddarh ne yayi sanyi jin waɗannan uban lissafi ita awa na state ɗinma ya aka ƙare balle na duniya Allah yasa taci na jahan ma tukunna, Toh dama Haka Abin Yake Idan akazo maganar sai taji jikinta Yayi sanyi laqwas....

"Aini nasan indai jieddarh ce zata cinye waɗannan tuni, wae bakuga wannan kyau ɗin bane ae ko wata ƴar american albarka come on mana Jieddarh yane wae da rasa qwarin gwiwa haka, haba mana keɗin fa, kece SARAUNIYAR KYAU ta gidan mu", Humaira ce duk tayi waɗannan maganar a domin kwantar ma da jieddarh hankali, fuskarta ɗauke da ƙayataccen murmushi, "Ko ba haka ba Yaa .H. da sauri yace Haka ne mana na yarda  highly.

Ganin yadda jikin jieddarh ɗin har yanzu  Yake a sanyaye yasa Auntie faɗin "kuma ae nasan yarinyar tawa kyakyawace zata ciyo mana kyauta na musamman,nasan da wannan.

Washe baki jieddarh tayi dan kuwa a kullum tanaso taji ana yabon ta, ana ƙara mata ƙarfin gwiwa, ana ƙara zugata kanta na hayaƙi😇.

"Kinga yanzu sai a fara shirya shirye ko?" cewar ʜumaira, Da sauri jieddarh ta ce "eh Haka ne to amma dame za'a fara?.
"Ku shirya kawai na kaiku kuyi shopping tun yau kuma yanzu ku zaɓo duk abin da ranku ke so, nauyin payment na ɗauka", Heesham ya faɗa yana kallon Humaira ɗin da ita Jieddarh.

Da sauri suka miƙe tsaitsaye harda ruga ruge  "kabamu minti biyar kawai Yanzu zamu shirya no wasting time suka faɗa a tare suna nufar hanyar staircases  domin shiga bedroom  don shiri domin kuwa a kullum Burinsu a fita shopping basa gajiya sam ko kaɗan da outing🥰.
Sallama suka ma Auntie Bayan sun shirya suka fita shopping mall aikuwa dai sun jido kaya kala kala a gurare daban daban Wani ma, ba amfani zayyi ba,kawai don a kashe kuɗaɗe suka jido.
Basu suka dawo ba sai da suka biya suka sha ice cream cheese Burger e.t.c.

Daga ƙarshe ma sai da aka biya dasu sukayi salon sannan suka dawo gida.
Aikuwa dai a gajiye suka dawo gidan,domin kuwa ko gurin Auntie basu biya ba don ko ba ƙaramin gajiya suka kwaso ba.zuwa lokacin Dare ya lula kowannen su  wucewa bedroom ɗinsa yayi domin samun Hutuu...




WANNAN KENAN

       
           



   ABUJA!





___Lailah

Ɓangaren Lailah tsaf ta shirya cikin sleeping dress payjamas pink color mai taushi, har zata kwanta ta tuna bata amsa wayarta wajen Afeefah ba,don haka hijjab ta sanya jikinta sannan ta fito daga bedroom ɗinta sai da ta fara biyawa kitchen ta haɗa black coffee mai kauri sannan ta nufa bedroom ɗin Afeefah, knocking ɗin ƙofar tayi, Daga ciki Afeefah ta bata izinin shiga.

Kwance tasa meta tayi daidai saman Royal Bed ɗinta tana ibadar da aka saba wato chatting😅.

Zama tayi kan stool na dress mirror sannan ba tare da ta kalleta ba Ta ce "malamar chatting bani wayata".

Ita ma Afeefah Ba tare da  ta kalleta ba saboda yadda gabaɗaya hankalin ta yana kan tsadaddiyar wayarta dake hannun ta, chatting da ƙawaye ya ɗauke mata hankali, Ta mata nuni da kan bed daga gefanta, ƙarisawa Lailah Tayi sannan ta ɗauka handbag ɗin ta ciro wayarta a ciki kana ta koma ta zauna saman wata doguwar softened stool dake ɗakin kana ta kunna Wayar dayake a kashe take.

Aikuwa kamar jira ake ta kunna saiga saƙonni nata tururuwar shigowa,Can kuma saiga kira ya shigo unknown number ne.
Dan haka batayi picking call ɗin ba saboda tsarinta kenan, sai da aka jera kira wajen uku sannan da ta gaji da kiran ta saka Wayar a silent..
Ganin taƙi ɗaukar kiran da ake mata ne gashi kuma shi mai kiran yaƙi haƙura Yasa Afeefah da ringing ɗin wayar ya isheta ta ɗago tana kallon Lailah kana Ta ce "Lailah ta ana kiranki fah ko bakiji bane?."

Tsaki Lailah taja sannan Ta ce "kinsan dai bana ɗaukar unknown number ko?", "Ki ɗaga kiji ko waye mana tunda anƙi dena kira kikasani ko wani abu mai muhimmancine?",
Ya mutsa kyakyawar fuskarta Lailah tayi "banajin haka kawai dai mara aikin yine".

Saukowa Afeefah tayi daga kan bed ɗin tana shirin ɗaga kiran wayar ta yanke saboda haka Komawa tayi ta zauna ta ci gaba da latsa wayar ta, Lailah zatayi magana wani kiran ya sake shigowa, Picking Tayi amma batayi magana ba, ta dai saka wayar a hands Free.

Jin ba'ayi magana ba sai Faman ajiyar zuciya da ake saukewa yasa Tayi kamar zata katse kiran,da sauri Afeefah tayi mata alama da karta katse ta Jira tukunna.
Cikin wata irin murya mai sanyi daga can ɓangaren mai kiran wayar Yayi sallama.
Cikin ƙyawawan yaran mister mai ƙyau dake bedroom ɗin wato Afeefah da Lailah rasa wanda zai amsa sallamar akayi, Sai can Lailah tayi ƙarfin halin amsawa da...

Wslm ta faɗa a gajarce kuma a taƙaice sannan a fusace,wata kalar ajiyar zuciya aka kuma sauke wa ta can ɗayan ɓangaren mai kiran,Sannan aka fara magana kamar haka.

"Lailah ta to ya kike kin isa gida lafiya, inata kira baki ɗauka ba har na karaya fah, sai naji kamar bansama shiga ba. Hala dai duk gajiyar birthday ɗinne?."

Shiru yayi da maganar Jin kamar hankalin ta Baya kanshi Tunda batayi magana ba. "Hello Lailah ƙawa ta did you hearing me?, please say something mana!.

Da sauri Lailah da mamaki ya kusa kasheta ta ce "wrong number, Malam kai waye kuma sannan miye haɗina da kai?",

Jin tana ƙoƙarin katse kiran ya sa shi faɗin Lailah Abubakar Mai Kyau koh ko ba ita bace?, 
A mugun fusace Lailah Ta ce "ni ce sai akayi Yah Yah,huh Nace sai akayi YahYah zaka takumin fa, Tayi maganar a fusace domin kuwa akwaita da saurin hawa,kamar momy Rafi`at Mahaifiyarta.

Oh baki gane mai maganar ba kenan,Bangane ba,Oh sabbir salis mai Naira ne, Da muka haɗu dake wajen birthday party ɗin classmate ɗin ku, ɗazunnan.

Dariya ne ya kusa kama Afeefah amma tayi saurin ƙumshe wa,Ita kuwa Lailah mamaki ya hanata cewa komai,"Kinajina kuwa sabbir ya faɗa daga ɓangarenshi.

Uhhhm ta faɗa a taƙaice tana hamman gajiya da wannan futile maganar.
"it's okay My friend Bacci kikeji bari na barki kawai,kiyi mafarkina fah kinji ko friend ɗita bye bye!!
Tana shirin yin magana ya katse kiran..
Baki sake Lailah take bin wayar da kallo tana mamakin yaushe har suka haɗu dashi wai har ta bashi numbar ta, ita da ko lambar namiji ɗaya, babu a wayarta daga na dady saina uncle's ɗinta da kuma YaYYinta maza na Dangi shikenan.

Ganin yadda Lailah take bin wayar da kallo kamar wacca bata cikin hayyacin yasa Afeefah taɓota....
Firgigit ta dawo hayyacin ta tana kallon ta, "menene Haka kuma ba saurayin ki bane zakiyi wani pretend a gaba na" Afeefah ta faɗa tana cin magani kamar da gaske....

Da sauri Lailah ta ce "ni wallahi banma sanshi bah kema dai kinsan ba abinda yake haɗa ni da maza hasali ma tsoron su nake saboda namiji ba abin yarda bane, ki duba saboda namiji zaynerb ta koma wata kalar Rayuwa Wanda Farin ciki Yayi ƙaranci a ciki."
Shiru tayi tana tunani,ita ko Afeefah ta zuba mata ido tana kallon ta gaskiya Lailah ta faɗa wasu mazan abin tsoro ne tunda gashi a dalilin ɗa namiji zaynerb ta faɗa mummunar hali amma ae ba duka aka haɗu aka zama ɗaya ba.

"Oh ohoo Yes na ganoshi" Lailah ta faɗa tana bin Afeefah da kallon tuhuma...
"Kin gano shi wayeshi?".  Kuma meyasa kikemin wannan kallon kamar nayi miki sata Afeefah ta faɗa cikin tsare gida.

"Bashi bane wannan guy ɗin dana ganku ɗazu wajen birthday ɗin zahrah Lailah" Ta faɗa tana kallon Afeefah.
Sannan ta ci gaba da faɗin "wato idan na fahimta ya takura miki saikin bashi lambar ki shine kika bashi nawa kamar yadda kika saba ko?."

Shi kuma harda kira na da murya like a sweet... mtsshhooiii taja tsaki.
Ita dai Afeefah dariya kawai takeyi tana mamakin kaifin tunani irin na Lailah  tanajin yadda Lailah ke ƙarewa sabbir cin mutunci.

"Kiyi dariya da kyau daga ke har shi saina koya muku hankali daga kaina bazai ƙara takurawa mata ba".
Lailah ta faɗa a kufule ganin yadda Afeefah ta mayar da ita abin dariya
Tsayawa Afeefah tayi da dariyar da takeyi tana kallon Lailah saikuma ta kuma fashewa da dariyar.
Tana nuna Lailah da yatsa Ta ce "kalleki yanzunnan kika gama cewa kina tsoron maza fa.
Saikuma kice zaki koya mishi hankali?". Shiru Lailah tayi ba ta  ce komai ba sai Faman muzurai da takeyi da hura hanci 👃.

Ganin haka yasa Afeefah ta miƙa mata wayarta.  
Duba ki gani na samo zazzafan dress ne, mai zafi fa,sosai zai taimakamin wajen shigata na gasa, Afeefah ta faɗa tana miƙawa Lailah wayar dan taga alamar kamar ranta ya baci.
Ko kallon wayar Lailah bata yiba ta miƙe zata bar ɗakin tana faɗin bani da Lokaci yanzu,amma wata gila gobe, dama dai kayan make-up ne toh wata ƙila na duba amma fah idan anci mugun sa'a.
Afeefah ba tace komai ba har Lailah ta fita. Ita ma tashi tayi ta fita, bedroom ɗin Zaynerb ta wuce.

Saman sallaya ta hango ta da alama sallah takeyi dan haka bata ƙarisa shiga ba ta juya bedroom ɗinta tana mamakin halin Zaynerb wani lokacin tana abu kamar mai hankali wani lokacin kuma kamar mai taɓin ƙwaƙwalwa ko kuma mai shafin aljanu 👹

Washe gari Afeefah kamar yadda ta saba bayan ta iddar da sallah tayi azkhar fita tayi, ɗakin Zaynerb ta shiga ta tashe tayi sallah sannan ta shiga na Haleesat da Lailah ta tashe su.

Sannan Kitchen ta wuce acan ta sama Auntie sa'adah tana aikin haɗa breakfast dayake ainihin ma'aikatan gidan mata Biyu ummi da Elina da Suke Aikin haɗa recipe na Gidan sun Tafi Gida hutun shekara,basu riga sun dawo ba.
Gaisawa sukayi cikin mutunci kamar yadda suka saba.

Afeefah matashiyar budurwa ce da bazata wuce shekaru ashirin zuwa da ɗaya ba a duniya, doguwa ce sosai highly gata akwai yalwar gashi har gadon baya yanayin fatar ta ita ba za'a kirata da fara ba, amma tafi ƙarfin a kirata da chocolate color ko Black beauty wata kalar fata gareta mai shinning gunin burgewa ga hancinta dogo mai tsini da yayi daidai da yanayin fuskarta bakinta ɗan ƙarami mai ɗauke da lips red gata da diri tabara kalla masha Allah, shalelen daddy wato MISTER MAI KYAU kenan, a hankali zamu tsunduma cikin wannan labari mai suna S⋆A⋆R⋆A⋆U⋆N⋆I⋆Y⋆A⋆R⋆♥︎K⋆Y⋆A⋆U⋆, domin jin wacece SARAUNIYAR nan, labari mai ɗauke da cin amana yaudara zamba cikin aminci chakwakiya hargitsi maƙirci danasani nishaɗantar wa game da zazzafar soyayya😜😜

Bata kulata ba ta ci gaba da cin breakfast ɗin ta.

ita kuma Lailah sanye take cikin abaya peach color wanda yayi daidai da kalar fatar ta, ta yafa veil ɗin abayar a akanta fuskar nan tasha heavy light make-up ta ƙarisa gurin daddy ta rungumeshi ta baya ta manna mishi sumba a gefan fuska tana faɗin "good morning Lovely dad!."

Ina kwana momy!!. Afeefah ta faɗa tana kallon abincin gabanta, ba ta re da da Momy Rafi'at ta kalleta ba ta ce lfy a taƙaice ita ma, suma su Lailah gaisheta sukayi ta amsa da sakin fuska.

Gyara zama Zaynerb tayi tana fuskan tar daddy cikin murya irinna zumuɗi ta ce "daddy nanda wata ɗaya  fa, za'a gudanar da zaɓan   S͓̽A͓̽R͓̽A͓̽U͓̽N͓̽I͓̽Y͓̽A͓̽R͓̽♥︎K͓̽Y͓̽A͓̽U͓̽ matakin jaha kuma sannan sunan Afeefah ya fito ma ina tunanin baka sama Labari ba,ko na ce SARAUNIYAR Gidan namu bata faɗa  maka ba" Zaynerb tayi maganar ne  tana kallon reacted ɗin  gabaɗaya mutanen dake a dining ɗin aikuwa dai tana lura da yadda saura kaɗan Momy Rafi'at ta qware da juice ɗin da take sha saboda tarin da yazo mata yi take ba qaqqautawa.


Da sauri Zaynerb ta miƙa mata ruwa tana mata sannu, ko kallon ruwan da zaynerb ke miƙa mata Momy Rafi'at batayi ba dan takaici gorar ruwan ever dake kusada da ita ta ɗauka sai da ta kwankwaɗe shi tas sannan ta aje gorar tana ɓallawa Zaynerb harara kamar idonta zai fito waje tsaban takaici  da tsanar da tayi mata.

   
Ita ko Lailah kasa haɗiye abincin bakinta tayi Dan kuwa maganar da Zaynerb ta faɗa a bazata ya zo mata da Ƙyar ta iya haɗiye abincin bakinta tana kallon daddy dan jin abinda zaice.

To Bangaren Haleesat ma Hakane sai dai ita ko a fuska bata nuna ba balle ka zargi wani abu ita ma dai zuba ma daddy ido tayi dan jin abinda zaice.

fuska washe Zaynerb take faɗin Hakane daddy zamuyi list ɗin abubuwan da ake buƙata duk da dai ita Afeefah ɗin bamusan ko wani kalan kaya zatayi amfani dashi a gurin ba Zaynerb tayi maganar tana maida hankalinta akan Afeefah.

"Zinaru ɗin ce ta faɗi hakan da kanta?", daddy ya tambaya cikin kaɗuwa suma dai su Haleesat baki wanga me suke kallon Afeefah xixin da ko one Year ba ta cikasa ba da zuwanta faransa karatu, zatace zata dawo bayan ita ta takura a paris take son yin karatu wajen Uncle Yunus harda su rashin lafiyar ƙarya dan daddy yaƙi Yarda daga farkon Lamarin....

A gurguje take shirin tana jin Humaira na ƙwala mata kira zasu tafi airport ɗauko "ABBA" zai dawo daga ƙasar Australia. Da sauri ta ƙara murza Jan baki a laɓɓanta tana kallon kanta a dress mirror saida tayi juyi sannan ta ɗauki wayoyinta  da handbag ɗinta ta fito tana yafa scarf ɗinta.


A palo kuwa Humaira ta cika tayi fam fam domin kuwa saura kaɗan ta fashe, da sauri ta ƙarisa saukowa downstairs ɗin da sassarfa ta ƙariso wajen Humaira bata tsaya jin abunda Humaira za ta ce ba ta jawo ta tana faɗin "afuwan my sis zo muje" ba yanda Humaira zatayi haka tabi bayan Jieddarh suka fita dan tuni Auntie tana jiransu a mota ita da Heesham da yake shi zai kaisu airport ɗin.



***
Sun isa airport lokacin saura five minutes jirgi Yayi Landing, dan haka suka tsaya jira basu wani daɗe suna jira ba jirgi ya sauka, a hankali fasinjoji suke saukowa duk gabaɗaya su jieddarh sun bada hankulansu suna jiran fitowar sa.


A hankali farin dattijo wanda akalla bazai wuce shekaru Hamsin zuwa da uku ba yake taka mattatakalan jirgi yana saukowa, Humaira ce ta fara hango shi aikuwa da sauri ta ƙarisa gurin shi bata tsaya jiran wani Abu ba ta rungumeshi tana faɗin "welcome Abba we miss you highly."

Murmushi kawai yake saki yana bubbuga bayan ta, lokacin ne su Jieddarh suka ƙariso gurin fuskar ko wannensu ɗauke da walwala.

"To ae saiki sakeshi ya huta ko, kin wani maƙalƙale shi, Like a baby girl!, Jieddarh ta faɗa cike da barkwanci dan kuwa duk, wanda yasan Jieddarh yasan ta da barkwanci,She`s flirting girl.


Dariya Humaira tayi tana faɗin "nayi kewar ABBA obaa highly shiyasa", ta faɗa lokacin da take sakin shi.


A haka dukansu suka yi hugging ɗin shi cike da farin ciki da Nishaɗi, Auntie ce ƙarshen yi da akazo kanta sai taƙi yin hugging ɗinshi wai tayi fushi saida aka gama da yara sannan za'a tuna da ita?.
Dariya sukayi dukansu hadda Abba Jieddarh ta ce "haba Auntie ae yanzu ba take akeyi ba tamu Abban mu yake ke kinyi tsufa kalli yadda kike tafiya kamar zaki yi kasa fah,  habah Auntyn mu kinyi zamanin ki fah wannan ƙarnin mune fah."


Dukan wasa Auntie ta kai mata da sauri ta ɓuya a bayan Abba tana dariya qwafa Auntie taja tana faɗin "zan kama ki ae fitinanniyar Yarinya kawai hmm hmm."

Dariya Abba yayi ya ce "ba ganewar da zatayi ae gaskiya ta faɗa dan haka a qyale min ɗiyata.


Heesham ya ce "da kunzo antafi gida a ƙarishe drama ɗin acan." 
"Aikuwa dai da zaifi armashi" cewar Humaira Ita ma.
Gabaɗaya suka ɗunguma gurin mota suka ƙarisa Heesham ya shiga mazaunin driver, Humaira kuma a gefan mai zaman banza, Yayinda Jieddarh dasu Auntie suke a kujerar baya Jieddarh tana nanuƙe da Abba a Haka har Gida.

🅷🅰🅻🅴🅴🆂🅰🆃♥︎🅜🅐🅘♥︎🅚🅨🅐🅤



Bayan fitan su Lailah school ɗaki ta koma kai tsaye Wayarta ta ɗauka ba tare da Wani ɓata lokaci ba, ta lalubo lambar xixi na ƙasar faransa ta danna kira, Sai dai a kashe wayar take.


 Yanzu ma Bayan tsawon mintuna Talatin, ta kuma kiran tana mai addu'ar Allah Yasa xixi ta ɗauka, saboda akwai maganar da take so ta mata, shin ko akan meyasa takeson dawowa Nigeria da karatunta bayan kuma ita ta zaɓa karatu a Abroad.



Cikin sa'a kuwa wannan karon xixi ta ɗaga kiran.

Wata nannauyar ajiyar zuciya Haleesat ta sauke tana mai zama akan Royal bed ɗinta.

"Hello!! xixi," Haleesat ta faɗa Cike zaquwa.
On the Other hand ƙasar faransa, xixi ta amsa da....

A tsakiyar palon ta tsaya tana ƙwaɗawa matar Gidan kira, faɗi Take  "Ummyn farhana", "Ummyn farhana!",  Da sauri wata ƴar dattijuwar mata wacca ba za ta wuce ƴar aikin gidan bane ta fito daga kitchen. Da sauri ta ƙariso gurin Zaynerb.....
Ganin ta Yasa Zaynerb ta sauke ajiyar zuciya sannan ta ce "Yawwa Babah dije matar Gidan tana nan kuwa?", Zaynerb ta tambaya Babah dije data fito daga Kitchen na Palon, ,Eh madam ki sama guri ki zauna bari na kira ta inaji Bacci ne ya ɗauketa kinsan masu juna biyu sai da hutawa", Babah dije ta faɗa tana nufar wani korido dazai sadata da bedroom ɗin matar Gidan.


Zama Zaynerb ɗin tayi tana latsa wayar ta kafin matar Gidan ta fito ilai kuwa bata daɗe da zama ba, Babah dije ta fito tana faɗin "gatanan zuwa bacci ne ya ɗauketa, amma fah batayi nisa ba domin kuwa ina magana ɗaya ta farka duk nisan bacci  irinna masu ciki,"  cewar babah dije lokacin data ƙariso gurin da Zaynerb ɗin take, Godiya Zaynerb ta mata da sauri dan ta fahimci tsohuwar shegen surutu ne da ita,dan wannan surutun da tayi ya fahimtar da Zaynerb ɗin haka domin ita ɗin ɗalibai lauyanci ne kuma zaqwaqwura don tasan makamar karatunta shiyasa da zarar ta haɗu mutun mai surutu ko wani abin take fahimta.
Ita kowa babah dije ta koma kitchen ta cigaba da abinda takeyi.

Zaynerb tana zaune can saiga wata ƙyakyawar mata wacca ba za ta wuce shekaru ashirin da bakwai ba, ta fito tana faɗin "ah ah Yau manyan baƙi ne a gidan namu ashe"  Ta faɗa tana zama a three sieter, Dariya Zaynerb tayi ta ce "uwan Triple yau kuma mune baƙin kodai cikin ne yasa ba'a gane mutane Yadda Ya kamata?"..
Dariya ita ma tayi sannan ta ce "ras nake gani kece dai kikayi nisa kinfa kai one week baki zo ba wallahi, Afeefah tafi ku kirki dukanku harsu Lailah ɗin don tafiku son zuwa nan alamu yana nuni da nan gaba ma saita fiku rike zumunci Yarda ya kamata.

"Okay tuba muke uwan Triple" Zaynerb ta faɗa tana miƙa mata kular hannun ta, Karɓa tayi tana faɗin "harda su tsaraba ne Allah yasa dai na'iya ci,"  Ta faɗa tana buɗe kular aiko tana arba da kwaɗonnan ta fara suburbuɗawa  Zaynerb godiya tana faɗin "dama yau da Wani irin kwaɗayin zogale na tashi gashi Allah ya aikomin ke Zaynerb ɗiyar Albarka dashi.

Ita dai Zaynerb latsa wayar ta take tana dariyar kwaɗayin masu ciki, "Ae kigama min dariya Allah kaimu ranar da zangan ki da ciki zan rama nima," sunainah ta faɗa ganin yadda Zaynerb ke mata dariyar kwaɗayi.
 
Da sauri Zaynerb ta ce "tuba nake Ummyn farhana kinsan Waya ne a hannuna, Don Haka ita ce ta bani dariya obaa  highly bawae keba."

Sunainah bata kulata ba ta ƙwalawa Babah dije mai aikinta kira, Da sauri dattijuwar tazo tana faɗin gani maman Ferhane!!😝😝.

Dariya Zaynerb tayi ta ce "babah dije ba "Ferhane" ake cewa ba "Farhana" ake cewa." 
Dariyar ita ma Babah dije tayi tana faɗin "ae wannan suna sai ku ƴan zamani ni ina zan iya cewa wani ferhane."
Sunainarh tana dariyar maganar Babah ta ce "Babah rabu da Zaynerb kawai, Plate zaki ɗaukomin a kitchen",  "To" Babah dije ta ce sannan taje kitchen ta ɗauko special plate ta kawo mata aikuwa ta ansa ta juye kwaɗonnan a plate ɗin nan ta hauci kamar ba gobe, Hannu baka hannu plate, Saida ta cinye tas ta sha Ruwa tana mai miƙa Appreciation to Allah.

Da mamaki Zaynerb ke kallon ta ganin ko minti biyar ba'ayi ba harta cinye,  "Lallai "Ummyn farhana" wannan ci haka anya ba Triple bane bamu sani ba," Zaro ido sunainarh tayi ta ce,  "Triple kuma Zaynerb na kaisu ina? ɗaya ma ya aka ƙare balle kuma har ƴan uku.

Dariya Zaynerb tayi ta ce "ainaga cikin minti biyar harkin cinye Kwaɗo cikin plate ba dole nace ƴan uku bane."
Murmushi sunainarh tayi ta ce "idan ma ƴan ukunne Allah ya bada masu albarka, ae Albarka ake nema ba wani abu ba." Zaynerb ta amsa da ameen, nan dai suka ci gaba da fira irinnasu na ƙawaye.

miƙewa Lailah tayi dan ta musu rakiya zuwa gurin motar su, sallama sukama su Zaynerb sannan suka fita Lailah na take musu baya suna ɗan taɓa fira suka fita.

Da dare Afeefah fitowar ta daga wanka kenan ta tsaya gaban dress mirror tana busar da doguwar sumar kanta da hand Drier saida ta busar da shi sannan ta nufi cloth set shiri tayi tsaf cikin kayan bacci riga fara mai taushi sai wando shima fari dogo kana ta ɗauki ribbons ta tufke gashin ta daya zubo mata har gadon baya, hula ta sa fara dayake ita ba ma'a bociyar sa kallabi bane, Saman doguwar softened stool ta zauna sannan ta ɗauki wayarta tana karanta Wani £nglish Novel.

Lailah ce ta shigo bedroom ɗin tana jan tsaki saman doguwar softened stool ta hangota a kwance ga waya a hannunta sai faman murmushi take ƙarisawa tayi ta zauna kusada ita.

Afeefah tana riƙe da waya tana zuba murmushi taji an wabce wayar, a firgice ta dubi Lailah dake sakin huci, malama bani wayata Lailah bata kulata ba ta koma ta zauna saman softened stool ɗin gefe tana dafe kanta.

Ganin haka yasa Afeefah tashi zaune tana kallon Lailah "what's wrong with you?", Afeefah ta tambaya Lailah lokacin da takai hannu goshinta da wuyanta tana so taji ko batajin daɗi ne Lailah bata kulata ba still.


Ganin haka yasa Afeefah ɗin dawowa kusa da ita ta zauna a ƙasa tana kallon ta a hankali ta kira sunan ta "Lailah!"

"ina cikin damuwa....
"Damuwa kuma?", Afeefah ta tambaya da sauri, girgiza kai Lailah tayi sannan ta ci gaba da cewa "ina cikin damuwa sosai, wallahi sister na rasa ya zanyi...
Shiru Afeefah tayi tana kallon ta ganin haka yasa Lailah ci gaba da faɗin "ina cikin damuwa guda biyu na farko akan family na kinga YaYa Maleek har yanzu ba'a san duniyar da yake ba gashi Daddy yaƙi sawa a nemo shi tunda ya bar gida har takai ta kawo ko maganar shi Daddy bayaso ayi mishi ga matsalar xixi ya kunno kai ta nace saita dawo gida gashi nayi nayi ta faɗamin abinda ke damunta taƙi ga shi ɗazu ɗazunnan munyi waya da Aunty Nailah faɗamin take wai zata dawo gida cuz sunyi rigima da mijinta, kuka fa ta kirani tanayi, A lokacin saina koma kamar ni ce YaYar ita kuma ƙanwar da kyar nasamu ta haƙura amma ta ce saita dawo gida gashi kinsan halin Daddy ba barinta zayyi ba tunda ga yadda tun farko yanayin auren nata ya kasance dama Daddy ba wani son auren yayi ba kin sani, ita kuma Aunty Nainarh munyi waya ita ma dai ba daɗi abin, wai dangin miji sun saka ta a gaba da maganar rashin haihuwa gashi ita kuma Momy ce matsalar Dan tun farko bata amince da Auren Aunty Nainarh ba, Toh kinji dai...
Lailah ta ƙarishe maganar tana mai jan dogon numfashi.
Tunda ta fara maganar Afeefah bata dakatar da ita ba saida taji tayi shiru.

Ajiyar zuciya Afeefah ta sauke tana kallon Lailah kallon mamaki dan kuwa gani take kamar wannan Lailah dake zaune a gabanta ba Lailah da ta sani bane, Lailah dai mace mai son kanta wacca bata damu da damuwar kowa ba sai nata kuma ba ruwanta da sha'anin kowa sai nata, Lailah da ake mata Kallon Haughty lady, Amma yau gata a gabanta tana faɗa mata damuwar wasu,Lallai.

Da wani ne ya faɗa mata wai cewa koda nan gaba ne Lailah zata damu da damuwar wani, ba zata yadda ba amma yau gashi taji kuma ta gani Lailah ce a gabanta tana koro mata damuwar wasu, Yaran Momy Rafi'at shida ne Abdulmaleek ne Babba, Sai masu bi mishi Nainarh nd Nailah twins ne, Sai kuma Haleesat wacca rana ɗaya aka haifesu da Zaynerb, Haleesat da safe ita kuma Zaynerb da Yamma aka haifeta sai kuma Lailah mai bima Haleesat, Sai Zinaru Auta sakamakon taci sunan kakar su ne mahaifiyar Momy Rafi'at Yasa ake ce mata xixi Yaran Momy Rafi'at Ba wasu shekaru masu Yawane a tsakanin su ba Haleesat da Lailah shekara biyu ne a tsakanin su, suma sauran Yaran duk shekara biyu biyu ne a tsakanin su kuma ko wanne halin sa ya banbanta, especially ma Lailah da halinta yafi banbanta dana sauran Yayyinta ana cewa halin Momy Rafi'at tayo a wasu lokutan.

Ganin kamar Afeefah ta shiga duniyar tunani ne yasa Lailah ɗan tafa hannu a fuskar Afeefah ɗin, "Hey my friend menene kina jina ma kuwa?,"  Lailah ta faɗa tana kallon yadda Afeefah ta zuba mata ido tana kallon ta.

Ita ma Lailah ɗan murmushin tayi tana kallon Afeefah ƙaunar ƴar uwan na ta na ƙara shiganta, "A'a shi wannan ba wani matsala bane sosai kawai dai Uncle Al'ameen ne ya takura min da maganar soyayya kuma ni kinsan tsarina ba soyayya a tafiya ta, Dariya Afeefah tayi "kina bani mamaki Lailah wani lokacin saiki ce wai ba soyayya a tsarin ki to haka zaki dogara ba aure ina tausayin ki duk ranar da soyayya ta shige ki ba zata miki shigan sauƙi ba Allah, Kuma da kike maganar Uncle Al'ameen ae naga ya daɗe yana ƙaunar ki kawai ki bada kai musha biki sister, Afeefah ta ƙarishe maganar tana dariyar shaqiyanci ganin yadda Lailah ta haɗe rai.

Tana so tasha whiskey ko kwalba ɗaya ne amma kuma tana tuna alƙawarin da tayi ma Afeefah na bazata ƙara shan wani abu daya danganci maye ba, Wanda wannan dalilin ne yasa ta  tashi ta ɗauro alwala a Bathroom, nafila tayi raka'a huɗu sannan tayi sallama Al qur'ani mai girma ta ɗauko tayi tilawa sannan Batareda ta cire hijjab ɗin dake jikin ta ba.
Ta tsaya gaban window tana shaƙar fresh air, Kamar da wasa ta hango mutum yana sanɗa ya nufi hanyar garden.
Tuni ta fahimci ko wacece Haleesat ce domin kuwa two months da suka gabata ta fahimci Haleesat tana satar hanya tana fita da dare, Ajiyar zuciya ta sauke sannan ta koma ta zauna saman bed tana mai yima Haleesat addu'ar shiryuwa a ƙasan ranta don banda wannan ba abinda zata iya mata.



Ita kuwa Haleesat tana fita ta nufi wata Jar mota ƙirar BMW dake a pake acan gefe, shiga tayi cikin motar ƴan mata ne aciki su uku biyu a gaban mota, Ɗaya a baya duk da Haleesat suka zama su huɗu.

Cire dogon hijab ɗin dake jikin ta tayi tare da Niƙab ɗin tana faɗin, "Oh God zufa huh", Budurwar dake kusa da ita tayi dariya tana faɗin "Baby leesat yau fa kin ɓata mana lokaci wannan tsayawa ɓadda kaman da kikayi".
"Ke dai rabu dani kawai, Kinji ki Zuree babe yanzu ma da ƙyar na fito, Samu nayi ba kowa a palo ma shiyasa kuka ganni, Cewar Haleesat lokacin aka tayar da motar suka bar gurin suna taɓa fira da budurwar da ta kira da Zuree babe da kuma sauran ƴan matan biyu dake gaban motar Feenah da kuma ɗayar budurwar Jenny.

Isarsu Night club ɗin suka fito a tare, Haleesat ce a tsakiya hannunta riƙe da hannun Zuree sai kuma Feenah ita ma hannunta riƙe da hannun Jenny shiga ciki sukayi, wa'iya zubilla ƴan mata ne kawai ciki kamar yaran arna ba wata suturar kirki bane a jikin su, abin dai ba ƙyan gani ga samari nan iri iri kuma abin da zai kuma ba mutum ta kaishi da mamaki shine zallah gurin Yaran Hausawa ne birjiq😭


Shigarsu wajen keda wuya, nanfa kiɗa ya canza manyan Yaran sun iso nan wajen ya ɗauki sowa da tafi faɗi, Suke ga manyan Yaran masu ji da lokaci sun iso ae dole a basu guri, don masu guri sun ƙariso, haka suke ta faɗi Cikin Yanayi irinna su.


Kujeru masu arnen kyau suka zauna akai Haleesat tare Zuree a kujera mai mazaunin mutum biyu sai Feenah tare da Jenny a ɗayar kujerar ita ma mai mazaunin mutum biyu sai fasa musu kai ake da yabo su kuma suna ƙarajin ba kamar ya su lokacin sune bawai bane😎


Wani ƙayatacen Tray na glass mai ɗauke da whiskey kala kala ma'aikacin gurin ya kawo ya aje saman glass table ɗin dake a gaban su sannan ya koma ya kawo wani tray ɗin kuma daban mai ɗauke da glass cups na zinare ya aje, Sannan cikin nutsuwa yake zuba musu whiskey ɗin acikin cups ɗin Haleesat ya fara miƙa ma dan kuwa ita ce jigon abun mahaifin ta yafi mahaifin su Zuree kuɗi nesa ba kusa ba shiyasa suke girmama ta ba kaɗan kaɗan ba shiyasa ta zama kamar ita ce shugaban su sai abin da ta faɗa ake bi.

Wani irin tsawa Haleesat ta bugawa ma'aikacin wanda yasa saida ya kusa zubar da whiskey ɗin dake hannun shi tsaban firgita da tsoro.
"Bala'i amma Kai wani kalan bagi dajen mutum ne ka taɓa ganin nasha whiskey a gurin nan, Ko shashancine da rashin kulawa yasa zaka bani whiskey saboda ka mayar dani nonsense wacca batasan abinda take yi ba.
Cike da masifa da kuma gadara Haleesat take maganar tana girgiza ƙafa, Game da sakin huci dan ƙiris ya rage ta kai mishi mari...
Gabaɗaya hankulan wasu daga cikin maza da mata na gurinne ya dawo kansu domin ganin abinda ke faruwa.

Shi ko ma'aikacin mai suna Vinood sai faman bata hakuri yakeyi duk yabi ya haɗa uban zufa jikin shi sai faman rawa yakeyi tsoron shi ɗaya shine karya ya rasa aikin shi a gurin.

Ganin Haka yasa Feenah wacca duk cikin su tafisu sanyin hali kuma bata cika san hayaniya ba, Alama tayiwa ma'aikacin daya tafi ya bar gurin, da sauri kuwa ya bar gurin har yana tuntuɓe.



Kamo hannun Haleesat Feenah tayi tana faɗin "please baby leesat ki kwantar da hankalin ki yanzu zai kawo miki abin da kike buƙata kinji?",  Feenah ta faɗa hakan ne saboda tasan Haleesat bata shan whiskey ko kuma Wani Abu daya danganci kayan maye...Tafi ganewa ta busha shisha ko wani abun, Amma bata shan kayan ruwa ma'ana abin da ya danganci maye. 
Ajiyar heart Haleesat ta sauke sannan ta ce "ba damuwa kawai dai ya ɓata min raine amma ba komai "forget about the pass".
Murmushi Feenah tayi "shiyasa nake son ki ta waje na akwai ki sauƙin hali", Feenah ta faɗa tana komawa ta zauna.
Feenah ƴace ga Alhaji Sulemanu  canji, shahararren ɗan canji ne a garin Abuja da kewaye, Yana da kuɗi obaa highly dan kuwa bayan harkar canji da yakeyi ɗan kasuwa ne shi na bugawa a jarida, Feenah itace ƴar shi ta biyar mahaifiyar ta ta rasu tun tana da shekaru sha biyu a duniya a gurin kishiyar uwanta take.

Yayinda Zuree take ƴa ga malam Adamu jama'are mahaifin ta shahararren malami ne a garin Abuja asalin shi ɗan garin jama'a re ne na jahar Bauchi, Kasuwanci ne da kuma malimta ya dawo dashi garin abuja har ya gina family ɗinshi anan yaran shi tara,  Zuwairiya wato Zuree ita ce ta fita zakka a yaran malam Adamu dan kuwa ƴar malam ce wacca taƙi halin malam.

Sai kuma Jennifer lopez, wato Jenny mahaifin ta cikake kuma shahararren mai dukiya ne a garin Abuja sannan kuma pastor ne wanda akeji dashi Jenny ta kasance, Ƴar shi ɗaya tilo tsoka ɗaya a miya shiyasa take abin da taga dama saboda a sangar ce iyayen ta suka raine ta bata san wani abu waishi wahala ba a rayuwa dukansu ajin su ɗaya a University sannan kuma course ɗaya suke karanta wato Microbiology.


Dogon tsaki Zuree taja tana faɗin, "Wallahi Baby Leesat kina Bani mamaki da takaici bansan ranar da zaki waye ba wallahi ke ba shan wannan kayan ruwa ba, Bakya taɓa harkar nan ke gabaɗayanki sai aslow wallahi", Tayi maganar cike da takaici.

Haleesat bata kulata ba da dan kuwa idan da sabo yaci ace ta saba da wannan tsoki burushun na Zuree.
Saima cewa da tayi, "Okay guys kuzo mu motsa Jiki mana,"
Gabaɗaya su ukun tashi sukayi gabaɗayan su suka hau kan dandamali nanfa akayi changing ɗin wakar speakers ɗin CLUB ɗin zuwa wani korean song na zamani, habba nanfa suka fara dancing kamar ba gobe cike da kwarewa suke rawan su abinsu ba ruwansu.




Tsaye take can ɗaga wani ɗan korido na club ɗin, Yayinda take ƙarewa club ɗin kallo dama society ɗin dake ciki.

Pin-up dress ne a jikin ta, Riga black color sai belt golden color da ta kama ƙugunta ta ɗaureshi, Rigar iya gwiwar ta tsaya sannan kuma ta kama ta ɗam, ga wani tsinannen takalmi hill a ƙafafunta kanta kuwa yasha kitson attach mai masifar tsada,  Hannunta na hagu kuwa zanen tattoo ne brown color.
Doguwa ce sosai dan kuwa a shekaru ba za ta haura ashirin da uku zuwa da huɗu ba, Idonta yana sanye cikin wani arnen glass no respect launin eye balls ɗinta kuwa abin burgewa ne ga mai kallon tsakiyarsu launin Blue color suke.

Hannyenta rings na diamond masu masifar kyau da tsada ne,  Ga wata arniyar chain na diamond a neck ɗinta siririya mai kyau da kuma tsada, Ear ɗinta kuwa ba`a magana wasu kalan earings ne a kunnenta na diamond amma kuma Yanayin shape ɗinsu cross ne, Fuskar ta kuwa tasha light make-up launin hair ɗin ta kuwa brown color ne dogo, ta fito sak Like a indian actress RAKUL PREET SINGH fara ce tas fatar ta har wani shaining takeyi saboda haske gashi kuma ya haɗe da jindaɗi Yanayinta tafi kala da mara sa son Hayaniya obaa highly.

Hannu tasa a pocket ɗin rigarta ta ciro wayar ta ƙirar iPhone 14promax daga inda take a tsaye take ɗaukar Haleesat a hoto, Tayi sunkai kala biyar sannan ta turawa wata number wanda akayi saving da MR. AMIGO sannan ta kira lambar cikin sa'a kuwa ringing ɗaya aka ɗauka magana ta fara cikin Harshen
farasanci, "Hello Mr. Amigo ga wasu pics nan na turo maka nan da gobe nakeso a samomin taƙaitaccen tarihin Yarinyar cikin pics ɗin in Tomorrow please."
On the other hand mutumin shima cikin Harshen farasanci ya ce "shikenan madam amma nace!,"
Bata tsaya jin abinda zai kuma cewa ba ta katse wayar ta mayar cikin gown pocket ɗin ta sannan ta fito daga cikin club ɗin gabaɗaya motar ta ƙirar bugatti ta shiga taja motar da gudu ta bar gurin.


A takaice dai su Haleesat basu suka bar club ɗinnan ba sai wuraren ƙarfe biyu da rabi na dare kamar yadda suka saba sauke ta sukayi a gurin da suka ɗauke ta ɗazu har zata fita a motar ta tsaya jin abinda Jenny take faɗa.
"Wai guys baku kula da wata red ɗin mota da take binmu ba tun ɗazu na lura da'ita fah, Tunda muka fito daga club Take binmu."

Ɗan juyawa sukayi a tare suna kallon inda Jenny ke nuna musu motar a daidai lokacin kuma motar dake binsu ƙirar Benz tayi ribas tabar gurin da speed, Tsaki Haleesat taja irin she don't care ɗinan "koma waye ne matsalar shine fa ba wai namu ba" Ta faɗa hakan yayin da take barin gurin bata tsaya jin abunda suke faɗa ba da sauri ta buɗe ƴar ƙaramar ƙofar da take bi ta gurin ta wuce.
Kai idan baka lura ba bama zakace da hanya a gurin ba balle har a sama ƙofar wucewa saboda gurin akwai wasu flowers da suka rufe ƙofar sai suka zama kamar kwalliya.

Cikin sanɗa take taku harta shigo palon gidan sannan in rushing ta wuce bedroom ɗinta.
Zaynerb da har gyangyaɗi ya fara ɗaukar ta, Baiwar Allah ajiyar zuciya ta sauke ganin Haleesat ta dawo cikin ƙoshin lafiya, Miƙewa tayi ta shige bathroom tanajin zuciyar ta ba daɗi, It's Necessary gareta ta sama Haleesat akan wannan satar hanyar da take tana zuwa Night Club, Ba tare da sanin ƴan gida ba.
Bayan wasu mintuna ta fito ɗaure da brown towel a chest ɗinta ko lotion bata tsaya shafawa ba cikin sauri sauri ta shiga cloth set ta shirya cikin sleeping dress riga na saka maroon color doguwa, Saman bed ta hau tayi addu'ar bacci sannan ta lumshe kyawawan idanun ta bayan wani lokaci bacci yayi awon gaba da ita.

Haleesat kuwa tana shiga bedroom ɗinta kai tsaye bathroom ta shige bayan ta cire dogon hijab ɗin dake jikin ta da niƙab da ta qwaqwama.
After some minutes ta fito jikinta sanye cikin bathrobe gaban dress mirror ta tsaya tana ƙara goge sumarta, da ɗan ƙaramin towel ɗin dake hannunta zama tayi akan stool na madubi tana shafa lotion Bayan ta gama busar da gashin kannata saida ta kammala shafa lotion ɗin, Sannan ta miƙe ta shiga cloth set ta shirya cikin pyjamas mai taushi milk color, System ɗinta ta ɗauka ta koma saman mattress ta zauna tana sarrafa system ɗin ta ɗauki lokaci tana amfani da system ɗin sannan ta mayar da system ɗin ma'ajiyar ta, Kana ta koma saman bed ɗin ta kwanta, Pillows ta rungumo a chest ɗinta tana sauke ajiyar zuciya ba batun addu'ar bacci a haka har sarkin masu iya sata yayi gaba da ita.


Can cikin baccinta mai daɗi taji ana tashin ta ana jijjigata ana kiran sunanta.
A hankali take buɗe lumtsatstsun idonta mai cike da bacci, Biji biji ta fara ganin mutum a gabanta kafin idanun ta suka buɗe duka.
 Afeefah ce tsaye a gabanta tana sakin murmushi, riga ce short a jikin ta iya ka gwiwar ta sai dogon wando ga apron blue color da ta ɗaura a saman rigar jikinta, Kanta kuwa hular apron ɗinne shima blue color da alama kitchen zata shiga.


"Aunty Zaynerb"  "Aunty Zaynerb!! ki tashi mana tun ɗazu naketa faman tashin ki, kitchen zamu shiga kinsan yaufa Aunty sa'adah bata ta tafi garinsu,"  Afeefah ce ke maganar tana tashin Zaynerb.

A Hankali ta sauko daga saman mattress ɗin miƙa tayi tana karanto addu'ar tashi daga bacci clock ɗin dake manne a wall na ɗakin takalla ƙarfe bakwai na safe da sauri ta diro daga saman gadon tana faɗin "Yah Allah yaushe lokaci ya tafi haka ban tashi nayi sallah ba,"  Bathroom ta shiga da sauri bayan wani lokaci ta fito ɗaure da towel fari a ƙirjinta dubawa inda tabar Afeefah tayi, Bata da alama ta fita ne, Cikin sauri ta zura doguwar riga, A jikin ta sannan ta saka hijab ta tayar da sallan asuba bayan ta iddar cire hijjab ɗin tayi sannan ta fito kitchen ta Shiga.

A can ta samesu da Lailah suna kici kicin haɗa breakfast.
"Yauwa Aunty Zaynerb please ɗan kira Haleesat tazo muyi mu kammala haɗa breakfast ɗinnan tare", Cewar Afeefah ba tare da kalla Zaynerb ɗin ba.

Fita Zaynerb ɗin tayi ɗakin Haleesat ta shiga bata ciki dubawa bathroom tayi nanma bata,  Fitowa tayi zata koma kitchen ɗin a palo sukayi karo da Haleesat Jogging suit ne a jikin ta da alama daga morning Exercise take.
"Good Haleesat ki shirya Yau dai kitchen za'a shiga dan haka ki shiga ki shirya muna kitchen muna Jiranki, Kinsan dai Yau Aunty sa'adah bata nan kuma su Ummi da Elina Basunan".
Zaynerb ta ƙarishe maganar tana bin ta gefan Haleesat zata wuce, Da sauri Haleesat ta sha gabanta a ɗan hasale ta fara magana "waime? ni Haleesat ni ce zan shiga kitchen ɗin huh to akan wani dalili?".
Murmushi Zaynerb tayi sannan ta ce "akan dalilin da yasa kike cin abinci mana" tayi maganar tana kuma bi ta gefan Haleesat zata wuce, "Yauwa kuma kiyi sauri fa, dan kinsan umarnin shalelen Daddy ne ba'a sanya ko jin kiri, Idan kuma akayi tofa rai yana ɓaci kinsan da wannan.
Tana gama faɗin haka tayi gaba abinta.
Tabar Haleesat da cizon yatsa tsaki taja tana bin Zaynerb ɗin da kallon banza dama can basa ga maciji da Zaynerb ɗin cuz indai zasu zauna guri guda tofa saisun raba hali.
Ɗakinta ta shige dayake ɗakunan nasu a jere suke ɗakin Afeefah ne a farko sai na Lailah sai kuma na Zaynerb dake ɗayan Wall side ɗin sannan sai na Haleesat a ƙarshe.

Zaynerb kuwa tana koma wa kitchen, Guri ta samu ta zauna a ɗaya daga cikin chair's na dinning ɗin dake a kitchen ɗin dayake kitchen ɗin babba ne highly.

Kallon Lailah tayi ta ce "Ke Lailah ba haka ake yanka cabbage ba kinje kinayi manya manya keda zaki yanka ƙana na haba, duh Allah ki dinga kula mana.

A hasale Lailah ta juyo, dama da Yayah Afeefah ta samu ta shigo kitchen ɗin gashi tun ba'ayi nisa ba Zaynerb ta fara kushe ta don jan ayi tsiya, ta ce "dan Allah sister Zaynerb zo ki yanka da kanki mana, Haba ni dama na gaji wallahi," Ta faɗa tana dafe goshi duk tabi ta haɗa uban zufa kamar mai amfani da ita ce.

Dariyar ƙetah Zaynerb Tayi sannan ta ce "a'a ba ruwana ae girki sai dai ku Lailah ni dai barni anan inda nake" Ta faɗa tana latsa tsadaddiyar wayar ta, Lailah zata ƙara magana Afeefah tayi mata inkiya da ido alamar tayi shiru qwafa taja kawai ta ci gaba da yayyanka cabbage ɗin da take cikin nutsuwa da rashin qwarewa a fannin.

Jin shiru Haleesat bata zo ba yasa,  Zaynerb miƙewa tsaye saboda tsaban rigima irinna ta, Ita ba aikin takeyi ba, Amma don neman magana zata je kiran wata, Tana shirin fita daga kitchen ɗin, Saiga Haleesat ta shigo kitchen ɗin sanye cikin riga doguwa da dogon wando jeans.

"Yauwa ɗiyar albarka kin shigo, To ga rigar girki ki sanya ko sister"  Zaynerb ta faɗa tana miƙa mata apron karɓa Haleesat tayi zata ɗaura, Da sauri Zaynerb ta ƙarisa ta bayan Haleesat ɗin ta ɗaure mata igiyar rigar,  "Yauwa sis a fara aiki"  Cewar Zaynerb tana komawa ta zauna.

Ita dai Haleesat bata kula ta ba, Hasali ma earpiece ta maƙala a kunen ta duka biyun, Kama sumu aikin tayi suka ci gaba da fafatawa dama can ita Haleesat gwana ce wajen iya girki kala kala domin har lecture na koyon girki take zuwa kawai dai shiga kitchen ɗinne ba ra'ayinta ba, A haka suka ci gaba da yi Zaynerb na zaune sai dai idan taga sunyi ba daidai ba tasa baki kuma fah kar kuyi tunanin ta iya ne a'a ko ɗaya ba wani girkin azo a gani ta iya ba kawai tonon tsiwa ne irin nata, Daga  ƙarseh ma ɗaukar wayarta tayi tana musu video wai zata ɗaura a Yanar gizo ga ƴaƴan MISTER MAI KYAU guda a kitchen suna girki NEW UPDATE  kenan🤣😂😅.


Lailah ce da surutun Zaynerb ya isheta ta juyo tana kallon Zaynerb ɗin ta ce "sister Zaynerb dan Allah zaki iya fita ki bamu guri keba aiki ba sai surutun tsiya haba,"
Zaynerb bata kulata ba ta ci gaba da nuna ma Haleesat wai yadda ake ɓare Maggi mai star, "ke Haleesat na faɗa miki ba haka ake ɓare Maggi ba kin ƙi ganewa, A haka ae zubewa yakeyi haba sis a dinga kula mana," Zaynerb tayi maganar tana miƙewa bayan tayi saving video ɗin data gama ɗaukarsu, Ƙarisawa tayi gurin Haleesat ta karɓa ɓare maggin, "Yauwa to ɗauka wani aikin bari na tayaki wannan🤔.


Ita dai Afeefah sai daɗi takeji Zaynerb ta fara dawo walwalarta kamar da, Bayan lokaci mai tsayin gaske don shekaru biyar baya da Rayuwarta ta shiga tasko farin ciki yayi ƙaranci ciki kuma ya rushe, Bata irin waɗannan abun, Amma yanzu da takeyi hakan na nufin ta manta komai kenan ta barma Allah zaɓinta?.

Haka suka ci gaba da aikin su Zaynerb nayi musu gyara kamar itace boss.
Cikin ɗan ƙanƙanin lokaci suka kammala haɗa haɗaɗɗen breakfast mai rai da lafiya sannan na Alfarma.
Jerewa sukayi a dinning Room sannan ko wace ta shige haɗaɗɗen bedroom ɗinta domin shiryawa.

Zaynerb tana shiga bedroom ɗin ta bathroom ta shiga takai minti talatin sannan ta fito ɗaure da towel blue color a chest ɗinta, Cikin sauri sauri ta shirya cikin kayan hausawa Atamfa pink mai ratsin fari anyima Atamfar zanen flowers doguwar riga ce mai dogon hannu, Sannan ta kafa ɗaurin ture kaga tsiya kayan sunyi masifar yi mata kyau kamar kamar me, Dama kuma gata mai kyau, Ɗiyar mai kyau, Jikar kyawawa, Feshe Jikin ta tayi da perfumes kala kala masu tsada da sanyin ƙamshi.
Sannan ta fito daga bedroom ɗinta tana baza ƙamshi, dukansu suna zaune a dinning room ɗin suna cin breakfast hadda Daddy MISTER MAI KYAU da Momy Rafi'at, ƙarisawa tayi taja kujera ta zauna tana greetings ɗin Daddy da Momy Rafi'at fuska sake Daddy ya amsa mata gaisuwar har yana tambayar lafiyarta duk da kasancewar lafiyarta ƙalau.
Ita kowa Momy Rafi'at da kyar ta amsa kamar anyi mata dole wai danma taga mijinta MAI KYAU yana gurin ne,  Zaynerb kuwa ko a jikin ta da irin amsa gaisuwar ta da Momy Rafi'at tayi, Murmushi ma tayi dan kuwa ita kaɗai tasan irin tsiyar da ta shirya mata, Jira take taga yadda za'ayi aje china a Yau.


...

Bayan sun kammala cin breakfast ɗin palo suka dawo suka zazzauna dayake yau su Lailah basu da lectures Haleesat ce ma take da lectures ɗin a yau amma sai zuwa ƙarfe uku na Yamma.
Lailah da Haleesat ne a palon sai Afeefah suna jiran Mister mai kyau ne dayake zasu rakashi airport zai wuce  germany a yau.
Yayinda Zaynerb ta koma ɗakinta bayan ta ce tana fama da headache sama sama,  Sai da Zaynerb ta tabbatar da sun fita raka Daddy airport ɗin, Sannan ta fito palon ba kowa dan gabaɗaya Ma`aikatan gidan da masu gyara palon ma sun gama sun koma ɓangaren su, Wanda hakan ba ƙaramin daɗi yayima Zaynerb ba, Don zatafi samun aiwatar da shirinta in carefully.

Upstairs ta hau bedroom ɗin Momy Rafi'at ta nufa kanta tsaye ɗan buɗe ƙofar bedroom ɗin tayi, Ta leƙa kanta a ciki ba kowa shiga tayi dukanta, Ƙaran ruwa taji a toilet da alama wanka takeyi dube dube ta fara a ɗakin amma bata ga komai ba jin alamar an taɓa ƙofar toilet ɗin za'a fito ne yasa ta fita da sauri daga ɗakin.

Kitchen dayake anan Up ɗin ta shiga da sauri sauri bayan wani lokaci ta fito hannun ta ɗauke da robar mai.
A bakin ƙofar ɗakin Momy Rafi'at ta tsaya, Aje robar man tayi a gefe sannan ta ɗauko mopper ta aje a gefe shima,  wannan robar man data fara ajewa, ta ɗauko ta juye shi tas a ƙofar ɗakin momyn sannan ta ɗauko moppern ta fara gogewa ta yanda ba za'aga alamar mai ba a gurin ba.
Cikin sauri sauri take aikin don bata buƙatar Momy Rafi'at ta fito ta sameta gurin, Saida ta kammala tas sannan ta mayar da moppern ma'ajiyar ta, roban man ma ta mayar kitchen da sauri sauri ta koma bedroom ɗinta.


AFTER SOME MINUTES

Jin Ƙarar shigowar motar su Afeefah da alama dai har sun dawo daga airport ɗinne.

Palo suka zauna suna ɗan taɓa fira ita ma Zaynerb ɗin ta sauko Down ta dawo palon ta kwanta a saman three sieter tana dafe kanta kamar ciyon kan gaskiya su Afeefah sai sannu suke mata cike da tausayi dan kuwa suna ƙaunar junan su highly.
Haleesat ce ta kalli Zaynerb cike da kulawa ta ce "ki shiga medical room mana ki ɗauki magani kinji sis,"  
Ƙasa ƙasa Zaynerb take kallon Haleesat ɗin sannan ta maƙale murya ta ce "karki damu sister nasha fa", T a faɗa da kyar kamar mai ciwon baki, Lailah ta ɗan yi murmushi ta ce "Lallai su Aunty Zaynerb an buɗe file kenan baki ya mutu kamar ba kece mai surutu game da ɗaukar video ɗazu ba,"
Zaynerb bata kulata ba don tafi kowa sanin halin Laila da tsokanar ta, "Sannu kinji sister" cewar Afeefah tana taɓa goshin Zaynerb ɗin taji koda zafi, "Yauwa ƙanwata"  Zaynerb ta amsa tana lumshe ido.




𝔐ℜ𝔖_𝔐𝔄ℑ_𝔎𝔜𝔄𝔘



Momy Rafi'at ta gama shirinta tsab na zuwa china da mummunar ƙuduri ta shirya tsab cikin expensive material fari tas da mayafi mai tsada, Ga jakarta shima fari tas ta fito sai baza ƙamshi takeyi, Tafiya takeyi bata kallon gabanta sauri take don saura mintuna goma jirgin su ya tashi ita bata masan ya akayi ta ɓata lokaci haka bata shirya ba.
Cikin tafiyarta batasan ya akayi ba haka zalika bata lura da mai ta taka ba, Sai dai kawai taji wani uban zamiya game da zantsi ya kwashe ta, Aikuwa sai dai tim tittim tim akeji ta faɗi saman tiles, Bata dandara ba ta miƙe da kyar tana dafe ƙuƙurunta saboda wani uban azaba da takeji aikuwa miƙewan data kuma yi santsi ya kuma ɗibarta fiye da farko, Saiga Momy Rafi'at tana faɗowa daga saman staircases gabaɗaya bata dena juyi ba saida tayo kasa tun daga saman bene saida ta dangana da ƙasa.

Da gudu su Haleesat sukayo kanta suna kiran sunan ta amma ina Momy Rafi'at tayi sumar wucin gadi, Dan kuwa ba ƙaramin buguwa tayi ba, Ƙunƙurunta da bayan ta dama sauran jikin ta sai faman kiran sunanta suke cikin tashin hankali,  Amma ina Momy Rafi'at bata ma jinsu dan a sume take.
Dq sauri Lailah ta ɗauko ruwa a fridge ta yayyafa mata shiru bata farfaɗo ba,  Haleesat hadda kukanta ganin haka yasa Afeefah ƙara yayyafa mata ruwan sai gashi ta farfaɗo tana ihu tana faɗin bayan ta zai ɓalle jikinta ciwo maganar take kamar bata hayyacinta.
Ganin ta kasa tashi yasa Afeefah ta kalli Lailah "Sister mu kaita hospital kawai zaifi, don na fahimci ba ƙaramin buguwa tayi ba,"  Cewar Afeefah tana kuma kallon yadda Momy Rafi'at take ihu kamar wata karamar yarinya, Da sauri suka taru su ukun banda Zaynerb wacca take kwance a three sieter tana baccin ƙarya, Da kyar suka iya cicci ɓarta suka nufa waje da ita a mota suka sakata sai faman haki suke, Ko tsayawa jiran driver da escort basuyi ba, Afeefah ce taja motar da gudu suka bar gidan zuwa hospital dan ma sunsa mayafan su tun kafin su fito.


Zaynerb kuwa tana tabbatar da sun bar gidan ta fashe da dariya hadda riƙe ciki, A fili ta furta Allah ya ƙara Yasin banji tausayinta ba, Don bata da imani wannan matar har kisan kai zata iya yi tama yi bama zata iya ba, Tsaki taja tana tuna yadda tasha azaba a hannun Momy Rafi'at kafin abin ƙaunar su mahaifin su MISTER MAI KYAU ya ɗaukesu ya mayar dasu Germany.

Zama tayi tana tuna wani lokaci shekaru GOMA SHA ƊAYA baya da Momy Rafi'at take dukan Afeefah, Zaynerb tana kuka tana roƙonta ta dena duka mata ƴar uwanta amma Momy Rafi'at taƙi ji tohm ana cikin haka saiga Daddy yayi dawowar bazata yayi kyakyawar gani kuwa Momy Rafi'at tana dukan shalelen shi da wayar wuta aikuwa a lokacin saida ya saki Momy Rafi'at, Sannan ya ƙwace Yaran shi duka amma banda su Lailah ya bar ƙasar dasu a lokacin sai da kyar aka samu ya mayar da Momy Rafi'at.

Ajiyar zuciya ta sauke sannan ta tashi ta hau Up da sauri sauri ta goge duk gurin da ta tabbatar tasa mai sannan ta dawo palo tayi kwanciyar ta abinta.

Koda su Afeefah suka isa asibiti an duba Momy Rafi'at an tabbatar da ta samu goje war ƙashi da wasu gurare a jikin ta da ta sama karaya basu suka baro asibitin ba sai wajen ƙarfe biyu, Gida suka dawo da ita tayi jinyar anan don ita ta zaɓa hakan, Nanfa ƴan uwa da abokan arziki da suke a kusa suka fara zuwa duba jikin ta, Gashi daddy baya ƙasar dole sai Afeefah ce ta kira shi a waya a faɗa mishi Halin da ake ciki.
