Zaune suke a dinning room suna lunch, Yayinda ƴan aikin gidan ke jere musu lafiyayun Abincicika kala kala masu rai da lafiya wasu kuma na aikin serving ɗinsu cikin nutsuwa, dukansu ƴan gidan ne suke a dinning ɗin, Jieddarh, Humaira sai Heesham da Auntie sai kuma Abba wato Alhaji Jafar Komai Naka.
Dayake dinning table ɗin mai mazaunin mutum takwas ne, cikin nutsuwa suke yin lunch ɗin abinsu.

Bilkisu ce ƴar aikin su budurwa mai kimanin shekaru sha tara ta ƙariso da saurinta har ta kusa faɗi saboda sauri gurin Heesham ta ƙarisa cikin rawan jiki take serving ɗinshi yayinda ta kafeshi da ido kamar zata cinyeshi ɗanye saboda so da ƙauna, Shi kuwa baima kula da irin kallon da take mishi ba gabaɗaya yafi mayar da hankalin shi wajen kaima cikin shi haƙƙin da.
Ganin ta gama serving ɗinshi amma bata bar gurin ba yasa shi dakatawa da cin abinci da yakeyi ya ɗago da kanshi yana kallon ta.
Ae bilkisu sai da ta kusa sumewa ganin yau ta ciri tuta ya kalleta, "malama baki gama bane ko dai kina jiran wani abinne?,"
Ya tambaye ta cikin tsare gida da ɗan faɗa ganin yadda ta ƙureshi da ido, Da sauri ta ce "na gama oga,"  gurin Jieddarh ta ƙarisa tana serving ɗinta amma gabaɗaya hankalinta yana kan Heesham wanda shi baimasan tanayi ba.

Kaf gurin ba wanda ya kula da irin kallon da take mishi sai Jieddarh cikin zuciyar ta ta ce " Wow kaga wani kalan love aikuwa sun dace da juna ,Allah ta biyo ta hannuna zan taimaka mata", Ganin yadda Bilkisu ta wani ƙurawa Heesham ido kamar yau ta fara kallon shi dama ta lura da take taken Bilkisu kwana biyu akan YaYannata Heesham tunda ya dawo daga karatu ta ganshi gabaɗaya bata cikin nutsuwar ta.
Ɗan gyaran murya jieddarh tayi sannan ta kalli Bilkisu ta ce "please  Bilkisu ɗan ɗaukomin maltina mai sanyi a fridge mana,"  Kallon Jieddarh Bilkisu tayi kawai saboda, ta katse mata jindaɗinta shine zata wani ce, Ta ɗauko mata maltina mai sanyi a  fridge bayan ga wasu drinks ɗin a gabanta daɗin daɗawa kuma ae tafi ta kusa da fridge ɗin.
Tana ƙunƙuni taje ta buɗe fridge ɗin ta ɗauko mata, "gashi" ta miƙa mata ba tare da ta buɗe mata ba, "Ɗan buɗe min mana" jieddarh ta faɗa tana kallon yadda Bilkisu ta ɓata fuska ga Heesham ya kusa barin gurin bata gama ƙare mishi kallo san ranta ba😂.

"Buɗe mata mana Bilki ko ba ƙyaji ne" Cewar Auntie ganin yadda Bilkisu ta zuba ma jieddarh ido tana kallon ta kamar bata fahimci mai take nufi ba.


Da sauri ta buɗe miƙa mata tayi tanaji kamar ta mari jieddarh ɗin dan haushi, Tana shirin barin dining Area ɗin Jieddarh tayi saurin cewa "please Bilki ɗan dafamin noodles mana na kasa cin wannan abincin na gaba na," Tayi maganar tana ture plate ɗin gabanta.

"Ba Ƙya jin daɗine mamana naga yanayin ki kamar baki da lafiya" Abba ne ya tambayi jieddarh cikin kulawa, Lafiya qalau Abba kawai bana cin wannan abincin ne gwara a dafamin ko noodles ɗin naji ko zan iya ci.


"Okay Toke Bilki kinji don haka jeki dafa mata noodles ɗin mana kin tsaya kina kallon ta kamar baki santa ba," Auntie tayi maganar tana hararar Bilki danta tsana talaka.

Jieddarh fah ta rantse saita hana Bilki kallon Heesham cike da takaici Bilki ta wuce kitchen tana tsaki cikin ranta sai dai ba yadda ta iya, Cikin minti biyar ta kammala girka mata noodles ɗin zubo mata shi tayi a plate ta kawo mata, A lokacin Auntie bata a gurin ta kammala Humaira ita ma ta tashi jieddarh ne da Abba sai Heesham a gurin, Aje mata tayi a gabanta " To gashi nan.
Ɗan murmushin rainin wayo jieddarh tayi ta ce "to gashi naga kamar kinajin yunwa malama Bilkisu mai gadon zinare zauna muci kawai dan naji na ƙoshi."
Ɗan waro ido Bilki tayi jin abinda jieddarh ta ce, Yo ae ita ko karen hauka ne ya cijeta ba zataci wannan girkin ba, "kici mana naga fa tun safe kike zurzurga baki samu kinci abinci ba" muryar jieddarh ne ya dawo da ita daga duniyar tunanin da ta lula, Ganin yadda Abba Alhaji Jafar Komai Naka da kuma, masoyinta Heesham suka zuba mata ido ne yasa ta zauna a zuciyar ta ta ce "yau na mutu sai dai kuma gawata Yau dai jieddarh ta Rantse saita kasheni🤔.

Aje mata plate na noodles ɗin jieddarh tayi a gabanta "gashi Bilkisu mai gadon zinare muci ko,"  ƙara gyara zaman ta tayi sannan ta ɗauki spoon ta ɗi ba takai bakinta tana rumtse ido, ae ba shiri ta fito dashi jin wani uban yaji ga gishiri game da uban maggi da yayiwa abincin yawa tari ta fara ba qaqqautawa kamar zata shiɗe, Da sauri jieddarh ta miƙa mata gorar ruwa na Mr. V.

Hannu na rawa ta karɓi ruwan saida ta shanye ruwan tas sannan ta sauke gorar daga bakinta tana sauke ajiyar zuciya da sauri ta ɗauki maltinar data ɗaukowa jieddarh bata shaba, ta kafa a bakinta tas ta shanye maltinar harda karkaɗe kwalbar, Dariya ma taba su jieddarh ganin yadda take karkaɗe robar , na gode Bilki ta faɗa da sauri lokacin da ta ɗauki plate ɗin indomie ɗin tabar gurin kitchen ta shiga da sauri.

jieddarh na kiranta amma ina tayi gaba ko juyowa batayi ba, dariya jieddarh takeyi dama shiyasa taƙi cin indomie ɗin dan kuwa tasan da wuya idan Bilki bata shirya mata mugunta a ciki ba gashi abinda take zargi ya tabbata, Ita ko Bilki koda ta shiga kitchen ta ƙofar kitchen ɗin ta fita ta koma sashen su na ƴan aiki cike da takaicin jieddarh da ta sa ta shan yaji ba tare tayi shiri ba, gashi ko gama kallon masoyinta Heesham ba tayi ba, A fili ta ce Allah ya isa na Jieddarh'

"Wai kuwa gwaggo na nace yanzu muna sauka a motar nan shikenan mun shiga birni ko kuma ma a birnin garin habuja ko?, daga yau shikenan bani ba ƙauyenmu,bah su delu babu ƴan ƙauyenmu balle kuma na rama abinda sukamin shikenan sun shani a musilla."

Ta ci gaba da cewa "waima gwaggo na yanzu aikin me zan dinga yine idan munje gidan aikin da zaki kaini?, bata tsaya ba ta ci gaba da cewa "kuma shikenan kina kaini zaki dawo gida kiyi auren ki, abin ki, Ki barni a Habuja ni kaɗai bansan kowa ba kowa bai sanni ba, kawai saita fashe da kuka, Gwaggo ta kalleta cike da takaici ta ce "au yanzu dije tun kafin na kaiki gidan aikin zaki faramin kuka kamar na dake ki faɗamin na dake kine ko kuwa gidan aikin kikeyi ma kukan ban sani bah."

Cikin muryar kuka budurwar ta ce "toh gwaggo ba kece kika ce a birni zaki barni ba ni kaɗai ba, kuma ae nace miki kiyi hakuri bazan ƙara faɗa dasu hanne ba, kuma fah su suka fara tona na shine ni kuma na rama kuma ke gwaggo ma ae kinsan sarkin yawa yafi sarkin ƙarfi shine sukamin duka, gefe ɗaya kuma ga dukan da wannan matar tayimin gashi jiki na baigama warkewa bah, fuska ta ma haka shi ne kika kawo ni aiki don a ƙarisani."

Ɗan tausayinta ne ya kama gwaggon nata, a hankali gwaggon ta ce "ae ke ce dije na hanaki wasan banza dasu hanne amma ba kyaji sannan kuma da kike cewa na kawoki aiki birni ae mahaifin ki shine ya ce a kawo ki aiki birnin bani ba.".

shiru budurwar tayi tana kallon gwaggon sai kuma tayi murmushi ta ce "toh gwaggo makaranta na fah zan ci gaba da zuwa anan abujan kuwa?, ɗan shiru gwaggon tayi kamar mai tunani sai kuma can ta ce "eh to idan kika nutsu highly, ƴan gidan suka ga irin nutsuwar ki wata ƙila su iya saki a makarantar.


Dariya tayi cike da farin ciki da yarinta kamar ba ita ce mai kuka yanzu ba ta ce "shikenan yanzu sunana ya tashi daga dije sai dai Nanah ko kuma ace min Deejarth ko Deejarh irinna ƴan birni, na bar su hanne da wahalar ƙauye da sunan ƙauye," ta kuma gyara zaman ta a motar tana kallon cikin garin abuja ta glass ɗin motar, sannan  ta kuma juyowa tana kallon gwaggo ta ce "gwaggo yanzu fah, su ƴan birni sunan su bah irin na ƙauye bane koh?," gwaggo tayi dariya ta ce "sosai ma, aisu ƴan  birni sunan su na daban ne, special ne in miki na turawa.


Dije ta ce "ae ina faɗamiki gwaggo na muna zuwa gidan aka tambaye ni sunana zance Deejarh ta faɗa tana nuna yadda zatayi wajen faɗan sunan nata idan aka tambaye ta, wannan karon ba iya gwaggo ne tayi dariyar ba hadda sauran ƴan motan wata mata a motar ta ce "Deejarh aikuwa suna mai daɗi, it's nice name."

Dije tana yanga ta ce "tnx" wai irinna ƴan birni matar ta kuma cewa "hala daga ƙauye kike, Ah ah su Dije an sama guri ga abokiyar hira ta samu ta ce "Yasin kuwa kamar kin sani, daga ƙauye nake amma yanzu na shigo birni ae," matar da alama ita ma "A" ce a gurin surutu ta ce "to su sunan ƴan ƙauyen da naji kina faɗa ya suke?.
wannan karon kallon matar Dije tayi highly, ta dai ga alama gilama ko sunan ƙauye matar bata sani ba.

Hadda sauke Ajiyar zuciya tayi sannan ta ce "sunan ƴan ƙauye yanzu kamar dai ace sunan ki Hafsat a birni tofah a ƙauye ina faɗamiki sai dai a kira ki da hanse ko kuma ace hansi ko kuma hansatuwa 🤣😂😅.
 
ko kuma sunan ki sa'adatu a birni tofah ina faɗamiki idan a ƙauye ne sai dai ace miki sa'ade ko kuma a kira da sa'a duk da dai sunan gwaggo nane sa'aden amma Yasin na faɗa.

Gwaggo dake gefe ta ce "ae dama kin rainani shiyasa kike kiran sunana haka gatsal"  Dije tayi dariya ta ce "gwaggo kenan ae wannan bayani nakeyi shiyasa kikaji na faɗa sunan ki a haka,"  Dije ta kuma kallon matar ta ce "ni kuma yanzu kamar ni sunana "khadeejah" amma a ƙauye kinsan me ake cemin kuwa?, matar tana dariyar Dije ta ce "a'a saikin faɗa ae zan sani ko?."
Dije ta girgiza kai alamar takaici sannan ta ce "wai fah Dije ake cemin, wasu ma har magajiya suke cemin, a birni kuwa ae sai dai kiji ance deejarth Deenah ko kuma ace Nanah ko Nainarh koma hadiza ga sunan nan dai kala kala sai wanda mutum ya zaɓa a birni.


Wata budurwa ce ta ce "ni kuma sunana AYSHA idan a ƙauye ne, me ake kiran mai sunan?... Dije ta kalli budurwar da ta kira kanta da AYSHA gata ƴar gayu kyakyawa da ita, sannan tayi murmushi ta ce "ae Yasin ina faɗamiki Yadda kike da kyau ɗinnan a ƙauye ba ruwan su sai dai kiji ankira ki da a'i ko kuma indo ko esha ko ma shatu, bah wani sunan ki AYSHAN NAN Yasin, AYSHA tayi dariya ta ce "amma za'a iya cemin Humaira ko A`ishatu acan ɗin ko?," Dije ta ce "idan kinci sa'ah ba."

Haka dai Ｄije ta ware da ƴan motar sunata kwasan fira kamar dama can sunsan juna dayake ita haka take bata da wuyar sabo da mutum, kowa ya faɗamata sunan shi saita faɗa yadda ake kiran sunan nashi a ƙauye a haka har suka sauka a tasha, sannan  suka hau napep izuwa gidan da gwaggon nata take aiki.
A napep Dije ta kalla gwaggo ta ce "wai gwaggo na miye sunan unguwan da gidan aikin naki yake ne?,"  gwaggo tayi murmushi, tambayar da Dije ta mata, tuno mata da ƴan gidan da tayi musamman ma Afeefah, cike da fara'ar da ya bayyana saman fuskarta ta ce "MAI KYAU DISTRICT" a taƙaice.
A hankali Dije ta maimaita sunan a ranta a fili ta ce "kilama unguwan zatayi kyau tunda naji sunan da daɗi fa, kai Yasin ma daga ji bama KYAU kaɗai ba, harda kyan gani."
Ita dai gwaggo bata ƙara kulata ba har suka isa unguwan, zare ido kawai Dije takeyi ganin fantsama fantsaman gidajen da suke wucewa sunƙi su ƙare.

Dije ta ce "lah lah lah gwaggo Yasin gidajen masu shegen kyan kyawu ne, Allah kai sai kace fadar aljannar duniya," ta faɗa tana ƙanƙame gwaggon nata kamar za'a ƙwaceta daga hannun gwaggon nata, Humm ae Dije bata ƙara tsurewa ba, har sai da taga ƙaton Jibgegen katafaren gidan da mai napep ɗin ya saukesu a bakin gate ɗin shi, numfashin ta ne ya kusa tsayawa cak saboda tsantsan kyan gidan wai danma dare ne shiyasa amma ae ba ta ga komai ba. A fili ta ce "aljanar duniya kenan wannan ƙato kuma gandameman gidan haka Yasin ni Dije ta ƙauye naga gurin zama🤣.

Ƙwanƙwasa ƙaramar ƙofar gate ɗin gidan gwaggo ta fara, gate man ne ya leƙo ganin gwaggo ne yasa shi ya buɗewa, da sakin fuska da alama sun saba da gwaggon.


Shiga gidan sukayi ita dai Dije sai ƙarema gidan kallo takeyi ga hasken lantarki da expensive lights ya haske ko'ina da ina na gidan, ga kuma shuke shuke gashi gidan ƙato gaske "ae yasin gobe da safe saina fito na ƙarewa gidan kallo, kai ina zan bari kallon daular aljannar duniya ya wuce ni, Dije ta ƙarishe maganar nata, a dai dai lokacin da suka ƙarisa bakin ƙofar palon katafaren gidan, don ita ma kanta ƙofar palon na glassa ne, kuma glass ba wai irin normal glass ba, a'a glass ne irin highly expensive glass ɗinnan wanda ba'a ko'ina ake samun kalar shi ba,sai gurin da naira ta zauna aci asha sannan aka tada kai da ita abar da take juya duniyar wato  ita 𝑁𝐴𝐼𝑅𝐴💸💲💰

Dije a zuciyarta ta ce ita kanta ƙofar ma abin kallo ne harda Wani Glass, ga kuma gurin danna lambobin sirri, oh ni Dije ta ƙauye yau dai ina ganin ikon Allah da abin mamaki a birni,kuma a gidan shugaban ƙasa. Dije ita duk a tunaninta fah gidan president take,don tun lokacin da ta ga waɗannan jibga jibgan jami'an tsaron dake tsaron gidan ta sadaƙar da cewa nan fa gidan shugaban ƙasa ne, gwaggo ta kawota.

Kallon gwaggo dake knocking ɗin ƙofar palon tayi saboda ta gaza riƙe abinda ke ranta ta ce "gwaggo hala wannan gidan shugaban ƙasa ne ko?," dariya tambayar Dijen yaba gwaggo tayi sannan ta ce "a'a me kika gani?,"  Dije ta saki baki da hanci ta ce "lah wai dama nan ba gidan shugaban ƙasa bane, to gidan waye?," gwaggo ta ce "ko kaɗan ba gidan shugaban ƙasa bane,"  Dije ta ce "tabɗijan aikuwa dai...Dije tana shirin yin wata maganar taga alamar ana buɗe ƙofar palon, nutsuwa tayi a zuciyarta kuma cewa tayi bari na natsu saboda aji daɗin sakani a makaranta da wuri.
Wata haɗaɗɗiyar ƴar Gayu ce ta buɗe musu ƙofar, Dije tana shirin gaisheta taga ta juya ta koma bayan ta bisu da kallon banza, baki sake Dije tabi ta da kallo ganin gwaggo na shirin shiga ciki ta barta ne yasa tabi bayan gwaggon da saurinta.

 
Ae Dije baki,hanci, kunne,ido, ba abinda bata saki ba ganin irin haɗuwar palon na fitar sense ƙarewa palon kallo ta shiga yi kamar wata sakarya komai na palon sky purple ne and white color gashi ƙaton gaske 100by100.

Jin murya ana magana ne yasa ta dawo hayyacin ta tana bin ƴan palon da kallo kamar yadda suma suke kallon ta.
Muryar gwaggonta ta tsinkayo tana gaishesu, baki ta kuma saki jin wai gwaggo ne take gaishesu su kuma ba kunya sun amsa a zuciyarta ta ce "bala'i Yasin ba shegiyar da zan gaisar tunda ba'a haifeni ba caɓɗijan, gyara tsayuwar ta tayi tana kuma bin mutanen palon da kallo a zuciyarta ta ce wa'innan suba turawa suba larabawa sannan kuma ba chanistawa bah🤣😂😅. 

Afeefah dake saukowa downstairs ta hango mutane tsaye a palo kamar masu jiran kota kwana ga wasu abubuwa hannunsu kamar tsummokara🤣.
Ita batama gane su ba saida ta kusa saukowa sannan ta gane kosu waye cike farin ciki ta ce "laha'ila Auntie sa'adah kece?," da ɗan saurinta ta ƙarisa saukowa sannan ta ƙariso gurin su tana faɗin "Auntie sa'adah yaushe kika dawo ne?, yana ganki a tsaye ki zauna mana please, kallon Dije tayi wacca ta zuba mata ido kamar zasu zubo ƙasa saboda kallo.
"Lah Auntie sa'adah wannan fah?, ta tambaya bayan su Dije sun zauna a two sieter ita kuma ta zauna a one sieter. murmushi Auntie sa'adah tayi sannan ta ce "wannan ƴar yaya tace nazo da ita zata dinga tayani girki da wasu ayyukan. tayi maganar a gajarce.
Murmushi Afeefah tayi Sannan ta ƙara kallon ƴar kyakyawar baƙauyiyar yarinyar dake kallon ta har yanzu kamar ta sama television, ta ce "barkanki dai please may i know your name?,"

"Sunana die dee deejarth, eh deejarh." Dije ta faɗa cikin inda inda, game da daburcewa na ganin tsantsan kyau irinna wannan kyakyawar halitta dake gabanta.
"Hala dai kina inn inane" Afeefah ta tambaya.
 Da sauri Dije ta ce "a'a banayi 'Balarabiyar india'.
 Murmushi Afeefah tayi ta ce "Wacece kuma 'Balarabiyar indiyar?." Dije ta ce "ke mana ae kece 'Balarabiyar india' don naga kinfi wa'incan kyau, ne sa ba kusa ba Yasin. Ta faɗa tana nuna mutanen palon da suke harkar gabansu kamar basusan da wata hallitar a gurin ba inma sun sani to basu damu dasu bah🙄.

Kallon su duka Afeefah tayi yaran ƙanwar Momy Rafi'at ne Hajiya Sailiba, su biyu fareeda da fadeela twins ne sai kuma Lailah dake kwance a Three sieter da alama karatu take bacci ya ɗauketa sai kuma can dinning table  Nainarh da Nailah ne suke cin dinner ɗinsu.

"To Ke ma miye sunanki" Dije ta tambaya.
"Sunana Afeefah Abubakar Mai ƙyau", "wow nice name" cewar Dije hadda haɗawa da ɗan turancinta da ta kowa a makarantar ƙauyensu. Ta kuma cewa "i'm very pleasure to meet you. 
A wannan karon ba iya Afeefah ba hatta su fadeela, fareeda,su Nainarh da Nailah dake dinning room saida suka juyo suna kallon Dije da tayi maganar dan kuwa basuyi tunanin tanajin turanci ba, tun daga yanayin shigar dake jikin ta, huh to akan mema zasuyi tunanin tana jin turanci bayan kallon irin shigar jikinta,duk wanda ya kalleta lokacin kai tsaye da Villager girl zai kirata.
Dije tayi murmushi dan kuwa da gangar tayi turanci dan ta kankarowa kanta daraja, a zuciyarta ta ce jisu sun ɗauka kowama dusa ne taja qwafa🤓.

Afeefah ta ce "wow you are so cute nd adorable. Nanfa ɗaya Dije ta fara zarar ido tana rabawa dan bataji abinda Afeefah ta faɗa a ƙarshen maganar ta ba, turancin ma da tayi yanzu da yaya ta haɗasu, da kyar ta iya cewa "na gode," amma a ranta ta riƙe kalmar "adorable" ƙam saita bari daga ita sai Afeefah ɗin, zata tambaye ta fasarar kalmar.

Afeefah na fita Dije ta daka tsalle ta haye saman gado a fili ta ce "huh saini Dije ta ƙauye a birni kuwa deejarh nake Yasin.
Tashi tayi tana kuma ƙara bin ɗakin da kallo iya yinta saida tayi iyayinta sannan ta cire  yamu shashshe hijjab ɗin jikinta wanda yaga jiya yaga yau.

Toilet ta shiga jin ana kiran sallan isha'i alwala ta ɗauro as a muslim girl, bata tsaya ƙarewa toilet ɗin kallo ba ta fito, jakar kayanta ta buɗe ta ciro sallaya da hijjab sabo.
Gurin da taga an tanadar domin sallah taje tayi, bayan ta idar da sallan ta cire hijjab ɗin, jakar kayanta ta ɗauka ta fito da kayan ciki duka waje tayo dasu, saboda tsaban iyayi irinna Dije a wardrobe ta jeresu tas bayan tayi musu ninki irinna guga.
Sannan ta tashi toilet ta koma tana kuma bin ko'ina da kallo yayi kyau sosai gaban mirror ta tsaya tana kallon kanta saman mirror ta kalla taga abubuwan wanke kala kala sai wanda mutum ya zaɓa.
Ae ba shiri Dije ta zaro ido ganin abubuwa na ƴan gayu iri iri ba'a magana, kayanta ta cire, ta ɗaura towel ɗin da taga anyi hanging ɗinshi in toilet wall.
Ɗaya daga cikin abin wanke kai ta ɗauka taga an rubuta "shampoo" buɗewa tayi tatso a saman hannunta sannan ta murza zuba saman gashin kanta saida ta gama sawa Sannan ta kunna famfo tasa ruwa ta wanke tas, wani ta kuma ɗauka taga an rubuta "All hair conditioner" ba tunanin komai shima ta wanke kanta dashi bayanta gama wanke sumar kanta da shampoo's ɗinne, tayi wanka ta fito ɗaure da towel fari ga wani a hannunta tana tsane sumar kanta dashi hand Drier ta gani da sauri ta jona a charge sannan ta fara bushar da gashinnata bayan ta kammala ko shafa lotion batayi ba ta je wardrobe ɗin data jere kayanta riga doguwa na material ta ɗauka duk taji duniya ta saka bayan ta gama komai ta haye saman bed sai bacci. {Dije anya kuwa wannan daga ƙauye take?, E mana gani nayi ae ta iya komai daya danganci bathroom sai kace wacca dama can ta saba shiga🤔}.

Ɓangaren Afeefah kuwa tana fita daga ɗakin da ta sauke Dije kai tsaye bedroom ɗinta ta koma, system ɗinta ta ɗauka tana karatun exam dayake sun fara ne.
Tana zaune saiga Lailah ta shigo ita ma hannunta riƙe da littafai irinna su na likitoci, zama tayi tana faɗin "a gaskiya na gaji wannan karatun, wallahi karatun likita ba sauƙi saina Allah....kai ina, ni da ma a soke karatu kwata kwata adena a duniya."
kallon Afeefah tayi dake faman aiki da system da alama ma batasan ta shigo ɗakin ba. "Hey miss feefah, duk karatun ne haka ba'asan ma lokacin da na shigo ba." Lailah tayi maganar tana tafa hannunta a fuskar Afeefah ɗin, ɗagowa Afeefah tayi tana kallon Lailah fuskar ta ɗauke da murmushi ta ce "yane likita karatun exam ɗinne, yayi zafi ko?," Aiyar zuciya Lailah ta sauke ta ce "ana fama wallahi, ni fa ko, wani lokacin sai naji kamar nabar wannan karatun likatancin fah." Waro ido waje Afeefah tayi tana ɗan murmushi ta ce "lah lah Lailah aikuwa wannan maganar sai a kunnen daddy, ko dai duk karatun exam ɗinne ya kawo hakan kike saɓa lamba haka."
 "Hummm kedai ba zaki gane bane kawai Miss feefah sai dai kawai abar zancen anan," cewar Lailah, "haba zan gane mana idan kika ganar dani Afeefah ita ma ta bawa Lailah amsa tana dariya.ɗaukar littafin karatunta Lailah tayi ba tare da ta ce ma  Afeefah ɗin ci kanki ba😂.


**

Sai wajen sha ɗaya na dare, sannan suka kammala karatun nasu wai nanma Lailah ce ta dage bacci takeji shiyasa suka haƙura.

**
Kiran sallah ne da sanyin asuba dake kaɗo curtains ɗin ɗakinne ya tashi Dije daga bacci tashi tayi tana miƙa alamar dai baccin bai isheta ba. Kai tsaye bathroom ta shiga ta ɗauro alwala ta fito ta tada kabbarar sallar asuba bayan ta shimfiɗa sallayar ta da ta zo dashi daga ƙauye.
Tana cikin raka'ar ƙarshe ne aka turo ƙofar ɗakin aka shigo Afeefah ne jikin ta sanye da hijjab fari tas har ƙasa na sallah zama tayi saman bed tana jiran Dije ta idar da sallah.

Juyowa Dije tayi bayan ta idar tana kallon Afeefah ta ce "lah Balarabiyar india kece ashe ina kwana,"  murmushi Afeefah tayi ta ce "lafiya ƙlau deejarh ya kike ya baƙunta kuma fatan kina cikin ƙoshin lafiya.
Washe baki Dije tayi ta ce "ae Balarabiyar india ina faɗamiki babu baƙunta don tuni yabi gado, amma wai Balarabiyar india na tambaye ki mana idan babu damuwa?," cewar Dije.
Afeefah ta ce "ina jinki Allah yasa na sani," washe baki Dije ta kuma yi cike da farin ciki wannan kyakyawar Balarabiyar india tana mata magana ta ce, " ah dama Balarabiyar india wannan ɗakin hala na ƙanwar kice?." Dariya Afeefah tayi ta ce "a'a ko ɗaya bana ƙanwa ta bane, amma me kika gani ne?," Dije ta ce "ae naga akwai komai da komai ne, a ciki hadda man wanke gashi fah na gani kala kala a can wajen ta nuna bathroom.
"Okay ae ɗakin baƙi ne idan suka zo sai suke zama anan, kamar dai ace ƴan mata kamar ki, aikin ga ɗakin irinna ƴan mata ne waɗanda basu wuce 15years ba ko," Dije ta ce "E, mana gashi ɗakin mai kyau kuwa, Balarabiyar india wai Menene ma sunan ki na gaskiya sunan akwai daɗi amma kuma sai wahalar faɗa ko miye ma "AFEEF" ne ko "AFFAF" ta tambaya tana kallon Afeefah ɗin.

Yauwa na tuna Afeefah hakane ko?,"  'E,' Afeefah ta bata amsa sannan ta ce "follow me muje kitchen Auntie sa'adah na kiran ki zamu haɗa breakfast okay,"  tashi Dije tayi tabi bayan Afeefah bayan ta cire hijjab ɗin jikinta ta ɗaura ɗan kwalin rigar dake a jikinta.

Dije sai faman kallon kitchen ɗin takeyi tana kallon kyau da tsaruwa, muryar gwaggon ta taji suna gaisawa da Afeefah da sauri ta kalla gurin da gwaggon nata take ta ce "lah gwaggona kece wai, to ina kwana ni yasin na manta dake mah," gwaggo ta ce "toh dama tayaya zaki tuna dani kina can kin sama a.c kina kwasar bacci harda minshari.......washe baki Dije tayi tun kafin gwaggonnta ta ƙarisa maganar da takeyi Dijen ta rigata da faɗin, "Yasin kuwa gwaggo bakiji ba kamar karna fito daga ɗakin domin wani irin madarar daɗin bacci ɗakin yake dashi.
Dariya Afeefah tayi ta ce "to malama deejarh mai ɗaki, ga wannan juyo in ɗaura miki, kallon abinda Afeefah take miƙo mata Dije tayi ta ce "laha'ila wannan kuma Menene wai ku nan birni idan za'ayi girki sai an saga wannan abunne?."
Afeefah ta ce "E, mana ta yadda bazai ɓata kayan ki ba" Dije ta kuma washe baki ta ce "to Balarabiyar india! au Adda Afeefah miye sunan wannan ɗin shi kuma muji idan akwai daɗi.
 Saida Afeefah ta gama ɗaura mata sannan ta ce "cikin murmushi sunan shi apron.

Dije ta ce "aikuwa daga yau na riƙe sunan shi don fita kunya, apron ko kika ce ae,"
Gyaɗa mata kai Afeefah tayi Sannan ta bata kayan vegetables ta ce "ina fatan dai kin iya yankawa ƙanana ƙanwata?," Dije ta ce "E, mana Balarabiyar india sosai ma, na iya a ƙauye.
"Okay to yanka mu gani tunda kince kin iya," ɗauka Dije Tayi ta fara yankawa iya qwarewarta, Afeefah tana lura da'ita tana mamakin yadda yarinyar ta iya aikin highly, don ita da farko ma tayi tunanin yarinyar ba za ta iya aikin komai ba, sai kuma gashi yanzu yarinyar ta bata mamaki.
Kallon Auntie sa'adah Afeefah tayi Sannan ta ce "Auntie jiya naji kamar kince zaki faɗamin wani abu koh, ɗan juyowa Auntie sa'adah tayi tana kallon Dije sannan ta nuna ma Afeefah, Dije ɗin ta ce "ita wannan sunan khadijatu kuma ɗiyar  YaYa na da muke uwa ɗaya uba ɗaya dashi ne, mahaifiyarta ta rasu tun tana da shekaru biyu a duniya, a lokacin naso YaYa na wato mahaifin ta ya bani ita na ci gaba da rainonta tunda kamar aka sani ne ban taɓa haihuwa ba amma Allah bai nufa ba.

A hannun kishiyar uwanta ta taso, matar ba ƙaramin wahalar da'ita tayi ba, don  da kyarma aka samu ta bari aka sakata makaranta na arabic da na boko, to haka rayuwa ta ci gaba da tafiya Dije tana hannun kishiyar mahaifiyarta har ta kawo wannan lokacin sati ɗaya baya, daya wuce ne wai su isu isu ƴan matan ƙauyen wai suka haɗa gasa wai suna gasar 𝑆𝐴𝑅𝐴𝑈𝑁𝐼𝑌𝐴𝑅 𝐾𝑌𝐴𝑈 sannan tsarin gasar shine, duk wacca tafi kyau to zasu haɗa kuɗin da ƴan kallon gasar suka haɗa, su ba wacca tayi nasara, to ana haka shine fah, ita ma Dijen ƴar ƙaniya da ita, ta shiga kuma taci shine da aka bata kuɗinta a matsayin wacca taci nasarar, ita kuma ba ta ba Ladidi ba, ta yi harkar gabanta dasu, Ladidi wato matar babanta da take riƙonta, shine ita kuma Ladidi taji haushin hakan wanda dalilin kenan da yasa ta kamata ta ɗaure a ɗaki ta mata dukan mutuwa kuma taƙi ta kwanceta abinci ma taƙi bata saida YaYa na wato mahaifinta ya dawo gidan yaga halin da take ciki, a lokacin ranshi ba ƙaramin ɓaci yayi ba, nan dai ya kwanceta ya kawota gurina shine ni kuma na yanke shawarar na kawota nan aikatau saboda na kusa Aure nanda sati ɗaya ne InshaAllah shiyasa jiya kika ganmu da dare, Auntie sa'adah ta ƙarishe maganar tana sauke ajiyar zuciya, duk da cewa yankan kaɗan daga cikin tarihin rayuwar Dije tayi ta ba Afeefah a taƙaice kuma a gajarce don ba duka bane, hasali ma iya labarin da ta ba Afeefah kaɗanne ko na ce kamar talla ne daga cikin labarin rayuwar Dije.

Ita ma Afeefah ajiyar zuciyar ta sauke tana bin Dije da kallon tausayi sannan ta mayar hankalinta kan Auntie sa'adah ta ce "to amma dai hukuma ta hukunta matar ko, dubi yadda duk jikin ta yake a farfashe fa. Tayi maganar tana kuma kallon yadda wasu gurare ajikin Dije suke duk wounds ne.
Hucin takaici Auntie sa'adah ta sauke tana faɗin, "wani hukunci ae ba abinda aka mata hasali ma, shi YaYan nawa da kanshi, ya kasa ko mata kallon banza balle azo maganar hukunci.
Da tsananin mamaki a wannan taƙin Afeefah take kallon Auntie sa'adah ta ce "kamar yaya bayyi mata komai ba kenan kinaso kice min babu abinda aka mata, aka barta ta ci gaba da rayuwa a haka. "E' haka nake nufi" cewar Auntie sa'adah.

A fili Afeefah ta ce "amma wallahi mutumin ƙauye bayyi ba wani lokacin haba dan Allah," Auntie sa'adah tayi murmushi ta ce "gwara dai da kika ce wani lokacin", "sorry for say so, kawai dai abin ne ya bani takaici, Afeefarh ta faɗi hakan ganin yadda yanayin fuskar Auntie sa'adah ɗin ya sauya. Sannan ta ci gaba da cewa.
"Auntie sa'adah amma wallahi, matar nan ta bani haushi sosai, duka duka ita Dijen ɗin nawa take da har zatayi mata abin da tayi," Auntie sa'adah ta ce "ba za ta wuce shekaru goma sha biyar ba, da haihuwarta a duniya," Afeefah taja qwafa tana faɗin, "dama da gani ba za ta haura hakan ba nasani. Oh naji ma kamar kince zakiyi Aure ko?, Yanzu shikenan zaki tafi ki barmu da kewarki?," tayi maganar cike da shagwaɓa da rashin jin daɗi, Auntie sa'adah tayi murmushi tana faɗin "to ba ga Dije ba, ae zata zauna mada dina."
Ita ma Afeefah murmushi tayi ta ce "ae munfi sonki akan ita Dijen ɗin cuz mun daɗe dake highly, don ina ganin kusan six years muke tare dake, ita kuwa kwata kwata jiya tazo, kuma muka fara ganinta.
Da sauri Dije dake yanka onion akan chopping board ta ce "au yanzu, Balarabiyar india kinfi son gwaggo na akaina kenan?," Afeefah ta ce "E, mana nafi sonta akanki sosai ma, kuma sannan  sunan ta ba gwaggo bane, ki dinga kiranta da "Auntie sa'adah" daga yau," Dije ta ce "shikenan naji, kuma nima ta dena cemin Dije sunana Deejarh ko kuma ta ce Nanah, ko kuma dai suna mai daɗi, amma ba irinna ƙauye ba. Afeefah ta ce "Okay, to dai kinji Auntie sa'adah, Deejarh ta ce ki dena kiranta da Dije, sai dai kice Nanah ko Deejarh." Auntie sa'adah ta ce "to to shikenan na dena Dije na," Dije kamar zatayi kuka ta ce "kuma dai kin ƙara faɗar Dijen, Afeefah tana dariya ta ce "to ae ba tashi ɗaya bane zata iya dena faɗa, dole sai da kaɗan kaɗan sannan.
Dije ta ce au bari dai alƙalamina ✍ ya kiyayi rubuta wannan Dijen tunda bataso kar tayimin kuka 😢😂.

Deejarh ta kalla Afeefah ta ce "Balarabiyar india, amma dai zakije ƙauye bikin gwaggona Auntie sa'adah ɗinki ko?," Afeefah tayi murmushi tana faɗin,  idan Allah ya nufa kafin lokacin mun kammala exam a school zani Insha Allah.
Tun kafin Deejarth ta ƙarayin wata maganar Auntie sa'adah ta rigata da faɗin "a'a, ae bama sai kinje ba abin da ba mutane za'a tara ba kawai ɗaurawa ne da tarewa, rana guda, Afeefah ta ce "Okay to Allah ya kaimu musha biki, kuma Allah ubangiji yasa gurin zama ne," Deejarh ta amsa Ameen Ameen Balarabiyar india ta kuma Allah yasa tana shiga wata tara kuma koma suna.
Dukan wasa Auntie sa'adah ɗin ta kaima Deejarh tana faɗin "na shiga tara na ni, Dije yanzu da tsufa na ne zanje ina wani  haihuwa, ae haihuwa yanzu sai yara.
Dariya kawai Afeefah takeyi yayinda take kare Deejarh da ta ɓoya a bayan ta, bakinta bai mutu ba take cewa "ae Yasin gwaggona baki wuce haihuwa ba, kwata kwata shekarunki nawa ne, ae rannan ina jinki lokacin da kike faɗawa Dillaliyar da zaki saya kayan ɗaki gurinta, cewar shekarunki Talatin da tara da watanni ne baki ci ke Arba'in ba, kuma ae ance sai mace takai shekaru arba'in ne ko fiye da haka take dena haihuwa."

"Au kenan ba za kiyi shiru ba ko?," cewar Auntie sa'adah tana hararar Deejarh ɗin.
"A"a Yasin nayi shiru bazan ƙara cewa komai ba, tunda na lura bakya so ina faɗar gaskiyar" ita Deejarh ta ba gwaggon nata amsa tana ci gaba da yanka Albasar hannunta.
Ita dai Afeefah tana jin yadda Deejarh gwaggon nata suke kwasar drama, ita kuma tana kwasar dariya, kwando kwando🤣.

Haka dai suka ci gaba da aikin, suna taɓa fira kuma shan dariyar Dije ta ƙauye da ta rikiɗe ta koma Deejarh a Birni🤓.
Har suka kammala haɗa haɗaɗɗen breakfast  ɗin tare suke jerewa a dinning room har suka kammala jerewa duka sannan  Afeefah ta wuce bedroom ɗin domin yin wanka da Shirin school, ita kuwa Deejarh a faffaɗan plate ta ɗi ba duka kalolin abincin breakfast da suka haɗa, babu kalar da bata ɗi ba  san ranta ba, sannan takai ɗakinta, kana ta dawo ta kuma ɗiban juice ɗin da Afeefah ta haɗa ta koma ɗakinta dashi.



Sai da ta cinye abincin data ɗi bo tas ta kora da ruwa game da sassanyar juice sannan ta tashi ta shiga bathroom wanka tayi ta fito ɗaure da towel, kalolin expensives lotion ɗin da ta gani a saman dress mirror ta shafa abinta ba ruwanta, bayan ta gama shafa lotion ɗin wardrobe ta buɗe inda ta shirya kayanta ta ɗauki wasu riga da zani na atamfa da suka ga jiya suka ga yau, ta saka sannan tasa hula da alama kayan basu da kallabine.
Ɗukar plate ɗin da taci abinci a ciki tayi, ta fito haɗaɗɗen palon gidan da bata gajiya da kallon shi, kitchen ta shiga ta wanke plate ɗin, sannan ta mayar dasu ma'ajinsu ta fito daga kitchen ɗin tana bin palon da kallon ƙauyenci.
Sam bata lura da mutanen dake a dinning table suna cin breakfast ba, don a nata shirmen bedroom ɗin Afeefah take nema, amma batasan ko wannene ba ciki ƙerarrun bedroom's dake zagaye da palon, zata wuce ta koma ɗakinta kenan tunda bata ga ɗakin Afeefah ɗin ba, saidai me?, turus taja tunga ganin mutane a dinning room suna cin abinci, cikin nutsuwa da rashin hayaniya, lura da tayi kamar basu lura da'ita ba yasa tayi sauri zata wuce zuwa ɗakin, karaf taji muryar Afeefah tana kiran sunan ta.



A hankali ta juya ta nufa dinning ɗin, ƙarisawa tayi, gaisar da mutanen gurin tayi, bata jira sun amsa ba, ta kalla Afeefah tana washe baki ta ce "Balarabiyar india gani, naji kamar kina kira na ko?," Murmushi Afeefah tayi ta ce "kinci abincin kin ƙoshi kuwa?," "E, naci sosai plate ɗin ma, na mayar kitchen na wanke tas don nasan gidan ƴan gayu nake, Deejarh ta bata amsar amma hankalin ta yana akan kallon Haleesat da Zaynerb da suke mata mugun kama, bata ganesu ba dan jiya bata gansu ba tadai gane Lailah da su fareeda da fadeela. 

Mister mai kyau, Daddyn su Afeefah ne ya kalla Deejarh kallon nutsuwa da nazari irinnasu, sannan ya ce cikin muryarsa mai sanyi da taushi da kuma girma, "ɗiyata ya sunan ki, kuma daga wani gari kike?," A hankali kamar mai tsoron magana ta ce "sunana 'Nanah khadijatu' amma ana cemin Dije a ƙauye a birni kuma Deejarh😅.

Ɗan murmushin manya Daddy yayi sannan ya kalla Afeefah ya ce "wato dai ita ce kenan?, yayi ma Afeefah tambayar ne, don tuni tayi mishi faffaɗan bayani akan Dije ɗin yadda zai fahimta, kuma ya fahimta ɗin highly.
Afeefah ta ɗaga mishi kai alamar 'E, sannan ta kalla Deejarth tana murmushi ta ce "Malama Deejarh, Let me explain my family to you.
Nuna Nainarh tayi sannan  ta ce "wannan sunan ta Auntie Hassana, amma muna kiranta da sister Nainarh ita ce Babba cikin mu, tanada Aure, amma bata haihu ba tukunna, Deejarh ta ce "Allah ya kawo Yaran masu amfani, suka amsa da Ameen, hadda Nainarh wacca da farko ɗaure fuska tayi, amma tanaji ance Allah ya bata Yara ta saki fuska hadda murmushi, don a duniya bata da wani buri da ya wuce ganin ga ta da yaro ko yarinya wata rana, wannan shine burin Nainarh Abubakar Mai Kyau a Duniya.
Afeefah ta nuna Nailah ta ce wannan kuma sunan ta Auntie Hussaina, ita ma muna kiranta da sister Nailah, twins ne ita da Auntie Nainarh ita ma tanada Aure da yaro ɗaya watan shi bakwai a duniya.
Deejarh tayi murmushi ta ce "ae dama daga ganin su anga ƴan biyu yasin, Afeefah ta kuma yi ma ta nuni da Haleesat ta ci gaba da cewa "ita kuma wannan sunan ta Auntie Haleesat ita ma muna ce mata sis leesat a wani lokacin."  Da sauri Deejarh ta ce "Yasin kuma tana kama ƴar indiyar nan miye ma sunan ta ne kuwa?,  Yauwa na tuna sunan ta 'Krithi Shetty, wannan mai dimples din gata kyawawa, Yasin komai nasu iri guda da na wannan Haleesa ɗin."  Dariya sukayi hadda Haleesat ɗin Afeefah ta ce "haka da yawan mutane suke cewa.
Sannan ta nuna Zaynerb tana murmushi ta ce "ita kuma wannan ƴar kyakyawar da kike gani, ita ce my Lovely Aunty sunanta Auntie Zaynerb sannan rana ɗaya aka haifesu ita da Auntie Haleesat."
Deejarth ta ce " ato kice shiyasa naga suna kama, amma sai dai ita wannan, ta nuna Zaynerb ta ce "tafi kama da meliz ta wannan turkish film ɗin 'Elimi Birakma' wato Rayuwata wannan Turkish actress 'Cemre Baysel' Yanayin gashin kanta mai tsayi da sheƙi iri ɗaya da komai ma, Deejarh tayi maganar ne hango gashin kan Zaynerb da ya leƙo ta Hular dake kan Zaynerb ɗin, da tayi.
Sukayi dariya kafin Afeefah ta kuma cewa wani abu Deejarh ta ce "suma sunada Aure ne?," Afeefah ta zaro ido ta ce "Aure kuma?, a'a basu da, Deejarh da mamaki ya kusa kaita zaune ta ce "cabɗi jan ae wa'innan da'ace a ƙauyen mune da yanzu sun kusa Aurar da Yaran su, ma yasin huh, lallai ƴan birni a gaiaheku.
Dariya Zaynerb tayi ta ce "sai dai kuma nan birni ne ba ƙauyen ku ba.

Afeefah ta ci gaba "wannan kuma ta nuna Lailah ta ce sunan ta Lailah kuma ita ɗin, my sweet sister ta ce, kuma ƙanwata ce don na bata wata biyu,  Lailah tayi murmushi ta ce "amma kuma idan aka ganni aka ganki sai dai ace ni ce Babba ko, tayi maganar tana yima Afeefah ɗin  gwalo.
Deejarh  da bakinta baya shiru ta ce "Yasin Auntie Lailah!.
Lailah ta maka mata harara, Deejarh taja bakinta ta ɗinke da allura😬.
Afeefah tayi dariya ta ce "saikuma wa'innan ta nuna su Fadeela sannan ta nuna Fadeelan  ta ce sunan ta Fadeela ita kuma wannan ta kuma nuna Fareeda ta ce sunanta Fareeda suma twin's ne suma, kuma yaran ƙanwar momyn mune." Deejarh ta ce "to amma ina momyn naku take banganta ba?,"  Da sauri Zaynerb ta ce bata jindaɗi ne shiyasa, tana upstairs, Deejarh ta ce "inane kuma upstairs?," Wannan karon Haleesat ce ta magana ta hanyar cewa, "tana nufin tana bedroom ɗinta acan saman bene," Deejarh ta ce "ohooo, au yanzu na fahimta ae. Afeefah ta ce "saini kuma Shalelen Daddynta da Auntie Zaynerb ɗina, Sunana Afeefah Abubakar Mai Kyau,"  Deejarh ta ce "suna mai daɗi, Afeefah ta ce "ga kuma Daddyn mu, Mister Mai Kyau."
Deejarh ta kalla Daddyn tana murmushi ta ce "ina kwana Daddyn mu, nima ka zama Daddyna.
Lailah ta ce "ba kun gaisa ba ɗazu?,"  Deejarh ta ce "E' mana mun gaisa amma wannan gaisuwar ta musamman ce😍.

Sannan ta kalla Afeefah ta ce "Balarabiyar india, bari na  koma ɗaki, anjima zan fita in ƙara ganin wannan ƙaton ƙerarren gidan, kamar na shugaban ƙasa." Ko kafin Afeefah tayi magana, tuni Haleesat ta riga ta faɗin, "wannan sunan kuma fa?," Deejarh ta ce "wai Balarabiyar india kike magana?,"  Haleesat ta ce "E,Shi fa,"  Deejarh tayi murmushi ta ce "sunan Aunty Afeefah ne, ae nina saka mata, Jiya da dare kuma ya kamata a yanka rago gaskiya."  Lailah ta ce "Yau nake ganin ikon Allah ganin ido na, wai ma dakata, to ae naga babu larabawa a ƙasar india ko?,"  Deejarh ta ce "ae saboda tafi ƴan ƙasar indian kyau ne shiyasa, nake ce mata Balarabiyar india, ko sunan bayyi ba, na canza ya koma Balarabiyar America ko London?," "Aikuwa dai sunan  yayi mata kyau obaa highly, inaga a barshi a Balarabiyar India ɗin kawai zaifi," cewar Zaynerb, tana ƙumshe dariya.
Deejarh ta ce "kunji ƴar gari, Lallai Auntie Zaynerb zamu je ƙasar India dake," Nainarh ce da surutun Deejarh ya fara isarta ta ce, " wannan kyakyawar baƙauyiyar yarinyar akwai surutu kamar xixi, baki kamar radio cicihhh ceee basa gajiya,  Deejarh ta ce "laha'ila wacece xixi?, hala ita ƴar nan gidan ne, Nailah ta ce "E, mana, ƙanwar mu ce ae," "to tana ina?," Deejarh ta tambaya, Afeefah ta bata amsa da faɗin, "tana karatu a outside," "oh oh inane kuma outside?," Deejarh ta kuma tambaya tana qwalalo ido. Zaune take a bakin swimming pool, yayinda kyawawan ƙafafunta duka biyun suke cikin ruwa, tana lilawa a hankali da alama yanayin da ake ciki yana mata daɗi, kamar ko yaushe sanye take cikin ƙananan kaya Riga top white color, mai zanen stairs c.green sai wando shima crazy jeans c.green, sai hular vani ita ma white color saman hular anyi rubutu da zare c.green an rubuta  '𝐺𝑙𝑎𝑚' da ƙananan baƙi, wayarta ne a hannunta na dama tana tsalawa.

Can gefe kuwa Bilki sai faman safa da marwa takeyi takai mari takai goho, doguwar rigar atamfa ce a jikinta mai dogon hannu gaban rigar kuwa anyi ado da flowers masu kyau wa'inda suka ƙarawa ɗinkin kyau, sai kuma ɗan kwalin rigar datayi ɗaurin ture kaga tsiya.

Da ta gaji da safa da marwan saita ƙarisa gurin da Jieddarh, take ita ma ta sama guri ta zauna sannan ta kalla Jieddarh ta fara magana a hankali kuma cikin damuwa.

cewar Jieddarh dan ta lura idan ba tana dakatar da Bilkin a wasu abubuwan ba, tofah ita Bilkin ita ce zata sha wuya ba kowa ba.
"kedai malama kawai ki faɗamin abin dana tambayeki kinji,"  Bilki ta faɗa ko'a jikinta da abin da Jieddarh ta ce ma ta.



"inaga kawai kice mishi welcome yayi," 

"haba Jieddarh wani 'welcome' kuma?, ai welcome ya zama tsohon yayi gaskiya."
Cewar Bilki tana kyaɓe fuska.
Jieddarh ta ce "toni kinga dai ba soyayya nake ba, balle nasan me zance miki ko,"  Bilki ta ce "amma kuma, ai kinyi karatu kinfi ni nida na tsaya iyaka secondary school,"
Jieddarh tayi dariya tana faɗin "ai duk kanwar ja ce, don nima dai iya kana secondary school ɗin."
Waro ido Bilki tayi ta ce "haba yanzu kamar ke haka kin kammala secondary shine  saboda jin daɗi, kika tsaya a haka baki ɗaura ba,  ni ina mamakin abin da ya hanaki shiga University, gaskiya da walaki,"
Jiiddarh ta ce "bana ra'ayine kawai, that's why, ba wani abu ba," 
Bilki tana ya musta fuska alamar ita bata yarda da abin da Jieddarh ɗin ta ce ba, ta ce.
"haka kawai saboda iya shege kice bakya ra'ayi kawai dai da wata a ƙasa ki yarda da wannan madam,"

Zata kuma magana taji durar motar Heesham zumbur ta miƙe tsaye, ɗaukar gyalenta tayi ta nufi cikin gidan babu magana.

Girgiza kai Jieddarh tayi kurum a fili ta ce "gaskiya ni bansan wani kalan soyayya Bilki take ma Heesham ba, sai kace aikin sihiri?."


Ita kuwa Bilki da sauri ta ƙarisa parking lot a daidai lokacin da Heesham ya fito daga motar hannun shi riƙe da briefcase, har zai wuce ya jiyo muryar ta tana faɗin.

"yaya Heesham,"  juyuwa ya ɗanyi, aikuwa sukayi four eyes da Bilki zuba mata kyakyawan idanun shi yayi yana jira yaji mai zatace, gabaɗaya sai taji ta ruɗe ta duburburce da kyar ta iya haɗo kalmomi ta haɗasu guri guda.

Tana ɗan inda inda ta ce "amm uhm yaya Heesham 'welcome back home' ya gajiya?, umm dama nace kawo jakar naka na riƙe maka kaji?," da kyar ta iya haɗa kalmomin ta faɗa saboda yadda ya zuba mata ido ba ko kyaftawa.

Baice komai ba ya miƙa ma ta briefcase ɗin, hannun ta na rawa tasa ta karɓa, shikuma yayi gaba tabi bayan shi hadda ɗan saurinta.


Gabaɗaya Bilki ta manta da babin wata Ni'ima da irin laifin da ta mata har, saida suka shiga palon sukayi ido huɗu da ita, zaune saman 2sieter ta ɗaura ƙafa ɗaya kan ɗaya, tana taunar cingum
Hango shigowar  Heesham ne yasa ta wani taso da saurinta zata rungumeshi.


Da sauri ya matsa gefe yana kwaɗa mata harara sannan ya fara magana  kai daga jin maganar kasan ranshi ya fara ɓaci da irin halinta.

"Ni'ima meyasa bakyajin magana ne, dubi shiga ki fa, ya miki kala da shigar yaran musulmai?, kuma na faɗa miki ba sau ɗaya ba, akan, ki dena ƙoƙarin hugging ɗina, cuz keba muharrama ta bane, amma ba kyaji ko, so this is the last warning da zan miki akan wannan ugly ɗabi'ar taki.

Cike da shakwaɓa Ni'ima ke faɗin, "haba my world, mana yanzu miye laifin wannan shigar tawa?, sai kace baka waye ba, come on mana, ka shigo zamani mana,"
Ta faɗa tana kuma yin juyi a gaban shi. cike da takaici ya kalle ta.
Ya juyo ya kalla Bilki da tayi tsuru tsuru, yace "duba nan, dubeta, a tsakanin ita dake wacece tayi shigar rashin da'a fisabilillah faɗamin?,"  kallon Bilkin ita ma Ni'imar tayi taga shigar jikinta ya mutsa fuska tayi tana faɗin, "haba my world dubeta fa, wannan ai shigar ƙauyawa tayi.

Da sauri Bilki ta kalla Ni'ima jin abin da ta ce lallai ta latsa ta, ita Ni'imar sanye take cikin €nglish gown black color, mai hannun vest ko gwiwar ta rigar bata kaiba gashi ta ɗameta ɗam numfashi ma, da kyar takeyi.


Bai tsaya jin abin da zata kuma cewa yayi gaba abin shi, da sauri Bilki tabi bayanshi, suka bar Ni'ima a tsaye kamar gunki.
Suna shiga bedroom ɗinnashi dake upstairs ta aje mishi briefcase ɗin a palo ta dawo down ta sauko bataga Ni'ima ba dan haka batama damu ba garden ta koma gurin Jieddarh zuciyarta qhal💦






D̬̂â̬ĝ̬â̬ Â̬l̬̂k̬̂â̬l̬̂â̬m̬̂î̬n̬̂

*🌼ƚԋҽ ɳαιɳαɾԋ ƙԃ🌼*












🅰🅱🆄🅹🅰

Yau juma'a wanda yayi daidai da kwanakin Dije {Deejarh} biyar da zuwa gidansu Afeefah kuma yaune gwaggon ta wato Auntie sa'adah zata koma ƙauye, Zuwa yanzu Deejarh ta fara fahimtar kaɗan daga cikin halayen mutanen gidan, farko dai kamar:

'𝑵𝒂𝒊𝒏𝒂𝒓𝒉' ta fahimci mace ce ita, mai masifar rashin son shiga jama'a bawai kuma miskila bace, a'a ita haka halinta yake, sannan kuma ta fahimci tana masifar ƙaunar ƴan uwanta sannan kuma tana da kirki sai dai bata nuna kirkinnata kamar   AFEEFAH ita NAINARH nunawa take kamar bata da kirki, amma idan ka zauna da ita zaka fahimci ko wacece ita.

'𝑵𝒂𝒊𝒍𝒂𝒉' ta fahimci itaɗin mace ce ƴar bin ra'ayin kanta kamar mahaifiyarsu MOMY RAFI'AT sannan kuma kirkinta ragagge ne bisa kowa na gidan.

'𝒁𝒂𝒚𝒏𝒆𝒓𝒃' ita kuma Deejarh ta fahimci ZAYNERB mace ƴar komai saina shiga ma'ana ita dai ZAYNERB akwaita da neman rigima amma ga ƴan uwanta kuma rigima irin wacca za'ayi wasa da dariya.


'𝑯𝒂𝒍𝒆𝒆𝒔𝒂𝒕' cabɗi sarkin miskilanci kenan yadda Deejarh ta mata laƙani, don ta fahimci a rana guda ma za'a iya ƙirga adadin magana da HALEESAT tayi, sannan ta lura Haleesat mayyar Bacci ce, sannan kuma na lura Haleesat tana tafiya Club, sai dai bata yi gigin faɗawa kowa ba, sakamakon dalilai.

'𝑳𝒂𝒊𝒍𝒂𝒉' ta fahimci ita kuma ƴar Ji_ji da kai ce, ga kuma mugun iyayi, da alfahari da dukiyar iyayenta, sai dai ita ma tanada kirki iya nata, sannan kuma ta iya yaɓa magana babu taunawa ko wata kala ce kuwa.

'𝑨𝒇𝒆𝒆𝒇𝒂𝒓𝒉' uwar gayya kenan, a gaskiya Deejarh ta kasance tanajin daɗin zama da AFEEFAH fiye da kowa a cikin gidan MISTER MAI KYAU har shi kanshi dama matar sa MOMY RAFI'AT
Deejarh ta fahimci Afeefah mace ce ita mai masifar kirki highly domin kuwa ba ruwanta da kowa bata faɗa da kowa, haka zalika kowa nata ne ba ruwanta, sannan bata ɗaurawa kanta Ji_ji da kai ba sam, sosai akwai bambanci mai yawa tsakaninta da ƴan uwanta especially ma LAILAH, sam Afeefah bata wulaƙanta kowa akan dukiyar da mahaifinta yake dashi. ko kuma wai irin ta nuna bambanci tsakaninta da ƴan uwanta akan ƙaunar da mahaifinsu MISTER MAI KYAU yake nuna ma ta sama da kowa sam bata haka, sannan tun zuwan Deejarh gidan bata taɓaji Afeefah tayima wani ko wata daga cikin ƴan aikin gidan tsawa ko masifa ba. ba kamar Lailah ba da a gaban Daddynsu ta mara ƴar aiki akan 'As depleted ta zubo mata tea a jiki ba tare da ta sani ba.

Afeefah tana da kirki fiye da tunanin mai tunani, sannan Babban mafarkinta a duniya shi ne zama SARAUNIYAR KYAU ta duniya gabaɗaya, Burinta kuma shine zama Babbar Likita da ƙasarta zatayi alfahari da ita, Fatan ta kuma shi ne zama House Wife ta gari💃



________________________________________________________________________________




Tun safe ake abu ɗaya da Deejarh, ta maƙale akan sai tabi gwaggon ta ƙauye ita kuma gwaggon ta ta ce, ba za ta koma da ita ba, yanzu ma haka abin da ake ciki kenan da ita.

Tsaye suke a parking lot Afeefah, Deejarh, Auntie sa'adah sai kuma Zaynerb da Lailah dake gefe suna kallon ikon Allah.

"Deejarh ki bari ta shiga mota, mana driver yaja su tafi, kinga lokaci yana ƙurewa fa, ko kinaso tayi dare a hanya ne?,"
Duk wannan maganar Afeefah ne takeyi ma, Deejarh a hankali kuma cikin rarrashi.
Deejarh tana sha re hawaye ta ce "to zata dinga zuwa dubani, a koda yaushe, da yaushe 😳.
Da sauri Auntie sa'adah ta ce "E, mana Dije na, ai duk wata zandinga zuwa duba ki ba fashi,"
"toki rantse min," cewar Deejarh iya gaskiyar ta.
Dariya Afeefah tayi ta ce "ai ba seta rantse ba, idan ma bata zoba nida kaina zan kaiki ƙauyen, ki ganta ko," da kyar dai akasamu Deejarh ta bari Auntie sa'adah ta shiga motar driver yaja, suna ɗagama juna hannu har suka fice daga gidan.




Su kuma suka koma ciki suna shiga Deejarh ta ɗare saman 3sieter kamar ba ita bace ta gama kukan rabuwa yanzu, zama Afeefah tayi a gefan Deejarh tana faɗin "dubeki kamar ba kece mai kuka yanzu ba,"
Dariya Deejarh tayi ta ce "to ai ni haka nake, yanzu zakiga ina kuka anjima kuma dariya."

Zaynerb ta kalla Afeefah lokacin da ta zauna a kujerar kusada ita ta ce.
"ni banga kina wani shirye shiryen gasa bane fa, kallon Zaynerb Afeefah tayi ta ce "shirya shirye kuma sai kace wacca zatayi aure?,"
Lailah tana shafa wayarta ta ce "bawai haka ba, amma ai ya kamata ko ɗan gyaran jiki kiyi ko?."


Baki Afeefah ta saki tana kallon Lailah sai kuma tayi dariya, "lallai Lailah bakiji da kyau ba, kawai saboda wahala sai naje ina wani gyaran jiki saboda gasa, a'a to bada ni ba,"
 Lailah ta ce "Wallahi da gaske nake fa,"

 Afeefah ta ce "nima ai da gaske nake,"

"nifa yau banga wulƙawan Auntie Nailah ba kwata kwata, Zaynerb tayi maganar.

Deejarh ta ce "ai lokacin da kika tafi makaranta ne suka fita ita da wannan ƙawan nata mai kama da jarumar india 'Pooja Hegde' ɗinnan, ni banmasan sunanta ba,"

"amma wallahi baki da kirki sadiqqa ce take kama da Pooja ɗin?," cewar Zaynerb tana dariya.

Dariya ita ma Deejarh tayi ta ce "Yasin Auntie wannan ƙawar Auntie Nailah ɗin, da pooja take kama har tsayin da hasken ɗaya, yanayin  gashinta ne, kawai bansani ba."

"ke kuma a'ina kikasan tsayin pooja ɗin?, uwar ƴan sanin kamanni?,"
Lailah ta tambaya, Deejarh ta ce "a cikin film mana,"
"wanne a ciki?,"  Lailah ta kuma tambayarta.

Ta ce "wannan wanda tayi ita da allu arjun ɗin mana na dai manta sunan shi ne."
"wai 'Alavaikunthapurramuloo' kike nufi?,"  Afeefah ta tambaye ta bayan ta faɗa sunan film ɗin, da sauri Deejarh ta ce "E, shi nake nufi.

Zaynerb ta ce "ai tafi jaruman india na yankin telugu kyau gabaɗaya."
Lailah tace "a'a wasa dai kikeyi ko amma me za'ayi da wata 'pooja' bayan gasu 'kajal aggarwal' a gefe,"

Zaynerb ta ce "wake maganar kajal, ai wannan ta tsufa,"
" a gurin ki ba, cewar Lailah, Afeefah ta ce "to Ladies a'ina kuka bar Rashi Khanna ɗin, gasu Tamannah Bhatia, Mehreen dasu Samantha duka a gefe,"
"faɗa ma ta dai sister," cewar Lailah ta ci gaba da cewa "ga kuma Shruti Hassan madam ina faɗamiki idanun nata kamar kamar me kyau da kyau aka haɗa fa."

Deejarh ganin suna magana suna haɗawa da turancine, gashi ita ba komai take fahimta ba, a zuciyarta ta ce "bari dai nima nayi turanci nan na haɗa da indian ci😎


Gyaran murya tayi sannan ta kalla Lailah dake kwance a  2sieter ta ce "Auntie Lailah na tambaye ki wani mana?,"
"inajinki da sauraronki cewar Lailah.
Deejarh ta ce "karfa kice bakyajin yaren da zan miki maganar dashi,"
Lailah ta ce faɗa mana baki da problem, to banda abinki Dije ta ƙauye yaruka goma nake ji fa, kuma duka na qware akan jinsu highly.

Deejarth ta kuma gyara zaman ta alamar za'a fara sannan ta ce.
"Menene is poonakaalu ahora?,"

Tashi zaune Lailah tayi tana faɗin, "ban fahimci me kike nufi ba, please ɗan maimaita muji, maimai tawa Deejarh tayi.

"Menene is poonakaalu ahora."

A hankali Lailah ta kuma maimai tawa ita ma, girgiza kanta tayi, "ke bansani ba, wannan ai hauka kawai kika haɗa."

"Ba hauka bane fa, tambaya tayi miki,"
Cewar Zaynerb kallon Deejarh Zaynerb tayi murmushi ta ma ta sannan ta ce.

"na farko dai da ta ce 'Menene' hausa tayi, na biyu kuma da ta ce 'is' turanci tayi, na uku kuma da ta ce 'poonakaalu' yaren telugu tayi, na huɗu kuma da ta  ce 'ahora' yaren spanish tayi."


"Nifa wannan dogon bayanin, ba ganeshi nakeyi ba jeki gundarin magana kawai kai tsaye," cewar Lailah.

Da sauri Afeefah ta ce "tana nufin ta ce 'ƙarfe nawa ne' fa, shine ta haɗa yare kala huɗu ko ba, haka bane?,"  tayi maganar tana kallon Deejarh ɗin, dariya tayi ta ce "E, mana Balarabiyar india haka nake nufi."

Fashewa da dariya Lailah tayi har tana faɗowa daga saman sofa.
Duk suka zuba mata ido, ita ma Zaynerb dariyar takeyi ba laifi.
Saida Lailah tayi dariya ma'ishi sannan ta dakata. Kallon Deejarh tayi cikin dariyar take faɗin, "amma fa, kinyi ƙoƙari waima, ke a'ina kika koya, wannan yarukan kala kala haka?,"


Deejarh ta ce "nima kawai naji na iya ne fa," dariya Lailah ta kuma fashewa da shi.


Afeefah da abin ya isheta buɗar bakinta cewa tayi, "Lailah kodai kin sama taɓuwa ne?,"  

Lailah ta ce "lafiyata qalau, kawai dai na manta rabon da, a sakani dariya kalar haka ne," 
"ai saikiyi da kyau an baki aiki cewar Afeefah.

"Lailah zoki rakani gidan Sunainah mana?," cewar Zaynerb tana miƙewa tsaye.
Tsayawa Lailah tayi da dariyar da takeyi ta kalleta "amma dai wasa kike ko?,
Zaynerb tana ƙoƙarin shiga bedroom ɗinta, ta juyo ta ce "da gaske nake, don akwai abin da nakeso ta koyamin, kinsan course ɗin da nake karanta shi ta karanta ko?,"
 
Tana faɗan haka ta shige ɗakinta, bata daɗe ba, ta fito sanye da hijjab blue black har ƙasa ga folder's rungume a chest ɗinta, kallon Lailah tayi ta ce
"zo mu tafi mana."
"Inason zuwa, amma wallahi ina tsoron karen gidanta,"  Lailah ta faɗa iya gaskiyar ta.
"ke matsoraciya, indai saboda kare ne, ba zaki ba to kisa ranki a inuwa domin karen yana ɗaure ma kaca."
"ke tayaya kikasani?,"
Lailah ta tambaya, Zaynerb ta ce "ita ta faɗamin, kinsan kuma ba zatamin ƙarya ba ko,"
Tashi Lailah tayi ta ɗauka gyalenta dake a gefen kujera ta yafa a kafaɗarta.

"Nima zan biku kinji Auntie Zaynerb, tunda na shigo gidannan, ban fita ba,"
Cewar Deejarh iya gaskiyarta, "idan bakyajin tsoron kare, kizo muje,"  cewar Lailah tana dariya, "banajin tsoron shi," 
"to zo muje," Zaynerb ta amsa ma ta.
Da sauri ta miƙe tsaye ɗakin da yake matsayin nata, ta shiga ta ɗauko hijjab ɗinta iya gwiwa fari.

"ke yanzu nanda maƙwabtan ne, saikin saka wani hijjab," cewar Lailah tana kallon Deejarh da koɗaɗɗen hijjabinta. Zaynerb ta kalla Deejarh ta ce "rabu da Lailah kawai don ta iya tonon tsiwa kala kala, ɗunguma sukayi wajen fita daga palon su ukun.



Suna taɓa fira a hanyar su har suka ƙarisa gidan, ƙwanƙwasa ƙaramar kyauren gate ɗin gidan, Zaynerb tayi shiru taji hannu tasa ta tura ƙofar tajita a buɗe.
Shiga gidan sukayi basuga mai gadi a kujerar shi, ta zama ba, basu kawo komai ba suka nufi cikin gidan a tsakiyar palo suka tsaya suna kwaɗa sallama, amma ba amsa.

"Kai kodai bata nan ne?," Deejarh ta tambaya, Zaynerb ta ce wata ƙila, wata ƙila kuma.....zata kuma magana saiga Babah Dije mai aikin ta fito, "yauwa Babah wai Ummyn Farhana tana nan kuwa?,"
Zaynerb ta tambaya, Babah Dije ta ce "aikuwa Ƴar nan bata nan ta fita ita da mai gidan, wato Alhaji Sohailu."
"to bata faɗa miki inda zata ba?," wannan karon Lailah tayi tambayar.
Babah Dije ta ce "eh inaga gidan surukanta suka tafi," "okay ba damuwa, idan ta dawo kawai kice mata munzo bamu sameta ba, Zaynerb ta faɗawa Babah ɗin hakan.
Da 'to' Babah Dije ta amsa musu, su kuma suka fito.

Lailah ta ce "danasani ma, ban biyoki ba, tunda dai batanan....Zaynerb ta ce "ai kin motsa jiki koba komai inagaa!....kasa ƙarisa maganar tayi ganin abin da ke gabansu, "kinaga mene?," 
Lailah ta tambaya jin Zaynerb ɗin ta kasa ƙarisa maganar bakinta.
A hankali takai kallon ta inda taga 𝑍aynerb ɗin ta saki baki da hanci tana kallo.

💅ᴛʜᴇ ɢʟᴀᴍ ǫᴜᴇᴇɴ, ᴡᴀɴɴᴀɴ ʟᴀʙᴀʀɪɴ ᴍᴀʟʟᴀᴋɪɴᴀ ɴᴇ, ɴɪ ᴋᴀᴅᴀɪ ɴᴀᴋᴇ ᴅᴀ ᴅᴀᴍᴀʀ sᴀʀʀᴀғᴀ ᴀʙɪɴᴀ ʏᴀᴅᴅᴀ ɴᴀᴋᴇsᴏ, ᴡᴀɴɴᴀɴ ʟᴀʙᴀʀɪɴ ɴɪ ɴᴀʏɪ ᴋᴜᴍᴀ ɴᴀsʜᴀ ɢᴡᴀɢᴡᴀʀᴍᴀʏᴀ ᴡᴀᴊᴇɴ ᴋɪʀᴋɪʀᴀɴsʜɪ, ᴡᴀɴɴᴀɴ ʟᴀʙᴀʀɪɴ ᴋɪʀᴋɪʀᴀɴsʜɪ ɴᴀʏɪ, ᴅᴏᴍɪɴ ɪʟᴍᴀɴᴛᴀʀᴡᴀ ᴅᴀ ғᴀᴅᴀᴋᴀʀᴡᴀ ʜᴀʀᴍᴀ ᴅᴀ ɴɪsʜᴀᴅᴀɴᴛᴀʀᴡᴀ, ɴɪ ɴᴇ ɴᴀsᴀɴ ɪʀɪɴ ᴡᴀʜᴀʟᴀɴ ᴅᴀ ɴᴀsʜᴀ ᴋᴀғɪɴ sᴀᴍᴀʀ ᴅᴀsʜɪ, sᴏ ɪɴᴀ ᴍᴀɪ ɢᴀʀɢᴀᴅɪ ᴅᴀ ᴋᴀᴋᴋᴀᴜsᴀʀ ᴍᴜʀʏᴀ ᴀᴋᴀɴ, ʙᴀɴ ʏᴀʀᴅᴀ ʙᴀ, ᴋᴜᴍᴀ ʙᴀᴢᴀɴ ʟᴀᴍᴜɴᴄᴇ ᴡᴀɴɪ ᴋᴏ ᴡᴀᴛᴀ sᴜʏɪᴍɪɴ, ᴀᴍғᴀɴɪ ᴅᴀ ᴡᴀɴɪ sᴀsʜᴇ ᴋᴏ ʙᴀɴɢᴀʀᴇ ɴᴀ ᴄɪᴋɪɴ ᴡᴀɴɴᴀɴ ᴋɪʀᴋɪʀᴀʀʀᴇɴ ʟᴀʙᴀʀɪ '𝐒𝐀𝐑𝐀𝐔𝐍𝐈𝐘𝐀𝐑 𝐊𝐘𝐀𝐔' ʙᴀ, ɪᴅᴀɴ ᴋᴜɴɴᴇ ʏᴀᴄɪ ᴍᴀғɪʏᴀ ʏᴀᴡᴀɴ ᴍᴀsᴜ ɪʏᴀ ᴍᴀɢᴀɴᴀ, sᴜᴋᴀ ᴄᴇ ɢᴀɴɢᴀɢᴀʀ ᴊɪᴋɪ ʏᴀ ᴛsɪʀᴀ, 👑ᴛʜᴇ ɢʟᴀᴍ ǫᴜᴇᴇɴ💅 ᴍᴀʟʟᴀᴋɪ ɴᴀ ɴᴇ, ɴɪ ᴋᴀ𝙳ᴀɪ🤙~*

*page 23&24*



Waro dara daran idanun ta waje Lailah tayi ganin abin da ke gabansu, wani ƙaton kare ne, irinna turawa wanda yasha training ya gaji sai faman hura fala falan kunen shi yakeyi ga mai kula dashi a gefe hannun shi riƙe igiyar da ya ɗaure ƙafan karen ɗaya.


Ita Lailah sam bata lura da ƙafan karen dake a ɗaure da sarƙa ba, gabaɗaya a mugun tsorace take ga wani guntun fitsari da ya matseta a mara.

Tattare skirt ɗinta ta farayi, ganin zata ruga ana kare yasa Iliya dake riƙe da karen saurin faɗin.
"hajiya karki gudu karki gudu fa, a ɗaure yake da sarƙa," Amma ina Lailah tayi nisa bata jin kira a three sixty 360 ta ranci na kare.


Shikuma kare dayaga haka ya rama mata baya, Toh abin yazoma Iliya a bazata ne, dan haka baima san lokacin daya saki igiyar hannun shi ba, shikuwa kare yace dama haka nakeso.


Hannu biyu Iliya ya ɗaura aka yana mai faɗin " yau ina ganin bala'i ganin ido na, dan Allah madam ki tsaya ki dena gudu, amma ina wanda yayi nisa bayajin kira.

Jama'a ba saiga Lailah da kare ana zuba tsere a compound ɗin GIDANMU ba, gabaɗaya Lailah ta rikice batama san inda take jefa ƙafafunta ba, ga kare da shegen gudu duk inda ta cire ƙafa nan yake jefa nashi.

Ganin Lailah tasha kwana shima karen ya mara mata bayane yasa su Zaynerb da Deejarh dake faman hawaye da ma Iliya gabaɗaya suma suka mara musu baya.

Lailah idonta ya rufe kawai gudu take ba ƙaƙƙautawa, fitsarin da take riƙewa batasan lokacin da ta kusa saki ba.
Tazo wuce swimming pool santsin gurin ya kwasheta, saiga Lailah saman ruwa kace kace, kare dayaga haka sai juyo zai koma inda ya fito a daidai lokacin kuma Deejarh dasu Zaynerb sukayo kwanar kawai sai aka tsaya kallan kallo a tsakanin Deejarh da kare yayinda Zaynerb da Iliya suke a daga baya ko  wannen su ido a zazzare tsaban tashin hankali.

Deejarh kuwa yi tayi kamar zata ruga da gudu, sai kuma taja wawiyar kwana ta zube a ƙasa tayi pretend kamar irin ta suma ɗinnan, shikuma karen daidai lokacin ya taho jama'a saiga kare ya tsallake Deejarh ya wuce, wata wawiyar kuma doguwar ajiyar zuciya Deejarh ta sauke a lokacin da karen ya tsallake ta.

Da sauri Iliya ya lallaɓa a hankali cikin sanɗa da kuma dabara wajen da karen yake dayake ya bashi baya ne, da sauri ya riƙo igiyar da ya ɗaure ƙafar karen.

Gabaɗayan su suka sassauke ajiyar zuciya hadda Lailah dake cikin ruwa tsundum har yanzu, da sauri Zaynerb ta ƙarisa bakin swimming pool ɗin ta riƙo hannun Lailah suka samu ta fito Lailah tana riƙe qugunƙumi  ita ma Deejarh tashi tayi daga kwancen da take.

Lailah ko kula Iliya dayake mata sannu batayi ba tsaban takaici, saima kallon ka cuceni da ta jefa mishi tayi gaba abinta, su Zaynerb suka mara maba baya.


A hanya tafe suke bamai magana sun zama kuramen ƙarfi da yaji, sai ajiyar zuciya da kowanen su yake saukewa, wani wawan shura Lailah takai ma ƙaramar ƙofar gate ɗin gidansu,  ba shiri gate man yazo ya buɗe don ganin wani mahaluki ne, wata wawiyar dariya ce ta tahoma Isah mai gadi ganin fuskar Lailah da tayi kamar wacca aka qwato daga bakin kura.

Aikuwa bai iyayin juriyar riƙe dariyar ba, dan saida yayi abinshi.
Wata uwar harara Lailah ta galla mishi cike da balbalin bala'i ta ce "wannan dariyar da kamin to, a bakin aikin ka kayita😨

Tana faɗa mishi haka tabi bayan su Zaynerb da sukayi gaba kamar zata tashi sama saboda sauri.

A tare duka su ukun suka kaima ƙofar palon duka su uku ae ƙofar ba shiri ta buɗe suka shiga ciki.

Afeefah, Nainarh da Nailah da suke kallon wani Chinese Drama 'Intrepreneurial age.'

A tare suka juyo dan ganin mahalukin da ya turo ƙofa kamar zai ɓalla haka, sunayin ido huɗu dasu Lailah a tamanin Auntie Nailah tayi jifa da jaririn dake hannunta zata gudu ganin fuskar Lailah da tayi tunaninta gamo tayi da ZOMBIE ko HORROR ko kuma FATALWA 👽, Allah ne kawai, ya tsare Nainarh tayi saurin cafe yaron cike da tsoro.

Su Zaynerb basu kula Afeefah da Nailah da suke rige rigen haye staircase's don neman gurin ɓuya ba, suka sama guri su duka suka zazzauna a saman 3sieter.

"Zaynerb lafiyar ku kuwa, kuka shigo ko sallama babu, sai kace ku ɗin  duka ba yaran musulmai ba," cewar Auntie Nainarh tana zama a 2 sieter babu alamar tsoro a tare da ita, don bata ma kula da fuskar Lailah ba, kallonta ta mayar kan Deejarh sannan cike da mamaki ta mayar kan Lailah zumbur ta miƙe tsaye, tanabin Lailah da kallon tsoro da mamaki.

"Lailah kece kuwa, miye haka ya sameki?, kodai faɗa kikayi da kura ne?,"
Nainarh ta jerowa Lailah tambayoyin ko tsayawa batayi ba saida takai aya.


A hankali Afeefah da Nailah suka fara dawowa da baya jin abin da Nainarh take faɗa.
Da sauri Afeefah ta ƙariso tana kallon Lailah "to kodai faɗan taje tayi da kuran bamu sani ba," cewar Afeefah da ta tsaya a gaban Lailah tana ƙare mata kallo cike da tsoro game da mamaki.

Buɗe baki Deejarh tayi zatayi magana sai kawai ta fashe da dariya tunowan da tayi lokacin da Lailah take tafka TSEREN MUTUWA tsakanin ta da kare.

"ah ah menene haka kuma, qalau kuwa kike ke Deejarh?,"
Nailah tayi tambayar tana kallon Deejarh ganin yadda take dariyar na ta na hauka tsakanin ta da Allah, "rabu da wannan mental ɗin kawai, ke Zaynerb menene ya faru ne wai na ganku haka 'So Bleak ne'," cewar Nainarh.

Zaynerb ta buɗe baki kenan da zummar zata musu bayani, ita ma ta tuno abin da ya faru ba shiri, ta fara ƙyaƙyata dariya harda riƙe ciki, ganin haka yasa Lailah dake harare harare ita ma ta fashe da dariyar.

Gabaɗaya su ukun suka haɗu suna ƙyaƙyata dariyar su sai yanzu ma abin da ya faru yake tariyo kanshi a kwakwalwar ko wannensu.

Uban tagumi Afeefah ta rafka hannu biyu biyu tana kallon ikon Allah, idan ta kalla Deejarh sai ta kalla Lailah idanta kalleta saikuma ta mayar da akalan kallon nata kan Zaynerb, dayake akwai kaifin kwakwalwa da basira, kafin ta ankara tuni wani tunani yazo kanta, kawai ita ma saita fashe da dariyar.

Nainarh ta ce "wa'innan yaran fa, sun sama mental illness  nake gani,"
"ae ki bari kawai suyi mai isansu, idan suka gaji su da kansu zash dena," cewar Nailah tana karɓar yaron ta.


'𝑭𝒊𝒗𝒆 𝒎𝒊𝒏𝒖𝒕𝒆𝒔 𝒕𝒐 𝒈𝒐'


Saida sukayi dariyar ta ishesu sannan suka tsaya suna sauke ajiyar zuciya a tare kamar wa'inda suka sha gudun fanfalaƙe.


"Okay kun kammala?, yanzu zaku iya faɗa mana abin da ya faru?," Nainarh ta tambaya tana jiran amsa.
Fashewa da dariya Deejarh ta kumayi kamar wata zautacciya.


Da kyar Zaynerb ta samu ta saita nutsuwar ta sannan ta fara basu labari kamar haka.

"Ɗazu ne fa, mun fita mu uku, ni da Lailah da kuma Deejarh zasu rakani gidan Sunainarh, domin ta kowa min wani topic,  tohm munje gidan batanan, ta tafi gidan surukanta ita da mijinta, to shine fa, mun fito zamu dawo gida, a daidai compound ɗin gidan muka haɗu da karen gidan, shida mai kula dashi da alama sun dawo daga £vening exercise ne, shine fa, ita Lailah gabaɗaya saboda ruɗewar da tayi na ganin wannan karen, batama lura karen a ɗaure yake da sarƙa ba, ina faɗamuku da ta tattare skirt ɗinta ta ranta da gudu, shi kuma kare yabi ta a three sixty 360.

Nan suka dinga zagaye gidan Lailah tana gudun ceton rai, sai daga ƙarshe ta samu, ta faɗa swimming pool 'In indistinctely, sannan ta samu ta tsira sai kuma kawai ya dawo kan Deejarh dayake tanada wayau sai tayi kamar ta suma shikuma kare da yaga haga yayi gaba abinshi, sannan ne fa, Iliya ya samu ya ɗaureshi da dabara."





*'𝑫𝒂𝒅𝒂 𝒂𝒍𝒌𝒂𝒎𝒊𝒏'*


*~☆ƚԋҽ ɳαιɳαɾԋ ƙԃ☆~*






Tunda ȥaynerb ta fara rattaɓa bayani,  Nailah da Afeefah suke ɓaɓɓaka dariya kamar taɓaɓɓu, ita kuwa Nainarh, baki ta saki tana kallonsu su duka cike da mamaki, saida taji Zaynerb takai aya, sannan ta sauke ajiyar zuciya.

Kallonta ta mayar kan Lailah, ɗan murmushi tayi ganin yadda gabaɗaya hallitar Lailah ya canza dan wuya, don komai mazewar ka idan kaga fuskar Lailah a lokacin kuma aka baka labarin abin da ya faru dole kaji dariya tazo maka 😀




Still cikin ɗan murmushi Nainarh, ta ce "au ashe dama Lailah har yanzu kinanan da wannan shegen tsoron karen naki?,"

Lailah tana shafa fuskarta dake ma ta raɗaɗi raɗaɗi ta ce "wallahi Auntie Nainarh bazaki gane bane kawai, dan bakiga yadda karen nan ya biyoni bane kawai, ni harna sadaƙar ma wannan mayen karen sayyayi Bitten ɗina, sai kuma Allah ya tsare, na faɗa pool.
Afeefah tana ɗan saita nutsuwarta ta ce "yanzu tunda kin tsira, sai kije kiyi wanka, ki gurje duka dattin da ƙuran da kare yasa kika kwaso ko?," Afeefah ta ƙarishe maganar da alamar tsokana.
Harara Lailah ta maka ma ta sannan ta ce "ai ko baki faɗa ba, madam yanzu zan wuce."
Kallon Zaynerb tayi ita kanta Lailah ɗin, dariya yana zuwa ma ta amma ta maze ta ce, "wai kuwa Auntie Zaynerb nace ina folder's ɗin, dana gani ɗazu a hannun ki ne, waɗanda sanadin su na shiga halin nan?,"


Ajiyar zuciya Zaynerb ta sauke tana mai ƙoƙarin ta tuna inda ta zubar ta ce.

"ni kaina, banmasan a gurin dana zubar ba, inaji garin gudun ceton rai ne, na zubar."

"Gashi fa, a hannuna na riƙe, ɗazu garin gudu kika zubar, ni kuma na kwashe na riƙe miki, a hannuna,"  Deejarh ta faɗa tana miƙawa Zaynerb folder's ɗin.
Karɓa Zaynerb tayi tana faɗin, "gaskiya kinyi abin kirki nagode miki obaa highly."
Lailah ta ce "aini da komawa gidan Sunainah kuma sai wani ikon Allah ɗin, dan bazan har abada ba, kuma bazan ce nan nan kusa ba."
Lailah tayi maganar a lokacin da ta miƙe tayi hanyar bedroom ɗinta, tanaji su Afeefah suna kuma ma ta dariya amma ko tsayawa batayi ba.









________***___🎀




𝑾𝒖𝒓𝒂𝒓𝒆𝒏 𝒌𝒂𝒓𝒇𝒆 𝒕𝒂𝒓𝒂 𝒅𝒂𝒊 𝒅𝒂𝒊 𝒏𝒂 𝒅𝒂𝒓𝒆💫


Afeefah ta tura ƙofar bedroom da Deejarh take kwana, a hankali ta shiga, kamar yadda tayi hasashe, kwance ta sameta saman bed tana fuskan tar stylish ceiling na bedroom, idonta a buɗe kamar mai  tunanin wani abu ko ƙarisowar wani abun.

Guri ta samu a gefen bed ɗin, ta zauna tana kallon Deejarh wacca alamu ya nuna har yanzu batasan ma da shigowar ta ba.

A hankali ta kira sunan ta 'Deejarh'," juyowa Deejarh tayi tana kallon ta ba ta ce komai ba, murmushi Afeefah tayi Sannan ta ce "mekike tunani ne haka?, na shigo bakisani ba."

"Tunanin abubuwa da yawa, Balarabiyar india ina tunanin abubuwa da yawa da yawa sosai fa," tayi maganar tana kallon Afeefah ta ci gaba da cewa, "ina tunanin Ummata da ta rasu ta barni, ina tunanin Abbana da ya fi son yaran matar shi akaina, ina tunanin gwaggona da take matuƙar ƙaunata, ina tunanin komai da kowa, ina tunanin yadda GOBE NA zata kasance, ina cikin kaɗaici sosai, Balarabiyar india."


"Tunda ta fara maganar Afeefah take kallon ta, tana mamakin ashe tana da hankali haka, da har zata tsaya tunanin iyayenta, da gobenta, ta wani ɓangaren kuma tana kuma tausayawa Deejarh ɗin yarinyar ta taso batasan Menene soyayyar iyaye ba, kuma a irin labarin da Auntie sa'adah ta bata ta fahimci yarinyar ba wani shaƙuwace a tsakanin ta da mahaifin ta ba, daɗin da ɗawa kuma ta fahimci yarinyar gabaɗaya ba'a ma ta training irinna na uwa ba, sannan kuma tana da matuƙar wauta.

Ajiyar zuciya ta sauke tana kuma kallon Deejarh ɗin sannan ta fara magana.

"Deejarh kenan, Ni ma a baya, a lokacin ban wuce shekaru irinna naki ba a duniya, haka nake ƙurewa can cikin bedroom  nasha kukana idan na tuna mahaifiyar mu, wani lokacin kuma idan Momy Rafi'at ta siyoma su Lailah kayan wasa banda, ni da ƴar uwata Zaynerb, haka zan shige ƙasan bed nasha kukana idan Auntie Zaynerb tana nan ta rarrasheni, idan kuma bata nan haka zantayi har saina ba uku lada." badan komai nake wannan kukan saidan irin ƙiyayyar da Momy Rafi'at take nuna mana ni da Zaynerb ƙiri_ƙiri."

Da sauri Deejarh ta ce "dama ba Momyn bane ta haifeku ke da Aunty Zaynerb ɗin?,"
Dariya Afeefah tayi tana share hawaye ta ce "E, ba ita ta haife mu ba ni da Auntie Zaynerb, Mahaifiyarmu ta rasu ranar da ta haifeni sunanta HAJARA ina matuƙar kewarta da begenta."

"Lah Balarabiyar india, kuka kikeyi ne?," Deejarh ta tambaya tana miƙewa ta dawo kusada Afeefah ɗin ta zauna.
Ba shiri Afeefah tayi dariyar pretending sai dai maganar da tayi niyyar faɗa ba ita ce, ta fito daga bakinta ba, tun daga ƙasar zuciyarta maganar ta fito, cewa tayi.
"E, mana kuka nakeyi, kukan tuna mahaifiyar mu nakeyi,"

"To tana inane mahaifiyar taku?,  Deejarh ta tambaya dan bataji lokacin da Afeefah ta ce ta rasu ba.

"Ta rasu a washe garin ranar da ta haifeni," Afeefah ta faɗa tana kuma share hawaye ta ci gaba da cewa "ni da ƴar uwata Zaynerb mun taso kwata kwata bamusan Menene soyayyar mahaifiya ba, mahaifin mu kawai muka sani kuma dashi kaɗai muka shaƙu sai kuma kakarmu AMMA Zaynerb ce ma ta shaƙu da ita fiye da dani,"
"to ita AMMA ɗin tana ina ne, halin yanzu?," Deejarh ta tambaya.
"Tana KANO STATE," Afeefah ta bata amsa, Deejarh ta kuma cewa.

"to meyasa bata dawo nan ba, ta zauna tare daku," Afeefah ta ce "tana zaune a gidan ƙanin mahaifin mu Uncle sagir ne da yake zaune a KANO ɗin shida iyalan sa, shiyasa bata dawo nan ba, kumaa, shiru tayi ganin Deejarh tana hawaye "Menene kuma ya sakaki hawaye?,"
Afeefah ta tambaya, cikin kuka Deejarh ta ce "ba komai kawai dai na tuna kakanni nane dukansu sun rasu Ummata ma ta rasu, Abbana kuma baya sona,"
Janyota zuwa jikinta Afeefah tayi tana rarrashi ta ce "addu'a zaki musu ba kuka ba, kuma Abbanki yana sonki, kawai dai ki bashi lokaci shida kanshi zai nemeki kinji ko?," gyaɗa ma ta kai tayi alama taji.

"Good girl haka nake so" Afeefah ta faɗa tana miƙewa ta nufa wardrobe dake bedroom ɗin, tana faɗin "bari muga kayan ƙawantawa ƙilama sunfi nawa kyau da tsari highly," 😍

Ba shiri Deejarh ta fashe da dariya tana faɗin "kai Auntie Afeefah," 
Dariya ita ma Afeefah tayi ta ce "E, mana da gaske nake wata ƙilama sunfi nawa kyau, ta ce kuma gobe ki shirya zamu fita  shopping, Daddy yace gobe muje ki zaɓa duk abin da ranki yakeso, kijido baki da wata damuwa koma duka kayan SHOPPING MALL kikace kinaso yace a kwaso a kawo miki gabaɗaya😂
Haka Afeefah ta ci gaba da janta da fira saboda ta mantar da ita damuwarta.

Kallon Haleesat dake hawaye, Momy Rafi'at tayi, sannan murya ƙasa ƙasa ta fara ma ta magana, "Haleesat ina kika shiga ne?,  yau ba'a ganki ba a gidannan ba, gabaɗaya lungu da saƙo na gidannan babu inda bamu duba ba, amma bamu ganki ba, faɗamin Menene ya faru dake kuma daga ina kike yanzu?, please tell me, everything my dear."

Shiru Haleesat tayi taƙi tayi magana sai faman matsar qwalla takeyi.
"wai nace bada ke ake magana bane, da zaki tsaya kina ma mutane kukan banza da hofi," a wannnan taƙin Nainarh ce tayi maganar ranta a mugun ɓace, dan ji take kamar ta tashi ta shaƙe wuyan Haleesat ɗin, har saita faɗa ma ta gidan uban da ta kwana, ta tabbatar da Haleesat ba'a gidan ta kwana ba.

A bakin wani kata faren shopping mall motocin nasu guda uku duka farare sukayi parking, a tare ko wannensu ya fito daga motar da yake hanyensu cikin na juna.



Habba nanfa kallo ya koma sama wai akace shaho ya ɗau giwa, gabaɗaya hankalin ƴan mata da samarin, dake wajen ya dawo kansu, kyau iya kyau malam, To dayake ita ma Deejarh farace saita haɗe dasu kai sai kace ƙaramar ƙanwar su ce ita saboda yadda sukayi similar.


Cikin nutsuwa da wayewa irinna cikakuna ƴan boko, kuma waɗanda ilimin addini ya ratsasu highly, suke tafiya abinsu a tare.

'MAI KYAU ABBAN FEEFAH SHOPPING MALL'

Deejarh ta karanta abin da taga an rubuta a saman Ƙaton shopping mall ɗin da suka nufa, babban plaza ne mai kimanin hawa ashirin da biyar ciki, kusan komai na glass ne a gurin, ga security nan, masu kula da shige da fice.



Shiga ciki sukayi, baki da hanci kawai Deejarh ta saki, ganin irin kyau da tsaruwa irinna na gurin, gabaɗaya ma'aikatan gurin sun taru guri guda suna kawo gaisuwa, ga ƴaƴan 'MISTER MAI KYAU' idan wannan ya kawo gaisuwa aka wuce gurin da yake, wanda akazo kanshi shima yayi, Deejarh bata gama shan mamaki ba, har saida taga wasu daga cikin ma'aikatan gurin sun jero kekunan da ake zuba siyayya a ciki in  Aisle, sun taru guri ɗaya suna jira su Afeefah su fara zabar abin da suke so, su kuma suke turo kekunan suna binsu a baya, har sai sun kammala.




Hannun Deejarh da ta dage tana nuna ainihin kalar ƙauyanci a gurin, Afeefah taja ganin yadda ta saki baki da hanci tana kallo.
Ɓangaren kayan sawa ta kaita, kallon Deejarh ɗin Afeefah tayi, murmushi ta ma ta sannan ta ce.

"ki zaɓi kalar  abin da ranki keso kinji, baki da matsala,"  
"to shikenan,"  Deejarth ta ba Afeefah, amsa sannan ta fara ruwan ido, tsaban haɗuwar gurin rasa dame zata fara tayi, sai faman ruwan ido take tama rasa me zata zaɓa, gane hakan ne, yasa Afeefan tayi murmushi sannan ta fara jidan ma ta kaya kala kala iri iri.



Tun daga kan abayoyi, atamfofi, materials masu tsada, laces, shadoji, £nglish wears, jakun kuna, gyalulluka, takalma, sarƙoƙi na gold, abin hannu.
Zobe, kayan kwalliya, da dai sauran su.





Kallon Haleesat da tayi shiru, Zaynerb ta kuma a karo na ba adadi, tana ƙara karantar yanayinta, ta lura tunda suka shigo gurin Haleesat bata ɗauka favorates ɗin ba, wato €nglish wears ko wasu ƙananan kaya ba, saima atamfofi da yadin hijabai dama hijaban da kuma abayoyi da taga tana faman jido ido rufe.
Sosoi mamaki ya kamata highly, sai dai bata ma ta magana ba,  ta rabu da ita.





Hango wani perfect perfume, irin na musamman ɗinnan kuma wanda ta daɗe tana neman kalan shi, bata samu ba, sai yanzu, yasa ta saki murmushi sannan in over rushing, ta nufa gurin, ta kai hannu kenan zata ɗauka, taga wani hannun da ba nata ba, yayi gaggawan ɗauka kafin nata hannun ya ɗauka.
Da sauri ta ɗago dan ganin wani mahaluki ne, haka.

"Tabarakallahu ahsanul khalin😍 Masha Allah😎 Anya kuwa wannan mutum ne tsaye a gabana a wannan TAKUN?,"

Wato tsaban kyau da irin haɗaɗɗiyar surarar, jikin wannan kyakyawan Mutumin dake gabanta ne, yasa tayi wannan maganar, tare da yiwa kanta wannan tambayar, yayinda kyawawan idanunta, suke kafe ta ƙure wa, mamallakin haɗaɗɗiyar kyakyawar surarar dake gabanta😇




Ganin tsantsan kyau da kamala daɗin daɗawa, ga wani uban kwarjini da ya ma ta, wannan bawan Allah dake a tsaye gabanta, sanye cikin  Expensively GUCCI higher  Dress, tun daga kan p-cap brown color, dake nutse cikin sumar kanshi wacca jelar ta, tsaban tsayin da take dashi, ta leƙo ta waje.

Har izuwa Cost  shoes ɗin dake special kyawawan ƙafafun shi, komai na companyn 'GUCCI' ne, hatta tsadadden watch ɗin dake ɗaure a tsintsiyar hannun shi.

Ta sani kuma tasan sannan tanaji ana faɗin tanada kyau na nunawa a television kyau irin highly ɗinnan, amma yau kuma a yanzu sannan a wannnan taƙin, taga wanda ya damata a kyau nesa ba kusa ba, ya shanye.


Tsayawa tayi da kallon ƙurillan da take mishi, saida ta saita nutsuwar ta sannan ta kuma kallon shi ta ce "please bawan Allah, ka barmin wannan perfume ɗin, ka nemi wani."

Tun kafin ta ƙarisa mishi maganar ya jefo ma ta tambaya da faɗin.

"Because of what reason"

Gyara tsayuwar shi yayi yana lumshe kyawawan idanun shi, sannan ya ce "amma kuma, na ga maza ne ko, And naga ke macece, So bakya buƙatar amfani da abin da, ba naki ba."


Kallon shi tayi jin abin da yace, sai kuma tayi murmushin rainin wayo ta  bashi amsa.

"E, na sani akwai wanda nakeso naba ne shiyasa, sorry dan Allah ka bani, ka nemi wani, rayuwata zan ba, kuma kasan rayuwa yadda take ko?,"


Yi yayi kamar zai bata, sai kuma ya fasa ya jefa cikin kwandon dake gaban shi nasa kayan siyayyar, har zai tafi, sai kuma ya juyo ya kalleta sannan yace.

Yana faɗin haka yayi gaba abinshi ya barta da bin bayan shi da kallo tana cizan yatsa.

cikin sauri ya ƙariso gurin da suke a tsaye, kallon Afeefah yayi sannan ya mayar da kallon shi akan Lailah, ya fa ruɗe ya kasa gane wacece ma Lailah a cikin su saboda yanayin shigar dake jikin su kala ɗaya ne, baya da hakan kuma Afeefah da Lailah suna ɗan kama, normally irin wanda ake samu tsakanin ƴan uwa da suka haɗa uba ɗaya😍

 In rushing  ya juyo yana kallon ta, sai kuma yayi murmushi yana amsawa da.

Ta faɗa tana kallon Lailah dake galla ma ta harara ba ta bi takan Lailah ba, tayi gaba abinta.

"To ke Afeefah mana ki amince da wannan challenging ɗin, kodan kiyi winning akansu mana,"

Dama sun saba duk lokaci bayan lokaci, sukan zauna idan basu da aikin yi, ko school, haka zasu zauna suna challenging tsakanin junan su, duk wacca aka ƙalubalanta akan wani abun, idan tayi daidai yadda akeso, ta cike duka ƙa'idoji, tofah tayi winning, kuma tanada gwagwaɓar kyauta da mutum biyu, wato Momy Rafi'at [Mrs Mai Kyau] da kuma shi kanshi uban gayyan, wato Alhaji Abubakar Mai Kyau [Mister Mai Kyau].

Gwaggwaɓar kyautar ita ce.

Na Farko

Kyautar zunzurutun kuɗaɗe Dalar America Bundles Uku da kuma canjin Mota zuwa na yayi daga gurin Momynsu Rafi'at Mrs Mai Kyau


Na Biyu



Kyautar Tsadaddiyar Waya da kuma zuwa duk inda mutum yakeso, in free ba tare da securities ba, sannan da ƙarin kuɗaɗe.


To shine fa yau suka canza tsarin abin, ya koma zasu ƙalubanci Afeefah akan zasu haɗa lambobi dukaninsu, za'a kira waya tofah duk wanda ya ɗauka wayar, idan ta macece zasu ƙulla ƙawance da Afeefah, kuma Afeefah zata kasance ta faɗi sunan Yarinyar da kuma garin da state ɗin da take, ba tare da yarinyar ta faɗa ma ta ba.


Idan kuma Namji ne, tofah salon na daban ne, domin zata kasance a duk bayan kwana ɗaya, tana kiranshi a waya ne, kuma zasu ƙulla soyayya, mai ƙarfi tsakanin su, ba kuma tare da sunsan fuskar  junan su ba, kuma zata kasance mai tura mishi massage's in daily, sannan a yanzu idan ya ɗauka wayar zata faɗi sunan shi ba tare da an faɗa ma ta ba, baya da waɗannan ma akwai sauran sharuɗa daban daban, akan challenging ɗin.

"Tunda haka kukeso, to na amince, da za'a haɗa lambobi ayi kiran sannan abi yadda abin yake,"

Afeefah tayi maganar tana kallon su gabaɗaya, murmushi ko wannensu yayi sannan.

Lailah da ta fi kowa zaƙewa a abin ta ce, "Yauwa sis ƴar gari, yanzu tunda kin amince, sai a haɗa lambobin ayi kirar, Ni dai na fara da sifli {0} da kuma Tara {9} sai wata sifli {0} ɗin, saura ke sister Zaynerb, ae a ƙa'idar abin, ita Afeefah ba za ta faɗi lambar ba ko?,"

Lailah tayi tambayar tana kallon ƴan uwannata, su duka suka amsa da "E, haka ne, ita Afeefah ba za ta faɗa ba,"


Zaynerb ta gyara zama tana faɗin, "ni kuma na gaba kenan zan faɗa ko, tunda kin faɗa guda uku, to na zaɓa 'guda'  sai ke Haleesat,"

Zaynerb tayi maganar tana kallon Haleesat ɗin, alamar turn ɗin ita Haleesat ɗinne yanzu.



"ke Zaynerb menene kuma wani 'guda' karfa ki mana irinna 'MUFEEDA' mai cewa tayar mota takwas tayar mota takwas fa, ke da zakice 'ɗaya'😀


Gabaɗaya palon dariya sukayi harda ita kanta Zaynerb ɗin, wato kar tayi musu irinna 'MUFEEDA' ta littafin 'ASMABAFFA' mai taken 'KASUWANCINA'😅😇



"Kai sis Nainarh, to bari na canza, na zaɓa 'ɗaya' {1} gaban wanda Lailah ta faɗa," cewar Zaynerb ɗin tana dariyar maganar da Aunty Nainarh ɗin tayi.


"Okay, ae gaskiya na faɗa ni kuma na zaɓa 'ɗaya' {1} ɗin again a gaban naki," ita ma Nainarh ta faɗa sannan ta ce, "Haleesat saura ke,"

ita ma Haleesat zaɓa tayi ta faɗa, sannan Nailah, wacca duk drama ɗin da ake kwasa, tana can tayi, baƙuwa wata ƙawarta 'Saddiqa', to sun fito palon da 'Saddiqan' zata ma ta rakiya, ita ma ta tsaya akayi da ita, hatta saddiqa ɗin ita ma, sai da ta faɗa na ta, sannan Deejarh sai kuma Momy Rafi'at, da sukaga saura ragowar lamba ɗaya lambobin su cika, shine kawai Afeefah ta faɗa ɗayan lambar sannan lambar wayar ya cika Goma Sha Ɗaya cifffff😛




Taruwa sukayi guri ɗaya wayar Afeefah na hannun Lailah da ta latsa kiran wannan lambobi da suka haɗa, sai dai da privet number ɗin Afeefah, Lailah ɗin ta danna kiran, dan Afeefah ɗinne ta buƙaci hakan.

*ʜɪɢʜᴇʀ ʙɪʟʟɪᴏɴɪᴇʀ's sᴛʀᴇᴀᴍ Qᴜᴀᴛᴇʀ's*




Sunan Unguwan kenan, a farkon shiga Area ɗin, aka rubuta hakan jikin wani tsadadden 'sign board' wanda shi kanshi alamu ya nuna ba ƙananun kuɗaɗe, aka zubar wajen samar dashi ba, in fonts akayi rubutun da launin sky purple sannan kuma in stylish.


A gaskiyar lamari ita wannan haɗaɗɗiyar unguwa, ba irin na ƙananu ƙananun masu naira bane, wannan unguwar, tsadaddiyar unguwa ce, wacca sai kaci ka tada kai da naira sannan kake shiga irin ta, har ka kai ga zama cikinta, sannan ba'a zama cikinta haka kara zube, sai anyi register sannan kuma akwai takardu da lasisin zama cikinta.


Gabaɗaya gidajen Area ɗin, yanayi da tsari na ginin su da komai kala ɗaya ne, hatta yanayin haɗaɗɗun gate's ɗin kala ɗaya ne, da alama company ɗaya ne yayi, ko kuma ma gabaɗaya Area ɗinne mallakin wani companyn😎
