A gaskiya ganin cikin gidan daga ciki, yafi gani daga waje, domin komai na gidan abin burgewa ne, so thrilling, securities ne cikin gidan ta ko'ina sai dai yawan securities na gidan baisa gidan yana da wata haniya ba.


A gaskiya tsayawa faɗamuku irin tsari na ginin gidan, lokaci bazai bamu damar gudanar da hakan ba, sai dai kawai Just muyi manage da abin da ya samu.


Domin hatta ƙasan gidan, bawai inter log bane kamar yadda kuka saba gani a wasu gidajen, a'a wasu expensive tiles ne bawai irin masu cutarwa ba, no just tiles masu tsada da tsari, daga cikin katafen palon gidan kuwa, ma'aikata ne, wajen guda goma, ke kai kawo ciki.


Daga tsakiyar palon yake lumtse saman tattausar stool irin na sarauta, wanda ake zama ko kwanciya ciki.


Dogo ne shi, sannan mai kyakyawar haɗaɗɗiyar surar jiki ga qwamji, ga ƙira irinna mazajen sadaukai.

Yana da Jar fata, irinna larabawan ƙasar Ethiopia, sannan kuma yana da kyau irin na musamman ɗinnan, sumar kanshi, ba wata doguwa bace can irinna larabawa, sai dai tasha aski irinna haɗaɗɗun gayu in stylish, da ganin irin gyaran da aka ma sumar kasan ba ƙaramin, ji da ita ake ba.

Sanye yake cikin wata tsadaddiyar jallabiya ƙirar Saudi, hannayenshi gabaɗaya tallafe da Tablet {kwamfutar hannu} sakin tablet ɗin yayi ta koma saman lap's ɗinshi.

Yayinda yayi tagumi hannu bibbiyu, yana kuma waro fararen idanun shi, yana watsa su saman wannan tablet.

Murmushi tayi tana yiwa mai ɗaukarta video nuni da hannu alamar ta dena, sai dai da alama mai ɗaukar nata video ɗin, ƴar rigima ce domin taƙi ta dena abin da takeyi, saima juyawa da camara ɗin da take ɗaukar da ita gefe da tayi, nan fuskar wani matashin saurayi ya bayyana, kusan sakin camara ɗin, tayi tana waro ido, dan batayi tsammanin zataga mutum tsaye ba, ƙoƙarin jefar da camara ɗin, tayi tsaban firgici.

Da sauri wannan budurwa da batason a ɗauketa video ɗin, ta  miƙe tsaye cikin zafin nama, ta taro camara ɗin sannan ta saita camara ɗin fuskar wancan budurwar tana dariya, ta kuma juyo da camara ɗin, tana fuskantar fuskarta, dariyar nishaɗi kawai takeyi tana bin sautin waƙar dake tashi a gurin,  a hankali cikin zazzaƙatar muryarta, gefe guda kuma tana ɗaukar videon dramar da ake kwasa tsakanin budurwar farko mai ɗaukar video da wannan saurayi.


Murmushi yayi mai bayyana dimples, dai dai lokacin ita ma, tayi nata ƙayataccen murmushin, da ya bayyana dimples ɗinta duka biyun.




Daga can haɗaɗɗen dining area, dake can keɓantaccen guri cikin palon kuwa.


Cikin nutsuwa ya kammala lunches ɗin nashi, sannan yakai hannu ya murɗa sink dake jikin table dining ɗin ya wanke hannun shi, duk bada hannu yaci abincin ba, goge hannun yayi da expensive tissues.


Dai dai taƙin kuma, tsadaddiyar haɗaɗɗiyar wayarshi, wacca kai tsaye ba nace ko ƙirar wata company bane, domin banga tambarin ko wani company ba, sai wani haɗaɗɗen tambarin rubutu da akayi wanda ya tafi dan.


'ᴏғғɪᴄɪᴀʟ'  haka akayi tambarin rubutun jikin wayar, rurin da tayi ne a lokacin, ya tabbatar mishi da kira ne ya shigo.


"Okay, Pick up,"

Ya furta ba tare da ya kallah, tsadaddiyar wayar, nashi dake saman table dine ɗin ba.

Nan take wayar tayi picking call, ɗin da kanta, ba tare wani ko wata daga cikin jerin ma'aikata mata mazan dake tsai tsaye a dining ɗin, ya sarrafa ko taɓanta ba.



Shiru yayi baice komai ba, kamar yadda ɗayan ɓangaren na kiran ma ba'ayi magana ba.

To shi dama ɗabi'arshi kenan, dalilin da ya sa ma, yayi picking ɗin, tunanin shine ya bashi wata ƙila ɗaya daga cikin privet securities na shi ne ya kirashi.


"Hello, 'Official,"


On the other hand, abin da aka faɗa kenan, da ɗan mamaki gabaɗaya ma'aikatan gurin suka saki baki suna kallon wayar, jin mace ta kira 'Boss' ɗinsu kuma ta kirashi da sunan shi wanda ba kowa ne, yake kiranshi da hakan ba.

Wato 'Official,' gabaɗaya suna da tabbaci da yaƙinin cewa ba, ɗaya daga cikin ƙannin shi mata bane, ta kirashi.

Shi kuwa 'Boss' ɗin nasu in slowly ya juyo karo na farko ya kalla wayar.


"Hello, 'Official on line, and who is on the line too?."


Tsintar kanshi yayi da faɗin hakan, a lokacin da ya ɗauki wayar, ya dawo da ita saman right hand ɗinshi, kare a kunneshi again.


'Smile' wacca ta kasance ƴar asalin ƙasar china ne, kuma ɗaya daga cikin manyan ma'aikatan 'Official', tsaban mamaki da ya isheta ganin 'Boss' a yau ya amsa kiran wata budurwa, kasa haɗiye gulmarta tayi, ɗan gyaran murya tayi tana yima sauran ma'aikatan su biyar maza huɗu mace ɗaya, alama da suka kalla 'Boss'.


"humm dama sunana, eh sunana 'RISHITAH' daga mai diguri najeriya, amma kaifa?, oh na manta, sunanka 'Official' ko?,"

Shiru yayi yana mazarin abin da zaice jin irin amsar da ta bashi.

Alama yayi ma gabaɗaya ma'aikatan da su tafi, 'Smile' badan taso ba, haka tabar gurin, ranta babu daɗi dan taso taji yadda 'Boss' 'Official' zai ƙare da wannan budurwa da ta kira kanta, da 'RISHITAH' 😀



"Official, yau cikin farin ciki nake, domin naga muradin raina fa,"


haɗaɗɗen kyakyawan saurayin yayi maganar ne yana zama kan kujerar dake gefan wanda Official yake zaune saman ta, bayan ya ƙariso dining ɗin.

"To shikenan, Allah ya kawomu lokaci,  zaka fara surutun  naka kenan,"

Official ne yayi maganar bayan ya aje tsadaddiyar wayar nashi gefe, bayan budurwar da ta kirashi ita da kanta ta katse kiran, bayan tayi mishi introducing kanta, sannan yayi maganar yana kallon saurayin nan mai sanye cikin jallabiya ne.

Ƙayataccen murmushi yayi yana shafa ɗan gemun shi na gayu  yace.

"Official a yau da gaske nake, na ganta, kuma a video na ganta, a pajin wata wai 'ƴar rigima' ka yarda,"

Baya Official yayi sannan jingina da kujerar dining ɗin, hannayen shi biyu, harɗe a saman faffaɗan ƙirjinshi, so yake yayi magana, sai dai da ya buɗe bakinshi babu muryar da yakeji sai zazzaƙar muryarta, 'Rishitah' haka sunan yake ƙara tariyowa a kwakwalwar shi, ganin abin zayyi yawa, {Official da tunanin mace 😂}  yasa ya lumshe kyawawan idanun shi ba tare da ya furta komai ba.


"nasani, daga jin yadda yayi shirun nan baice komai ba, nasan bazai wuce tunanin ta yake ba," Saurayin ya furta ƙasa ƙasa yana kuma kallon picture's na ta, dake saman dekstop na tablet dake hannun shi.


"Bardiaa!"


Official yaja sunan idanun shi a lumshe still.


"E, sunana ne hakan, wanda ka raɗamin shekaru Ashirin da wani abu baya, challenging ɗinka nake, shin zaka ce min ba tunanin ta kake ba?,"


"Tunanin ita wa kenan?,"

Official ya tambaya yana buɗe Blue shines £yes nashi ya watsasu kan 'Bardia'.


"Ita mana, wacca ku ka haɗu ɗazu a shopping mall, har tana baka kyautar perfect perfume, ko zakace ba tunaninta kake ba, sannan ma ae ita ce, ta kiraka yanzunnan, 'Official' babu wani pretend da zakamin kawai dai 'KYAKYAWA' zatayi suruka ƴar Nijeriya nan da wani lokaci 😍


"Bardia na lura yau, ka zauce na ganinta fa, bari na baka guri, ka gama tukunna."

Official yayi maganar yana miƙewa tsaye ya ɗauki tsadaddiyar wayar shi, ya nufa haɗaɗɗen palon ya zauna abin shi. Murmushi Bardia ya raka Official dashi.


Official dogo kuma kyakyawan matashin baturen saurayi da ya haɗa jini da larabawa, yana da kyau obaa highly, ga kwanji jikinshi a mugun murɗe yake sosai, yana da yalwar sumar kai tamkar mace, don saboda yawanta har takai ga sayya ɗaureta ta sauka ta gadon bayanshi.


Gabaɗaya su biyun daga 'Official' ɗin har shi kanshi 'Bardia' ɗin ba kalar samarin da mace mai ji da kanta first class lady, zata gani kallo guda taƙi ƙara kallo bane.


Dukaninsu su biyun kyawawan samari ne ajin farko a kyau kuma jinin larabawa, domin shi 'Bardia' gabaɗayanshi balarabe ne, gaba da baya, iyaye da kakanni, yayinda shi kuma 'Official' have case ne domin ya haɗa jini da larabawa sannan ya haɗa da turawa.

Haka suka cigaba da fira suna taɓa snacks ɗin gaban su har aka dawo class.


Wannan karon macece ta shigo ta koyar dasu Hausa language,  'Malama Fateema' bayan ta gama ta fita wani ma ya shigo a haka har aka tashi sallah bayan sunje sunyi, sun dawo islamic aka koyar dasu dayake haka tsarin makarantar yake.


Ƙarfe uku dai dai aka tashe su, a tare Deejarh da Falilat da kuma Farhad suka fito.

"to shikenan,"

Falilat ta amsa ta sannan ta shiga motar su driver yaja suka tafi.

"oyoyo Daddy kayi surprised ɗinmu sosai, shine baka faɗa mana yau zaka dawo ba ko?."

Momy Rafi'at ta ce "tunda dai ya dawo lafiya, ai sai ki bari ya shiga daga ciki, sannan sai kiyi shagwaɓar yadda kikeso." Momy tayi maganar fuskar ta a washe.



Ɗunguma sukayi suka shiga ciki, hannayen Lailah cikin nasu su duka, Afeefah da taji Lailah shiru ta taso domin ganin abin da ke faruwa, saukayi ido huɗu da Daddynsh, ai batasan lokacin da ta taka da gudu ba, ta rungumeshi tana dariya shima Mister Mai Kyau, dariya yakeyi.

"zaki ɓalla mana Daddy fa, wannan abu haka kamar ƙaramar yarinya."
Zaynerb da suka ƙariso gurin ta faɗa hakan, Daddyn yace "abarmin shalelena ta wataya abinta, aini zanso ta karyani ma."

Nainarh tana murmushi ta ce "mu kuma ba zamuji daɗi ba, idan aka karya mana Daddynmu."



"Ya naga kamar ana wata qwarya qwaryar party, ba'a gayyacemu ba?."

Mister Mai Kyau, daya hango abubuwan maqulashe a dining, yayi maganar.


Zaynerb ta buɗe baki zata bashi amsa, da sauri Haleesat dake kusa da ita ta rufe ma ta baki da hannayenta, ganin haka yasa Lailah faɗin.


"ah dama Haleesat ce ta antaya soyayya fa, shine muka shirya wannan ƴar qwarya qwaryar partyn, domin tayata murna ta ƙarishe tana qintse dariya, ganin yadda Haleesat take antayo ma ta harara, tana raba ido kamar baiwar da tayiwa sarki ƙarya😝



"Okay shine ba'a gayyacemu ba, zakuyi ku kaɗai, to qwara da Allah yasa muka dawo yanzu sai ayi damu."

Momy Rafi'at tayi maganar tana ajiye Lab coat da jakar ta saman center table.

💘JIEDDARH💘





Daga kwancen da take a saman bed ɗinta, ta jiyo knocking sama sama, amma a yanayin gajiyar da takejin kanta, tana tunanin koma waye wannan mai knocking sai dai ya haƙura sai gobe koma miye.
Saboda baccin dake a idanunta sai dai me, jin da tayi mai buga ƙofar yaƙi haƙura, yasa ba shiri ta sauko daga saman gadon tana ya mutsa fuska alamar bacci takeji over.

Palon dake a bedroom ta fito sannan ta nufa ainihin ƙofar ɗakin ta buɗe ƙofar. 

Da mamaki take kallon wacca take a bakin ƙofar, tana sakar ma ta murmushi. Ni'ima ce sanye cikin sleeping dress, dogon wando fari sai riga blue color, kanta da hula fara mai haɗe da blue.


Martanin murmushin ita ma Jiddarh ta mayar ma ta sannan ta matsa gefe, domin Ni'imar ta sama damar shigowa, shiga ciki tayi, wannan shine karonta na farko da ta shigo bedroom ɗin Jiddarh shiyasa batasan lokacin da ta saki baki tana kallon kyau da tsaruwa irinna na bedroom ɗin ba.



Wani kalar zallar kyau ne a bedroom ɗin, na nunawa a television tundaga kan fenti na ɗakin da yake ruwan grey, shikanshi wall na ɗakin ba'a ganin shi, an rufe shi da wasu haɗaɗɗun glass grey color, ga wasu frames na Jiddarh guda biyar da aka jere a jikin bangon.

Tayi kyau a hoton sosai, dama kuma itaɗin mai kyau ce, daga ɗayan gefe na bangon kuwa, wata ƴar ma daidaiciyar plasma tv ne, mai kyau da ita, gefe da wasu dogayen glass vases masu ɗauke da flowers kala kala, masu fidda wani ƙamshi na musamman.
Daga can gefen bangon kuma mirror ne, na dogon yaro wanda yake manne da Wall ɗin.
Gefen bangon kuwa shima wasu frames ne, wasu ɗauke da hoton Jiddarh ita da Humaira a cikin qanqara da alama ba'a Nigeria sukayi hoton ba, yayinda wasu kuma hotunane masu kyau.




Kallonta ta mayar a tsakiyar madaidaicin palon wanda aka kashe naira wajen tsara shi, kujerune set ɗaya suma grey nd milk color, yayinda sofa's ɗin suke grey color, su kuma cushions ɗin suke milk nd grey colors, haka zalika suma curtains na ɗakin grey nd milk color ne, hatta da Droplight na ɗakin hasken grey nd milk color suke bayar wa.
Ga kuma wasu kyawawan wind chime masu bada iska suma still grey nd milk color.



Kamar ance ta kalla can gefe aikuwa ta hango wasu haɗaɗɗun stools, na zama grey color masu faɗi an jeresu, guda huɗu tsakiya ga kuma wani Glass coffee table daga tsakiyar stools ɗin.



Daga saman wani madaidaicin table kuwa 'FISH TANK' ne ga wasu ƙanana kyawawan kifaye ciki, ruwan cikin kuwa yana bada launin sky blue ne.


Ni'ima batasan lokacin da ta koma ta zauna saman 1sieter ba, saboda kaɗuwar da tayi, da ganin palon Jiddarh.

Yes tasan mahaifin ta cikaken mai dukiya ne, wanda ba iya Nigeria kaɗai sunanshi ya zagaya ba, hadda ma ƙasashen waje, amma ita tunda take a rayuwar ta, bata taɓa ganin palon da ya tafi da imanin ta ba, irin palon Jiddarh, ya haɗu ƙarshe, idan akace haɗuwa to azo palon Jiddarh kawai magana ta ƙare🤗.




Tanan a zaune tana kuma bin ko'ina na palon da kallo gabaɗaya ta manta abin da ya kawota ɗakin, saboda tsananin kyan palon da ya kasheta, da sauran ranta a duniya😅

Jiddarh ta yaye wani curtains dake a can gefe, ta fito hannunta riƙe da mug na coffee sai faman steam yakeyi, tun lokacin da Jiddarh ta buɗewa Ni'imar ƙofa ta shiga wani ɗan korido da aka rufeshi da curtains, kai ba za ka taɓa cewa da wani abu a gurin ba, ashe wani ɗan ƙaramin kitchen ne, na dafa shayi a gurin.


Da sauri Ni'ima ta miƙe tsaye tana bin Jiddarh da kallo kamar na tsoro, bata jira komai taje ta yaye labulayen da taga Jiddarh ta fito ta ciki, sai kuma ta tsaya cak, wani mamakin yana kuma kamata wai still dai ga wani abun mamakin kuma, kitchen ne a palon daga ke ɓantaccen guri.


Ajiyar zuciya ta sauke sannan ta koma ta zauna tana kallon Jiddarh ɗan murmushin yaƙe tayi ta ce "ina fatan dai ban takura miki ba?."

Jiddarh ta girgiza kai alamar a'a sannan ta bata amsa da "ko ɗaya dama bacci nakeji kuma yaƙi zuwa...Sai kuma gaki, af ga coffee fa, kar yayi sanyi, miƙa ma ta tayi, Ni'ima ta karɓa yawunta har wani tsinkewa yakeyi, batasan lokacin da ta lumshe ido ba, sanda takai coffee ɗin a baki cikin ƙanƙanin lokaci, sai ga Jiddarh da Ni'ima sun ware  suna zuba fira, dama Jiddarh gata gwanar surutu.


Ni'ima ta ce "nifa rigar da kika ɗinka ne, nazo gani kinsan lokacin da kika fito da ita, kika nunawa su Humaira bana gidan."
Jiddarh tayi murmushi tana amsa ma ta da "ai ba damuwa tana bedroom ɗi na, zoki ganta mana."

Miƙewa sukayi tare suka nufi wannan mirror na dogon yaro dake cikin bedroom ɗin, wani waje Jiddarh ta danna a jiki saiga ƙofa ta bayyana, shi kuma mirror ya matsa gefe, ita dai Ni'ima sai zare ido take tana kuma kallon ikon Allah, "wato abin mamaki baya ƙarewa a duniya, yanzu wazayyi tunanin akwai wata ƙofa anan, balle kuma har a shiga bedroom ta gurin." Ni'ima take faɗan hakan a ranta.


Shiga ciki sukayi Jiddarh a gaba, Ni'ima kuma tana binta a baya.
"oh Allah yau dai, bazan gaji da ganin abin mamaki ba ashe," Ni'ima ta kuma faɗa a zuciyarta ganin tsantsan kyau da tsari da kuma burgewa na bedroom ɗin Jiddarh ashe ma ba komai ta gani a palo ba, a nan bedroom kallo yake.


Wani haɗaɗɗen glass sif dake manne a bango, taga Jiddarh ta buɗe ta ciro wata haɗaɗɗiyar riga ta shimfiɗe ta saman mattress.


Da sauri Ni'ima ta ƙarisa tana bin rigar da kallo kamar idanunta zasu zubo ƙasa, gabaɗaya kyan rigar ya tafi da ita, don ji take kamar ta ɗauki rigar ta ruga a guje, amma ina ba damar haka, akalla takai mintuna talatin tanabin ko'ina da ina na rigar da kallo.



Kallon Jiddarh tayi bakinta na rawa wajen faɗin "Jiddarh dan Allah na roƙeki ki faɗamin a wani company kika siya wannan special princely gown ɗin, wallahi tamin zan siya ko million nawa ne, koda kuwa companyn ba'a nan ƙasar yake ba🤩."


Dariya Jiddarh tayi ta bata amsa da "haba sister Beauty matar yaya H to be, ki kwantar da hankalin ki ba'a company na siya ba, ni ce ɗinka abina da kaina."

Waro ido Ni'ima tayi waje, ba shiri ta miƙe tana kallon Jiddarh cike da zaƙuwa take cewa.

"wai dan Allah da gaske, ke ce kika haɗa wannan, ko wasa kike."
Tambaya tana nuna rigar, murmushi Jiddarh kawai tayi ta bata amsa  "of course ni ce na ɗin ka, amma nakai a kalla watanni bakwai ina ɗin kawa idan ban manta ba."

Jiddarh tayi murmushi ta ce "haba, baki dai binkcika bane, amma ai ko telolin kowa da halin shi, ai baza'a taru a zama ɗaya ba, dole akwai na gari masu ƙoƙarin cika alkawari."


"E, wata ƙila baza'a rasa ba, amma wai meyasa bakya ɗinkin mutane ne, zafa ki sama kuɗaɗe sosai."
Dariya Jiddarh tayi sannan cikin nutsuwa ta ƙarisa wajen labule ta yaye curtains, ashe nan ma gabaɗaya wall ɗin gurin, glass ne.
Sai ga babban titin birnin Kano ya bayyana, kana ta juyo tana kallon Ni'ima da ta kuma sakin baki tana kallon ikon Allah, wato titi kuma yanzu, abin yayi fa, ba laifi tunda glass ɗin irin wanda mutumin da yake waje bazai hango komai bane, sai dai na ciki ya hango na waje.




Kafin Jiddarh ta ce wani abu, Ni'ima ta rigata da faɗin, "indai zaki dinga yi ni kuma zan dinga nemo miki customers kala kala, har saikin gaji dasu kedai kawai kice kin amince,"

Murmushi kawai Jiddarh tayi Ni'ima ta tono ma ta gurin dayake ma ta raɗaɗi, ita batasan menene matsalar ta ba, a baya can, ta fara ɗinki mutane suna kawo ma ta tanayi abu kamar wasa har sunanta ya fara zagaye gari qwararriyar tela lamba ɗaya a cewar mutane, saikuma batasan meya faru ba aka wari gari ko mutum ɗaya an dena kawo ma ta ɗinki haka kawai.

Ajiyar zuciya ta sauke tana mai amsawa da "shikenan ba damuwa zaki iya,"

Wani tsallen murna Ni'ima tayi kamar wacca aka ma ta bishara da aljanna, da sauri ta matso ta rungumo Jiddarh tana faɗin.

Palon sama ta fito ba kowa, dama ba tayi tunanin ganin kowa ba, tunda safe ne yanzu.

"Good morning Humaira, hope kintashi lafiya?."

Suna tafe suna taɓa fira har suka ƙarisa filin buga wasanni dake a can gefan gidan, ɓangaren buga wasan golf suka ƙarisa nan suka fara.

Harara Humaira ta galla ma ta ta ce "banayi, ko bakiji na ce yau ba ranar sa'ata bane?, dan haka, ja gefe kije ki Nemo ball ɗin ki da kanki."

"mutane suna bani mamaki, sai kiga idan mutum yayi wata gasa ko kuma dai, yayi rashin nasara a wani al'amari, sai kaji yana faɗin wai ranar ba ranar sa'a bane, ko nasara a gareshi, sai kace shi yake zaɓawa kanshi abin da zai faru a ƙaddararshi...A gaskiya mutane da yawa suna irin wannan kuskuren na faɗin haka wai ba ranar sa'a ba, da'ace nasara fa, yayi bazaka ji bakinshi ba, sai dai yayi gum hum."

Jiddarh ce duk takeyin wannan maganar cike da takaicin halin wasu mutanen.

Ajiyar zuciya Humaira ta sauke ta ce "a gaskiya Jiddarh you are right, saboda da yawa mutanenmu na yanzu, haka ake ba godiyar Allah sai dai kawai Allah yasa mudace. gabaɗaya su biyun suka amsa da Amin.





ᴍᴀɪ ᴋʏᴀᴜ, ғᴀᴍɪʟʏ



Zaune suke a dinning table suna karin kumallo da ya ke glass dinning  room ɗin yana ɗaukar kimanin mutane sha biyu ne, sai ya kasance Daddyn yana kujerar da take a miƙe, yayinda Momy Rafi'at ita ma take a ɗayar kujerar wacca ita ma take a miƙe saiya kasance Daddyn da Momynsuna suna fuskantar juna ne 😁.



Sannan kuma sauran kujerun ya kasance Nainarh tana daga ɓangaren hannun dama, ita kuma Nailah tana daga hannun hagu, saiya kasance suma suna kallon juna, gefan Nainarh kuwa Haleesat ce, ta ɓangaren hannun hagu, kuma Zaynerb ce suma kallon juna kenan.
Sannan kuma gefan Haleesat, Lailah ita ma suna fuskan tar juna ita da Afeefah, dake a hannun hagu sai kuma Deejarh a gefan Afeefan tana zaune ita ma.


Saiya kasance sauran kujerun dining ɗin guda uku, babu mutane wayam suke.

Daddy ya kallesu gabaɗaya, kowa ya mayar da hankali ana kai loma, yayi murmushi wani abu yana tsaya mishi a qahon zuciya, kamata yayi ace akwai yaro Namiji koda ɗaya ne, a gurin, amma haka Allah ya tsara kaddarar ta kasance.


Yana ina?, yana cikin ƙoshin lafiya, ko a kasin hakan, shin yana ma duniyar, idan ma yana raye a halin yanzu, wata kalar rayuwa yake gudanarwa, shin ya dena halayyar sa wacca ita ce sanadiyar da yasa shi barin gidan, ko kuwa bai dena ba, shin yaci abinci kuwa, shin akwai iyayen da suke kula dashi ne, shin yayi kewar Daddyn shi da Momyn shi da ƙannin shi kuwa, shin yana tunawa dasu a kullum.
Kamar yadda a  koda yaushe yake maƙale a zuciyoyin su, shin zai dawo gida kuwa, kamar yadda suke fata.

shin shin shin kalar waɗannan tambayoyin sune cike da koda yaushe a zuciyar shi, bai sani ba, amma yanaji yana da kaso tamanin cikin ɗari na halin da Babban ɗanshi, ɗaya tilo Namiji ya fa ɗa.


Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke sannan ya kore tunane tunanen da suke so, su rinjayo zuciyar shi daga dogon fushin da yake a ciki, ɗago da kanshi yayi karaf suka shige cikin na sahibar Matar shi /Momy Rafi'at/ da tun lokacin da ya faɗa tunanin, take an kare dashi, dama jira take ya ɗa go da kanshi..Girgiza mishi kai tayi alamar a'a.
Murmushi ya sakar ma ta kawai, ita ma ta mayar mishi da martanin murmushin.



Kallon shi ya mayar akan ƴa ƴan shi ma ta, farin cikin ko wani uba nagari😘

Ɗan jim yayi yana ƙare musu kallo kana yace, "ni banga hoton surukin nawa ba daɗin daɗawa, kuma ba'a faɗamin koda sunan shi ba, kodai rowan shi a keyi min?."

Ya ƙarishe maganar yana kallon Haleesat, fuskar shi cike da Annuri.

Wani irin tari ne mai haɗe da qwarewa yazo ma Haleesat, aikuwa nan tahau yi ba qaqqautawa.
Da sauri Zaynerb dake fuskantarta ta miƙa ma ta ruwa a cup, tana ma ta sannu.

Karɓa tayi ta kaba a baki ta shanye tas, sai faman sannu mutanen gurin sukeyi ma ta.

Ajiyar zuciya ta sauke tana kallon Daddyn nasu da ya zuba ma ta ido yana binta da kallon tambaya.

Da sauri ta ce "Daddy!," sai kuma tayi shiru, ta rasa abin cewa, dariya Zaynerb tayi ta ce.
"Allah Daddy komai za ta ce karka yadda saita faɗa maka sunan shi, sannan kuma ta baka hoton shi, mu ma mu gani, jiya ba kalar roƙon da ban ma ta ba, akan ta faɗamin ko da sunan shi ne. Amma taƙi."

Zaynerb ta ƙarishe maganar tana jefawa Haleesat hararar wasa.





Kafin wani ya kuma cewa komai, sai ga Lailah wacca ba wanda yasan lokacin da ta bar gurin, ta dawo hannunta riƙe da doguwar paper.


Gurin Daddyn ta ƙarisa da saurinta, ta tsaya ta gefan shi, sannan ta warware paper ɗin, sai ga zanen kyawawan matashin saurayi Gentle dashi, ya bayyana.

Lailah tana dariya tana faɗin "Daddy ga hoton surukin ka, wanda ɗiyar ka Haleesat ta zana shi, jiya jiyan nan."

Ƙurawa zanen ido Mister Mai Kyau yayi, yana yima zanen kallon kamar ya san fuskar a wani guri.

Yayinda duk kallo ɗaya da zayyima zanen, sai zuciyar Haleesat ta buga da ƙarfi, tayi tsuru tsuru tana jiran tsammani, ita da ko magana ta fatar baki bai taɓa shiga tsakaninta da mutumin ba, amma aje ta zana shi, har tana nunawa su Lailah masu bakin surutu, gashi yanzu tajama kanta Daddyn ya gani.



"To ba'a faɗamin sunan shi ba, ko shima saina roƙa ne?."

Taji muryar Daddyn yana faɗar hakan, da sauri ta ɗa go kanta tana kallonsu, gaba ɗaya sun zuba ma ta ido suna kallo, don suji abin da za ta ce.

Cike da in ina ta ce "sunan shi, wai sunan shi?, ohoo sunan shi, ae sunan shi 'MAHMOUD' ne fa." Tayi maganar tana cizan laɓɓanta na ƙasa da ƙarfi.


Gaba ɗayansu basu kula da halin da take ciki ba, sai Zaynerb ita kaɗai.
Lailah ta ce "suna mai daɗi, ashe shiyasa kike mana rowar faɗa." Tayi maganar cike da zaulaya.

"Anfa takura wa yarinyata, gabaɗaya kunsa sai kunya takeji haba dai."
Cewar Momy Rafi'at tana ɗan dariya. Suma dariyar sukayi Lailah ta kuma faɗin.
"Allah Momy ba wani kunya, hadda ma miskilanci." Tayi maganar tana komawa ta zauna gurin zamanta. 
Ta ci gaba da cewa "ni ina mamakin a inda Auntie Haleesat ta samo miskilanci, nasan dai Daddyn mu ba miskili bane, haka zalika Momy."

"kika sani ko'a cikin dangi ta gado?."

Nailah ta faɗa tana dariya, kallon bakusan komai ba Momy Rafi'at tayi musu, sannan ta ce, "abar kaza cikin gashinta kawai..."


Afeefah ta kalla Daddynsu ta ce "Allah Daddy ita ma Auntie Zaynerb, saita faɗa mana saurayinta, nasan dai ba za ta rasa saurayi ba, duk da ba kulasu takeyi ba..." Harara Zaynerb take ta faman jefawa Afeefah, amma tayi kamar bata gani ba, ta ci gaba da faɗin. "ina mamakin ki Auntie Zaynerb da kwata kwata bakyasan ayi miki maganar saurayi, koma aure gaba  ɗaya...Bakya kallon Momyn da Daddyn ne, gasu Auntie Nainarh da Nailah, duk gabaɗaya suna cikin farin ciki ae. soyayya da daɗi, ko ba haka bane?."

Tayi maganar fuskarta washe tana kallon su, duk ba wanda ya kulata saima  fuska da taga su Auntie Nainarh sun ɓata daga bisani ma tashi Nailah tayi ta bar gurin.
Baki sake Afeefah ta bisu da kallo, mai yayi zafi ita dai tasan iya kar gaskiyarta ta faɗa, a gurinta rayuwar aure farin ciki ne ciki gabaɗaya, a gurinta idan a kace ma'aurata, tofa anzo ƙarshen soyayya. Amma fa a nata silly tunanin😻

Ta miƙe zata bi bayanta, ta jiyo muryar Daddyn yana faɗin.

"dawo ki zauna?."

Da sauri ta kuma kallon shi, baki a sake sai tabi umarni ta koma ta zauna jiki ba gwari.






JIDDARH



"Ke kuma ina zaki da safiyar nan?."

Juyowa tayi dan ganin mai maganar, ganin su tsaye yasa ta saki fuska.
Ita batama lura da mutane ba, cikin sakin fuska ta ce "Jiddarh, Humaira kune ni?, harna ji tsoro banyi tunanin da mutane a bayana ba, sai kawai naji murya?."

Dariya Jiddarh tayi ta ce "ah mudai mun hangoki, tun daga nesa kinci kwalliya da safiyar nan, ina zakije haka?."
Ni'ima ta ce "gida zanje na dawo, daganan zan wuce gidan Auntie Nafeesah, ta ce naje na karɓo yarinyarta khausar, na kawowa wannan ƴar aikin naku, wai ita zata yaye ma ta ita."

"Badai Bilki ba?, ita Bilki ta ina ta iya wani raino, da har zatayi yaye?."

Humaira tayi maganar da mamaki. Dariya Jiddarh tayi ta ce "aikuwa zakusha mamaki indai Bilki ce, dan kuwa ta iya raino sosai.."
"oho dai koma miye ita Auntie Nafeesar tunda dai taji kuma ta yadda shikenan, mudai namu kallo ne."
Ni'ima tayi maganar tana shiga motarta tayi horn mai gadi ya buɗe ma ta gate ta fita. Bayan sunyi sallama.



Juyowa Jiddarh tayi tana murmushi ta kalla Humaira ta ce.

"kinsan wani abu kuwa?."

" a'a saikin ae faɗa.

Kamar mene kenan?."
Humaira ta tambaya, Jiddarh ta ce "idan na cinye ki, zaki bani wannan ƴar ƙaramar garayar taki, wato 'guitar' ah turance."

"Dube ki to india jita nane sai dai ki hakura domin kuwa bazaki taɓa samu ba." Humaira tayi maganar tana galla ma ta harara. Jiddarh ta ce "to ai sai ki tsaya na ƙarisa ko."

"Okay, inajin ki ai."

Humaira ta kuma bata amsa, Jiddarh ta ci gaba, "idan kuma kece kika cinyeni, wato kika rigani ƙarisawa, sai ki zaɓa abin da kikeso a kayana, ni kuma zan baki, ba tare da wani musu ba."
Murmushi Humaira tayi tana faɗin, "nidai wannan ƙaton frame ɗin, dake manne a bangon bedroom ɗin ki, zaki bani idan naci, don shi nakeso."
"wanne kenan?." Jiddarh ta tambayeta tana ma ta kallon 🤬.



Humaira ta ce "wannan dai mai ɗauke da hoton Babban birnin Indonesia 'JAKARTA',"
Jiddarh ta ce "kima dena sa ranki akan wannan a bun Tarihin a gurina, dan ba samu zakiyi ba,"
Humaira ta ce "to a gwada mana, a gani, ai sai da kira karen bebe yake gane gida." jiddarh ta ce to a gwadan mana."

Humaira ta fara ƙirƙe, "One Two Three Go.!"
Da gudu suka nufa ƙofar palon, nanfa aka fara daga ƙarshe dai da Jiddarh taga alamar dai, Humaira da gaske take zata wuce ta.
Ta dage iya ƙarfinta ta qwala ihu, wanda hakan yasa Humaira juyowa slowly tana kallonta, wannan juyowar da Humaira tayi, shiya ba Jiddarh damar kwasa da gudu, ta isa bakin ƙofar palon, tana sauke haki.

Ganin kalar wayon da Jiddarh ta haɗa, yasa Humaira ta sadaƙar kawai sai ta dena gudun ta tsaya ta huta, sannan ta ƙariso bakin ƙofar ta tarar da Jiddarh sai faman ihun murna takeyi, tana faɗin "nayi winning na cinye, dan haka dole a bani guitar."

Ita dai Humaira bata kulata ba, ta buɗe ƙofar ta shiga ciki, ganin haka yasa ita ma Jiddarh tabi bayanta tana faɗin ta jirata.




Suna shiga a palo suka tarar da Auntie da Abba zaune daga can gefe, suna cin breakfast a ƙasa. Da sauri Jiddarh ta ƙarisa gurin, tana faɗin.
"Abba, yau kuma anan ake karin kumallon?."
Alhaji Komai Naka, yana murmushi ya shafa kanta yace. E, Mamana, angama wasan golf ɗin kenan?."
Murmushi tayi ta ce "E, Abba, yanzu ma wanka zanje nayi,"  kallon Auntie tayi ta ce "ina kwana Auntie," fuska cike da fara'a Auntie ta amsa ma ta.
 Sannan ta ce "ai ina jira ku gama gaisawa ne, tukuna." murmushi Jiddarh tayi.
"Auntie kenan, bari na haura sama nayi wanka, na dawo akwai labari fa."
Tayi maganar tana hawa bene bedroom, ta shiga tun a palo ta fara rage kayan jikinta, tana shiga bedroom ɗin kuwa kai tsaye bathroom ta shige.



Takai tsawon mintuna talatin, sannan ta fito sanye cikin bathrobe milk color, ga wani ƙaramin towel milk tana goge sumar kanta dashi, zama tayi a kan swivel chair na dress mirror, sannan ta ɗauki hairdryer ta busar da dogon gashin kanta, kana ta ɗauki hair brush ta tace gashin, lotion ta shafa kala kala kana tayi light make up.

Ba wani sosai tayi kwalliyar ba, hoda kawai ta shafa sama sama sai kwalli da ta saka a idanunta, sai kuma lip gloss da ta shafa a saman siraran laɓɓanta.


Cikin ƙanƙanin lokaci ta shirya cikin riga da skirt na a tamfa mai launin kore da ruwan hoda, rigar tayi ma ta ɗass a jiki haka ma skirt ɗin ribbons ta ɗauka mai launin kore da ruwan hoda suma, sannan ta kamo dogon gashin kanta ta ɗaure da kyar saboda tsayin gashin, kallabi ta ɗauka babba ne sosai dan sai yafi rabin falle.


A hannunta ta riƙo kallabin ta fito palon bedroom ɗin ta, a gaban madubin dogon yaro ta tsaya, tana kici kicin ɗaura ɗan kwallin, amma ta kasa, haka dole ba yadda ta iya ta fito palon ƙasa.
Da sauri ta ƙarisa gurin su Auntie dake saman 3sieter ita da Abba suna kallon wani Indian Drama 'Bruce Lee The Fighter'.{ Na Ram Charan Da Rakul Preet Singh.} dayake ranar saturday ne ɗarshen mako ba zuwa office.

Tayi maganar a yayinda take ɗaura ma ta kallabin. Dariya Jiddarh tayi ta ce.
"Auntie kenan, ana zaune lafiya, zaki kawo wani zancen aure kuma."

Humaira ce tayi maganar a yayinda take saukowa downstairs hannayenta ƙiqe da wani abu ta ɓoye a baya, ita ma kalan kayan jikin Jiddarh ne a jikinta ƙarisowa palon tayi, sannan ta zagayo da hannunta dake riƙe da guitar waje.


Miƙawa Jiddarh tayi tana faɗin, "ga kyautar gasar da kika ci fa, duk da dai kinyi zamba ciki."

𝚖𝚒𝚜𝚊𝚕𝚒𝚗 𝚔𝚊𝚛𝚏𝚎 𝚝𝚊𝚔𝚠𝚊𝚜 𝚍𝚊 𝚛𝚊𝚋𝚒 𝚗𝚊 𝚍𝚊𝚛𝚎

Afeefah tana zaune a palo saman doguwar kujera mai mazaunin mutum ɗaya.
Hannunta riƙe da Alqur'ani mai girma, tana karatawa cikin zazzaƙar muryarta.
Yayinda Deejarh dasu Lailah suke zazzauna a sauran kujerun suma alqur'ani mai girma ne, a hannunsu suna tilawa a kalla sunkai mintuna talatin sunayi, kafin suka idar a tare sannan Afeefah tabi duk ta karɓa alqur'anan dake a hannayensu, ta mayar ma da kowa bedroom ɗinshi, sannan ta dawo suka ci gaba da fira.

Suna cikin yi saiga Mister Mai Kyau, da Mrs Mai Kyau, sun shigo palon a tare, da alama daga ɓangaren Daddyn suke.

Guri suka samu suma suka zauna a saman 3sieter ganin haka yasa su Zaynerb suka koma ƙasa suma suka zazzauna, sai ya kasance Daddyn da Momy Rafi'at suna daga saman kujera su kuma.
Nainarh, Nailah, Haleesat, Zaynerb, Afeefah, Lailah sai Deejarh suna daga ƙasa cikin girmamawa suka gaisar dasu suka amsa cike da kulawa.

Daddy da yake cikaken ɗan boko ne, na bugawa a mujalla, gashi kuma mai dukiya lamba ɗaya akaf faɗin Nigeria. Ga kuma Momy Rafi'at ita ma cikakkiyar likata ce, wacca take da hospital nata na ƙashin kanta a garin Abuja, ga ilimin boko da ya ratsata ciki da wajen ta.


Kallon su Daddyn yayi yana murmushi, sannan ya mayar da kallonshi akan Afeefah.

"Shalelen Daddynta."
Ya kirata dq sunan da yafi yawan kiranta dashi, tana murmushi ta amsa da, "na'am Daddyna,"

Daddyn yace

Da sauri Deejarh ta ce "shine kuma muke ganin ki anan, kuma naji kince jinki kike a sama?."


Dariya sukayi gaba ɗaya Lailah ta ce "Deejarh kenan idan mutum yace yana jinshi a sama, yana nufin bazai iya bayyana irin farin cikin da yake ciki ba. Misali idan wani abu na farin ciki ya sameshi kuma sayya kasance wannan abun yana da muhimmanci sosai a gurin shi, toh ina fata dai kin fahimta?."

Dariya Deejarh tayi tana cewa, "sosai ma, Auntie Lailah nima jina nake a sama fa."

"Saboda me?,"

Nainarh ta tambaya, Deejarh ta ce "saboda gobe Balarabiyar India zataje gasar sᴀʀᴀᴜɴɪʏᴀʀ ᴋʏᴀᴜ😆.

saman stool na dress mirror ta zauna, tana goge sumar kanta mai yawa da kuma tsayi santsi game da taushi ga wani ƙamshi na musamman da yake fitarwa, da wani farin ƙaramin towel, dake hannunta takai minti goma tana kuma gyara gashin, sannan ta shafa lotion sama sama a fatarta mai taushi da santsi har wani shaining takeyi da glowing, saida ta kammala shafa lotion ɗin, sannan ta saka lip gloss a red lips ɗinta...
